WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.uuid, i.hostname, ie.flavor, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY i.hostname ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE i.display_name = 'forge') SELECT ic.uuid, ic.hostname, ic.flavor, i.memory_mb, i.vcpus, (CAST(i.memory_mb * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(i.vcpus, 0)) AS memory_per_vcpu_mb FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON i.uuid = ic.uuid WHERE (CAST(i.memory_mb * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(i.vcpus, 0)) > 2048), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.HOSTNAME AS hostname, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (ORDER BY ie.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge') SELECT inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.hostname, inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.instance_extra_id, inner_cte.window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE (CAST(inner_cte.memory_mb * 1.0 AS REAL) / (SELECT MAX(inner_cte2.memory_mb) FROM inner_cte AS inner_cte2)) > 0.75) SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT q1.uuid) AS Total_Forge_Instances_Meeting_MemoryPerVcpu_Threshold, COUNT(DISTINCT q2.instance_uuid) AS Total_Forge_Instances_Above_75pct_Max_Memory, ROUND(CAST(COUNT(DISTINCT q1.uuid) * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(COUNT(DISTINCT q2.instance_uuid), 0), 2) AS Ratio_MemoryPerVcpuQualified_To_Above75pctMaxMemory FROM q1 CROSS JOIN q2
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.ID AS instance_id, i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.USER_ID, i.PROJECT_ID, i.HOSTNAME, i.VM_STATE, i.POWER_STATE, i.MEMORY_MB, i.VCPUS, i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, c.ID AS info_cache_id, c.NETWORK_INFO, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY i.PROJECT_ID ORDER BY i.PROJECT_ID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS c ON i.UUID = c.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge'), ZoneAverages AS (SELECT i2.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, AVG(i2.MEMORY_MB * 1.0) AS avg_zone_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i2 WHERE i2.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge' GROUP BY i2.AVAILABILITY_ZONE) SELECT inner_cte.instance_id, inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.USER_ID, inner_cte.PROJECT_ID, inner_cte.HOSTNAME, inner_cte.VM_STATE, inner_cte.POWER_STATE, inner_cte.MEMORY_MB, inner_cte.VCPUS, inner_cte.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, inner_cte.info_cache_id, inner_cte.NETWORK_INFO, inner_cte.window_column FROM inner_cte JOIN ZoneAverages AS za ON za.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = inner_cte.AVAILABILITY_ZONE WHERE inner_cte.MEMORY_MB > za.avg_zone_memory_mb), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) AS min_instance_memory_mb, SUM(m.MEMORY_PROCESSED) AS total_migration_memory_processed FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic ON i.UUID = ic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded') SELECT i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, inner_cte.min_instance_memory_mb, inner_cte.total_migration_memory_processed, CASE WHEN EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM MIGRATIONS AS m2 JOIN INSTANCES AS i2 ON m2.INSTANCE_UUID = i2.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic2 ON i2.UUID = ic2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m2.STATUS = 'completed' AND i2.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded' AND ic2.DELETED = 0) THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_network_info_cache FROM inner_cte JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded' GROUP BY i.VM_STATE, inner_cte.min_instance_memory_mb, inner_cte.total_migration_memory_processed) SELECT q1.instance_id, q1.instance_uuid, q1.user_id, q1.project_id, q1.hostname, q1.vm_state, q1.power_state, q1.memory_mb, q1.vcpus, q1.availability_zone, q1.info_cache_id, q1.network_info, q1.window_column AS min_memory_mb_within_project, q2.min_instance_memory_mb AS shelved_offloaded_min_instance_memory_mb, q2.total_migration_memory_processed, q2.has_network_info_cache FROM q1 LEFT JOIN q2 ON q2.vm_state = q1.vm_state WHERE q1.vm_state = 'shelved_offloaded' ORDER BY q1.availability_zone, q1.project_id, q1.instance_id
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME, COUNT(i.ID) AS instance_count, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) AS min_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(min_memory_mb) AS avg_min_memory_mb FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.VM_STATE, ic.DISPLAY_NAME, ic.instance_count, ic.min_memory_mb, (ic.min_memory_mb - ac.avg_min_memory_mb) AS deviation_from_avg_min_memory_mb FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS ac), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT MIN(i.ID) AS min_instance_id, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) AS avg_memory_mb FROM INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON i.UUID = iic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME) SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, inner_cte.min_instance_id, inner_cte.avg_memory_mb FROM inner_cte JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON i.ID = inner_cte.min_instance_id WHERE inner_cte.avg_memory_mb > (SELECT AVG(inner_cte2.avg_memory_mb) FROM inner_cte AS inner_cte2)) SELECT q1.VM_STATE, q1.DISPLAY_NAME, q1.instance_count, q1.min_memory_mb, q1.deviation_from_avg_min_memory_mb, q2.min_instance_id, q2.avg_memory_mb FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.DISPLAY_NAME = q2.DISPLAY_NAME ORDER BY q1.VM_STATE, q1.DISPLAY_NAME
WITH CompletedMigrations AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic ON i.UUID = ic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND ic.DELETED = 0), InstanceMemory AS (SELECT inner_cte.migration_id, inner_cte.SOURCE_COMPUTE, inner_cte.DEST_COMPUTE, inner_cte.instance_display_name, inner_cte.window_column, i.MEMORY_MB AS instance_memory_mb FROM inner_cte JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON m.ID = inner_cte.migration_id JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID), AverageMemory AS (SELECT AVG(instance_memory_mb) AS avg_instance_memory_mb FROM InstanceMemory) SELECT im.migration_id, im.SOURCE_COMPUTE, im.DEST_COMPUTE, im.instance_display_name, im.window_column AS rank_within_status, im.instance_memory_mb, (im.instance_memory_mb - am.avg_instance_memory_mb) AS deviation_from_avg_instance_memory_mb FROM InstanceMemory AS im CROSS JOIN AverageMemory AS am ORDER BY im.migration_id), Flare3HighMemInstances AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.uuid, i.display_name, i.vm_state, i.memory_mb, c.network_info, AVG(i.memory_mb) OVER (PARTITION BY i.vm_state ORDER BY i.memory_mb ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON i.uuid = c.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3' ORDER BY i.memory_mb) SELECT inner_cte.uuid, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.network_info, inner_cte.window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE inner_cte.memory_mb > (SELECT AVG(memory_mb) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY inner_cte.memory_mb) SELECT cm.migration_id, cm.SOURCE_COMPUTE, cm.DEST_COMPUTE, cm.instance_display_name, cm.rank_within_status, cm.instance_memory_mb, cm.deviation_from_avg_instance_memory_mb, f.uuid AS flare3_instance_uuid, f.vm_state AS flare3_vm_state, f.memory_mb AS flare3_memory_mb, f.network_info AS flare3_network_info, f.window_column AS flare3_windowed_avg_memory_mb FROM CompletedMigrations AS cm JOIN Flare3HighMemInstances AS f ON f.display_name = cm.instance_display_name ORDER BY cm.migration_id, f.memory_mb
WITH vm_state_migration_stats AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.vm_state, EXP(AVG(LN(m.memory_total))) AS geometric_mean_memory_total FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON i.uuid = c.instance_uuid WHERE m.memory_total > 0 AND c.network_info <> '' GROUP BY i.vm_state ORDER BY geometric_mean_memory_total DESC), benchmark AS (SELECT AVG(geometric_mean_memory_total) AS benchmark_geometric_mean_memory_total FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.vm_state, ic.geometric_mean_memory_total, b.benchmark_geometric_mean_memory_total AS benchmark, CASE WHEN ic.geometric_mean_memory_total > b.benchmark_geometric_mean_memory_total * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_MIGRATION' WHEN ic.geometric_mean_memory_total < b.benchmark_geometric_mean_memory_total * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_MIGRATION' ELSE 'AVERAGE_MIGRATION' END AS migration_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN benchmark AS b ORDER BY ic.geometric_mean_memory_total DESC), confirmed_ether60_migrations AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.INSTANCE_UUID AS instance_uuid, m.STATUS AS migration_status, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE AS source_compute, m.DEST_COMPUTE AS dest_compute, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.VM_STATE AS instance_vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS instance_memory_mb, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'confirmed' AND m.SOURCE_COMPUTE = 'ether-60') SELECT inner_cte.migration_id, inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.migration_status, inner_cte.source_compute, inner_cte.dest_compute, inner_cte.instance_display_name, inner_cte.instance_vm_state, inner_cte.instance_memory_mb, inner_cte.window_column, i.project_id AS project_id, CASE WHEN EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM migrations AS m2 JOIN instances AS i2 ON m2.instance_uuid = i2.uuid WHERE i2.project_id = i.project_id AND m2.deleted = 0 AND NOT m2.status IN ('completed', 'failed', 'error') AND m2.id <> inner_cte.migration_id) THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS project_has_other_active_migrations FROM inner_cte JOIN instances AS i ON inner_cte.instance_uuid = i.uuid ORDER BY inner_cte.migration_status, inner_cte.migration_id) SELECT cem.migration_id, cem.instance_uuid, cem.migration_status, cem.source_compute, cem.dest_compute, cem.instance_display_name, cem.instance_vm_state, cem.instance_memory_mb, cem.window_column, cem.project_id, cem.project_has_other_active_migrations, vms.geometric_mean_memory_total AS vm_state_geometric_mean_memory_total, vms.benchmark AS vm_state_benchmark_geometric_mean_memory_total, vms.migration_category AS vm_state_migration_category FROM confirmed_ether60_migrations AS cem LEFT JOIN vm_state_migration_stats AS vms ON cem.instance_vm_state = vms.vm_state ORDER BY cem.migration_status, cem.migration_id
WITH confirmed_migrations AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.status, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, i.display_name, ic.network_info, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.status ORDER BY m.status) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic ON i.uuid = ic.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'confirmed'), avg_rank AS (SELECT AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte) SELECT c.status, c.source_compute, c.dest_compute, c.display_name, c.network_info, c.window_column, a.avg_window_column, (c.window_column - a.avg_window_column) AS deviation_from_avg_dense_rank FROM inner_cte AS c CROSS JOIN avg_rank AS a), forge_instances AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, ie.CREATED_AT AS instance_extra_created_at, iic.NETWORK_INFO AS network_info, MIN(ie.ID) OVER (ORDER BY i.MEMORY_MB ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge') SELECT ic.instance_uuid, ic.display_name, ic.vm_state, ic.memory_mb, ic.vcpus, ic.instance_extra_id, ic.instance_extra_created_at, ic.network_info, ic.window_column, CASE WHEN EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie2 JOIN INSTANCES AS i2 ON i2.UUID = ie2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i2.UUID = ic.instance_uuid AND ie2.DELETED <> 0) THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_deleted_instance_extra FROM inner_cte AS ic) SELECT cm.status AS migration_status, cm.source_compute, cm.dest_compute, cm.display_name AS migration_instance_display_name, cm.network_info AS migration_network_info, cm.window_column AS migration_dense_rank_within_status, cm.avg_window_column AS avg_migration_dense_rank_across_confirmed, cm.deviation_from_avg_dense_rank, fi.instance_uuid AS forge_instance_uuid, fi.display_name AS forge_display_name, fi.vm_state AS forge_vm_state, fi.memory_mb AS forge_memory_mb, fi.vcpus AS forge_vcpus, fi.instance_extra_id AS forge_instance_extra_id, fi.instance_extra_created_at AS forge_instance_extra_created_at, fi.network_info AS forge_network_info, fi.window_column AS forge_min_instance_extra_id_window, fi.has_deleted_instance_extra FROM confirmed_migrations AS cm JOIN forge_instances AS fi ON fi.display_name = cm.display_name
WITH subq1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.HOSTNAME AS hostname, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (ORDER BY ie.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge'), avg_mem AS (SELECT AVG(memory_mb) AS avg_memory_mb FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.instance_uuid, ic.hostname, ic.vm_state, ic.memory_mb, ic.instance_extra_id, ic.window_column, (ic.memory_mb - am.avg_memory_mb) AS deviation_from_avg_memory_mb FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_mem AS am), subq2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME, COUNT(i.UUID) AS instance_count, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) AS min_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.VCPUS > 8 GROUP BY i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME) SELECT VM_STATE, DISPLAY_NAME, instance_count, min_memory_mb FROM inner_cte WHERE instance_count > (SELECT AVG(instance_count) FROM inner_cte)) SELECT s2.VM_STATE, s2.DISPLAY_NAME, s2.instance_count, s2.min_memory_mb, AVG(s1.deviation_from_avg_memory_mb) AS avg_deviation_from_avg_memory_mb_among_forge_instances_in_group, MIN(s1.window_column) AS min_windowed_memory_mb_among_forge_instances_in_group FROM subq2 AS s2 JOIN subq1 AS s1 ON s1.vm_state = s2.VM_STATE AND s2.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge' GROUP BY s2.VM_STATE, s2.DISPLAY_NAME, s2.instance_count, s2.min_memory_mb ORDER BY s2.instance_count DESC
WITH ShelvedOffloadedHighMemory AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, SUM(i.memory_mb) AS total_memory_mb FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON ie.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS iic ON i.uuid = iic.instance_uuid WHERE i.vm_state = 'shelved_offloaded' GROUP BY i.display_name HAVING SUM(i.memory_mb) > 20000) SELECT display_name, total_memory_mb FROM inner_cte WHERE total_memory_mb > (SELECT AVG(total_memory_mb) FROM inner_cte)), Top10Flare3CacheStats AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, COUNT(iic.id) AS cache_records FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON i.uuid = iic.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3' GROUP BY i.display_name ORDER BY cache_records DESC LIMIT 10), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(cache_records) AS avg_cache_records FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.display_name, ic.cache_records, ac.avg_cache_records, (ic.cache_records - ac.avg_cache_records) AS deviation_from_avg FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS ac ORDER BY ic.cache_records DESC) SELECT t.display_name, s.total_memory_mb, t.cache_records, t.avg_cache_records, t.deviation_from_avg FROM Top10Flare3CacheStats AS t JOIN ShelvedOffloadedHighMemory AS s ON s.display_name = t.display_name WHERE s.total_memory_mb > 20000 ORDER BY s.total_memory_mb DESC, t.cache_records DESC
WITH PerStateDisplayNameMinMemory AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME, COUNT(i.ID) AS instance_count, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) AS min_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(min_memory_mb) AS avg_min_memory_mb FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.VM_STATE, ic.DISPLAY_NAME, ic.instance_count, ic.min_memory_mb, (ic.min_memory_mb - ac.avg_min_memory_mb) AS deviation_from_avg_min_memory_mb FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS ac), PerStateMaxGeometricMeanMemory AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.vm_state, EXP(AVG(LN(i.memory_mb))) AS geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.memory_mb > 0 AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.vm_state ORDER BY geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC) SELECT vm_state, MAX(geometric_mean_memory_mb) AS max_geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM inner_cte GROUP BY vm_state ORDER BY max_geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC) SELECT psdn.VM_STATE, psdn.DISPLAY_NAME, psdn.instance_count, psdn.min_memory_mb, psdn.deviation_from_avg_min_memory_mb, psgm.max_geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM PerStateDisplayNameMinMemory AS psdn JOIN PerStateMaxGeometricMeanMemory AS psgm ON psdn.VM_STATE = psgm.vm_state ORDER BY psdn.VM_STATE, psdn.DISPLAY_NAME
WITH shelved_metadata AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.POWER_STATE AS power_state, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, iic.NETWORK_INFO AS network_info, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded') SELECT CASE WHEN LAG(metadata_key) OVER (ORDER BY metadata_key, instance_uuid) = metadata_key THEN NULL ELSE metadata_key END AS metadata_key, instance_uuid, display_name, power_state, metadata_value, network_info, window_column, COUNT(*) OVER (PARTITION BY metadata_key) AS total_rows_per_key, AVG(power_state) OVER (PARTITION BY metadata_key) AS avg_power_state_per_key FROM inner_cte ORDER BY metadata_key, instance_uuid), high_completed_migrations AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE AS instance_availability_zone, m.STATUS AS migration_status, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed') SELECT inner_cte.migration_id, inner_cte.instance_display_name, inner_cte.instance_availability_zone, inner_cte.migration_status, inner_cte.window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE inner_cte.migration_id > (SELECT AVG(m2.ID * 1.0) FROM MIGRATIONS AS m2 WHERE m2.STATUS = 'completed') ORDER BY inner_cte.migration_id), high_completed_migration_instances AS (SELECT DISTINCT m.INSTANCE_UUID AS instance_uuid FROM MIGRATIONS AS m WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND m.INSTANCE_UUID IN (SELECT i.UUID FROM INSTANCES AS i WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded') AND m.ID IN (SELECT migration_id FROM high_completed_migrations)) SELECT sm.metadata_key, sm.instance_uuid, sm.display_name, sm.power_state, sm.metadata_value, sm.network_info, sm.window_column, sm.total_rows_per_key, sm.avg_power_state_per_key FROM shelved_metadata AS sm WHERE sm.instance_uuid IN (SELECT instance_uuid FROM high_completed_migration_instances) ORDER BY sm.metadata_key, sm.instance_uuid
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.POWER_STATE AS power_state, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, iic.NETWORK_INFO AS network_info, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded') SELECT CASE WHEN LAG(metadata_key) OVER (ORDER BY metadata_key, instance_uuid) = metadata_key THEN NULL ELSE metadata_key END AS metadata_key, instance_uuid, display_name, power_state, metadata_value, network_info, window_column, COUNT(*) OVER (PARTITION BY metadata_key) AS total_rows_per_key, AVG(power_state) OVER (PARTITION BY metadata_key) AS avg_power_state_per_key FROM inner_cte ORDER BY metadata_key, instance_uuid), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, i.vm_state, i.memory_mb, c.network_info, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY i.project_id ORDER BY i.project_id) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON i.uuid = c.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0) SELECT inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.network_info, inner_cte.window_column FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(i2.memory_mb) AS avg_memory_mb FROM instances AS i2 WHERE i2.deleted = 0) AS mem_avg ON 1 = 1 WHERE inner_cte.memory_mb > mem_avg.avg_memory_mb) SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT q1.instance_uuid) AS shelved_offloaded_instances_with_system_metadata_count, COUNT(DISTINCT q2.display_name) AS above_avg_memory_instances_count FROM q1 CROSS JOIN q2
WITH completed_migration_memory_deviation AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic ON i.UUID = ic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND ic.DELETED = 0), InstanceMemory AS (SELECT inner_cte.migration_id, inner_cte.SOURCE_COMPUTE, inner_cte.DEST_COMPUTE, inner_cte.instance_display_name, inner_cte.window_column, i.MEMORY_MB AS instance_memory_mb FROM inner_cte JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON m.ID = inner_cte.migration_id JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID), AverageMemory AS (SELECT AVG(instance_memory_mb) AS avg_instance_memory_mb FROM InstanceMemory) SELECT im.migration_id, im.SOURCE_COMPUTE, im.DEST_COMPUTE, im.instance_display_name, im.window_column AS rank_within_status, im.instance_memory_mb, (im.instance_memory_mb - am.avg_instance_memory_mb) AS deviation_from_avg_instance_memory_mb FROM InstanceMemory AS im CROSS JOIN AverageMemory AS am ORDER BY im.migration_id), high_memory_shelved_instances AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, SUM(i.MEMORY_MB) AS total_memory_mb FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME HAVING SUM(i.MEMORY_MB) > 50000) SELECT inner_cte.DISPLAY_NAME, inner_cte.total_memory_mb FROM inner_cte WHERE inner_cte.total_memory_mb > (SELECT AVG(inner_cte.total_memory_mb * 1.0) FROM inner_cte)) SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT cmd.migration_id) AS completed_migrations_for_high_memory_shelved_instances_count, COUNT(DISTINCT hms.DISPLAY_NAME) AS high_memory_shelved_instance_display_names_count FROM completed_migration_memory_deviation AS cmd JOIN high_memory_shelved_instances AS hms ON cmd.instance_display_name = hms.DISPLAY_NAME
WITH top_projects AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT t1.UUID, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY t1.MEMORY_MB ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS t0 JOIN INSTANCES AS t1 ON t0.INSTANCE_UUID = t1.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS t2 ON t1.UUID = t2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE t1.DELETED = 0) SELECT i.project_id AS project_id, SUM(i.memory_mb) AS total_memory_mb, CASE WHEN EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN instances AS i2 ON ic.uuid = i2.uuid WHERE i2.project_id = i.project_id AND ic.window_column = 1) THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_rank1_instance_by_window FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON ie.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS iic ON iic.instance_uuid = i.uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 GROUP BY i.project_id ORDER BY total_memory_mb DESC LIMIT 10), instance_details AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.id AS instance_id, i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.user_id, i.project_id, i.display_name, i.vm_state, i.memory_mb, i.vcpus, c.id AS info_cache_id, MAX(i.id) OVER (ORDER BY i.id ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_instance_id_next_2 FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON i.uuid = c.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND c.deleted = 0) SELECT inner_cte.instance_id, inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.user_id, inner_cte.project_id, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.vcpus, inner_cte.info_cache_id, inner_cte.max_instance_id_next_2, project_totals.total_instances_in_project, avg_instances.avg_instance_count FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT project_id, COUNT(*) AS total_instances_in_project FROM inner_cte GROUP BY project_id) AS project_totals ON inner_cte.project_id = project_totals.project_id, (SELECT AVG(instance_count) AS avg_instance_count FROM (SELECT project_id, COUNT(*) AS instance_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY project_id) AS sub) AS avg_instances ORDER BY inner_cte.instance_id) SELECT tp.project_id, tp.total_memory_mb, tp.has_rank1_instance_by_window, id.instance_id, id.instance_uuid, id.user_id, id.display_name, id.vm_state, id.memory_mb, id.vcpus, id.info_cache_id, id.max_instance_id_next_2, id.total_instances_in_project, id.avg_instance_count FROM top_projects AS tp JOIN instance_details AS id ON id.project_id = tp.project_id ORDER BY tp.total_memory_mb DESC, id.instance_id
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.status, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, m.instance_uuid, i.display_name, i.memory_mb, ism.key, ism.value, MIN(i.memory_mb) OVER (ORDER BY m.id ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.vm_state = 'shelved_offloaded'), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_min_memory_mb FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.status, ic.source_compute, ic.dest_compute, ic.instance_uuid, ic.display_name, ic.memory_mb, ic.key, ic.value, ic.window_column, av.avg_window_min_memory_mb, (ic.window_column - av.avg_window_min_memory_mb) AS deviation_from_avg_window_min FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS av), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, COUNT(iic.ID) AS cache_records FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON i.UUID = iic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME ORDER BY cache_records DESC LIMIT 10), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(cache_records) AS avg_cache_records FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.DISPLAY_NAME, ic.cache_records, ac.avg_cache_records, (ic.cache_records - ac.avg_cache_records) AS deviation_from_avg FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS ac ORDER BY ic.cache_records DESC) SELECT q1.status AS migration_status, q1.source_compute, q1.dest_compute, q1.instance_uuid, q1.display_name, q1.memory_mb, q1.key AS system_metadata_key, q1.value AS system_metadata_value, q1.window_column AS window_min_memory_mb, q1.avg_window_min_memory_mb, q1.deviation_from_avg_window_min, q2.cache_records, q2.avg_cache_records, q2.deviation_from_avg AS cache_records_deviation_from_avg FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.display_name = q2.DISPLAY_NAME JOIN instances AS i ON i.uuid = q1.instance_uuid WHERE i.DELETED = 0 ORDER BY q2.cache_records DESC, q1.instance_uuid
WITH ForgeMetadata AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID, ism.KEY, ism.VALUE, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ism.KEY ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge') SELECT inner_cte.UUID, inner_cte.KEY, inner_cte.VALUE, inner_cte.window_column, i.MEMORY_MB FROM inner_cte JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON i.UUID = inner_cte.UUID WHERE i.MEMORY_MB > 16000), CompletedMigrationHighProcessed AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, i.availability_zone, m.status, m.migration_type, i.uuid, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY i.uuid ORDER BY i.uuid) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN migrations AS m ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed') SELECT inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.availability_zone, inner_cte.status, inner_cte.migration_type, inner_cte.uuid, inner_cte.window_column, (CAST(m.memory_processed * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(m.memory_total, 0)) AS memory_processed_ratio FROM inner_cte JOIN migrations AS m ON m.instance_uuid = inner_cte.uuid AND m.status = inner_cte.status AND m.migration_type = inner_cte.migration_type WHERE (CAST(m.memory_processed * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(m.memory_total, 0)) > 0.75), CombinedInstanceSignals AS (SELECT fm.UUID AS uuid, fm.KEY AS signal_key, fm.VALUE AS signal_value, fm.window_column AS signal_window_column, fm.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, 'Forge metadata (memory > 16000MB)' AS reason, NULL AS display_name, NULL AS availability_zone, NULL AS migration_status, NULL AS migration_type, NULL AS migration_window_column, NULL AS memory_processed_ratio FROM ForgeMetadata AS fm UNION ALL SELECT cm.uuid AS uuid, NULL AS signal_key, NULL AS signal_value, NULL AS signal_window_column, NULL AS memory_mb, 'Completed migration (memory processed ratio > 0.75)' AS reason, cm.display_name, cm.availability_zone, cm.status AS migration_status, cm.migration_type, cm.window_column AS migration_window_column, cm.memory_processed_ratio FROM CompletedMigrationHighProcessed AS cm WHERE cm.display_name = 'forge') SELECT * FROM CombinedInstanceSignals ORDER BY uuid, reason
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, SUM(i.memory_mb) AS total_memory_mb FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON ie.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS iic ON i.uuid = iic.instance_uuid WHERE i.vm_state = 'shelved_offloaded' GROUP BY i.display_name HAVING SUM(i.memory_mb) > 20000), AvgTotals AS (SELECT AVG(inner_cte.total_memory_mb * 1.0) AS avg_total_memory_mb FROM inner_cte) SELECT inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.total_memory_mb FROM inner_cte WHERE inner_cte.total_memory_mb > (SELECT avg_total_memory_mb FROM AvgTotals)), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID, i.HOSTNAME, i.VM_STATE, i.MEMORY_MB, c.NETWORK_INFO, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (ORDER BY i.ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS c ON i.UUID = c.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge') SELECT CASE WHEN LAG(vm_state) OVER (ORDER BY uuid) = vm_state THEN NULL ELSE vm_state END AS vm_state, uuid, hostname, memory_mb, network_info, window_column, COUNT(*) OVER () AS total_forge_instances, AVG(memory_mb) OVER () AS avg_forge_memory_mb FROM inner_cte ORDER BY uuid) SELECT q2.uuid, q2.hostname, q2.vm_state, q2.memory_mb, q2.network_info, q2.window_column, q2.total_forge_instances, q2.avg_forge_memory_mb, q1.total_memory_mb AS shelved_offloaded_total_memory_mb_for_display_name FROM q2 JOIN q1 ON q1.display_name = 'forge' ORDER BY q2.uuid
WITH forge_instances AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, i.uuid, i.memory_mb, c.network_info, c.id AS cache_id, AVG(i.memory_mb) OVER (PARTITION BY i.display_name ORDER BY i.memory_mb) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON i.uuid = c.instance_uuid WHERE i.display_name = 'forge' ORDER BY i.memory_mb) SELECT inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.uuid, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.network_info, inner_cte.cache_id, inner_cte.window_column, forge_stats.total_forge_instances, forge_stats.avg_forge_memory_mb FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT COUNT(*) AS total_forge_instances, AVG(memory_mb) AS avg_forge_memory_mb FROM inner_cte) AS forge_stats ON 1 = 1 ORDER BY inner_cte.memory_mb), shelved_offloaded_with_cache AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS shelved_offloaded_instances_with_cache FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic ON i.UUID = ic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded' AND i.DELETED = 0 AND ic.DELETED = 0) SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.HOSTNAME AS hostname, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ic.UPDATED_AT AS cache_updated_at FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic ON i.UUID = ic.INSTANCE_UUID CROSS JOIN inner_cte WHERE i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded' AND i.DELETED = 0 AND ic.DELETED = 0) SELECT fi.display_name, fi.uuid AS forge_instance_uuid, fi.memory_mb AS forge_memory_mb, fi.network_info AS forge_cached_network_info, fi.cache_id AS forge_cache_id, fi.window_column AS avg_memory_mb_within_display_name_ordered_by_memory, fi.total_forge_instances, fi.avg_forge_memory_mb, so.hostname AS shelved_offloaded_hostname, so.memory_mb AS shelved_offloaded_memory_mb, so.vcpus AS shelved_offloaded_vcpus, so.cache_updated_at AS shelved_offloaded_cache_updated_at, ROUND(CAST(fi.memory_mb AS REAL) / NULLIF(so.memory_mb, 0), 2) AS forge_to_shelved_offloaded_memory_ratio FROM forge_instances AS fi JOIN shelved_offloaded_with_cache AS so ON so.instance_uuid = fi.uuid ORDER BY fi.memory_mb
WITH mem_over_avg AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, SUM(i.MEMORY_MB) AS total_memory_mb FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME HAVING SUM(i.MEMORY_MB) > 50000) SELECT inner_cte.DISPLAY_NAME, inner_cte.total_memory_mb FROM inner_cte WHERE inner_cte.total_memory_mb > (SELECT AVG(inner_cte.total_memory_mb * 1.0) FROM inner_cte)), completed_migrations AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.INSTANCE_UUID, m.MIGRATION_TYPE, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, m.DEST_HOST, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.VM_STATE, i.MEMORY_MB, i.VCPUS, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, m.MEMORY_TOTAL, MIN(m.MEMORY_TOTAL) OVER (PARTITION BY m.MIGRATION_TYPE ORDER BY m.MIGRATION_TYPE) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed') SELECT CASE WHEN LAG(MIGRATION_TYPE) OVER (ORDER BY MIGRATION_TYPE, migration_id) = MIGRATION_TYPE THEN NULL ELSE MIGRATION_TYPE END AS migration_type, migration_id, INSTANCE_UUID, SOURCE_COMPUTE, DEST_COMPUTE, DEST_HOST, DISPLAY_NAME, VM_STATE, MEMORY_MB, VCPUS, instance_extra_id, MEMORY_TOTAL, window_column FROM inner_cte ORDER BY MIGRATION_TYPE, migration_id), filtered_completed_migrations AS (SELECT cm.migration_type, cm.migration_id, cm.INSTANCE_UUID, cm.SOURCE_COMPUTE, cm.DEST_COMPUTE, cm.DEST_HOST, cm.DISPLAY_NAME, cm.VM_STATE, cm.MEMORY_MB, cm.VCPUS, cm.instance_extra_id, cm.MEMORY_TOTAL, cm.window_column, moa.total_memory_mb FROM completed_migrations AS cm JOIN mem_over_avg AS moa ON cm.DISPLAY_NAME = moa.DISPLAY_NAME WHERE cm.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded') SELECT fcm.migration_type, fcm.migration_id, fcm.INSTANCE_UUID, fcm.SOURCE_COMPUTE, fcm.DEST_COMPUTE, fcm.DEST_HOST, fcm.DISPLAY_NAME, fcm.VM_STATE, fcm.MEMORY_MB, fcm.VCPUS, fcm.instance_extra_id, fcm.MEMORY_TOTAL, fcm.window_column, fcm.total_memory_mb, ROUND(CAST(fcm.MEMORY_TOTAL AS REAL) / NULLIF(fcm.total_memory_mb, 0), 4) AS migration_memory_to_instance_total_memory_ratio FROM filtered_completed_migrations AS fcm ORDER BY fcm.migration_type, fcm.migration_id
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, ie.CREATED_AT AS instance_extra_created_at, iic.NETWORK_INFO AS network_info, MIN(ie.ID) OVER (ORDER BY i.MEMORY_MB ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge'), AverageMemory AS (SELECT AVG(memory_mb) AS avg_memory_mb FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.instance_uuid, ic.display_name, ic.vm_state, ic.memory_mb, ic.vcpus, ic.instance_extra_id, ic.instance_extra_created_at, ic.network_info, ic.window_column, am.avg_memory_mb AS benchmark_avg_memory_mb, CASE WHEN ic.memory_mb > am.avg_memory_mb * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_MEMORY' WHEN ic.memory_mb < am.avg_memory_mb * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_MEMORY' ELSE 'AVERAGE_MEMORY' END AS memory_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageMemory AS am), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT t0.UUID, t0.HOSTNAME, t0.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, t2.NETWORK_INFO, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY t0.AVAILABILITY_ZONE ORDER BY t0.ID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS t0 JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS t1 ON t1.INSTANCE_UUID = t0.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS t2 ON t2.INSTANCE_UUID = t0.UUID WHERE t0.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge'), avg_ids AS (SELECT AVG(t0.ID) AS avg_instance_id FROM INSTANCES AS t0 WHERE t0.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge') SELECT i.UUID, i.HOSTNAME, i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, i.NETWORK_INFO, i.window_column AS rank_within_az_by_id, (t0.ID - a.avg_instance_id) AS deviation_from_avg_instance_id FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN INSTANCES AS t0 ON t0.UUID = i.UUID CROSS JOIN avg_ids AS a) SELECT q1.instance_uuid, q1.display_name, q2.hostname, q2.availability_zone, q1.vm_state, q1.memory_mb, q1.vcpus, q1.instance_extra_id, q1.instance_extra_created_at, q1.network_info, q1.window_column AS min_instance_extra_id_window_3_preceding_1_following_by_memory_mb, q1.benchmark_avg_memory_mb, q1.memory_category, q2.rank_within_az_by_id, q2.deviation_from_avg_instance_id FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q2.UUID = q1.instance_uuid
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.VM_STATE, SUM(i.MEMORY_MB) AS total_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic ON i.UUID = ic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' GROUP BY i.VM_STATE HAVING SUM(i.MEMORY_MB) > 50000) SELECT inner_cte.VM_STATE, inner_cte.total_memory_mb, (CAST(inner_cte.total_memory_mb * 1.0 AS REAL) / totals.grand_total_memory_mb) AS pct_of_included_total_memory FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT SUM(total_memory_mb) AS grand_total_memory_mb FROM inner_cte) AS totals ON 1 = 1), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME, COUNT(i.ID) AS instance_count, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) AS min_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME) SELECT ic.VM_STATE, ic.DISPLAY_NAME, ic.instance_count, ic.min_memory_mb FROM inner_cte AS ic WHERE ic.min_memory_mb > 0.75 * (SELECT MAX(min_memory_mb) FROM inner_cte)) SELECT q2.VM_STATE, q2.DISPLAY_NAME, q2.instance_count, q2.min_memory_mb, q1.total_memory_mb, q1.pct_of_included_total_memory FROM q2 JOIN q1 ON q2.VM_STATE = q1.VM_STATE ORDER BY q1.pct_of_included_total_memory DESC, q2.instance_count DESC, q2.DISPLAY_NAME
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, m.STATUS AS migration_status, SUM(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY m.MIGRATION_TYPE ORDER BY i.MEMORY_MB) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND m.STATUS = 'completed') SELECT CASE WHEN LAG(m.MIGRATION_TYPE) OVER (ORDER BY m.MIGRATION_TYPE, inner_cte.window_column, inner_cte.instance_uuid) = m.MIGRATION_TYPE THEN NULL ELSE m.MIGRATION_TYPE END AS migration_type, inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.instance_display_name, inner_cte.migration_status, inner_cte.window_column FROM inner_cte JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = inner_cte.instance_uuid AND m.STATUS = inner_cte.migration_status ORDER BY m.MIGRATION_TYPE, inner_cte.window_column, inner_cte.instance_uuid), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, EXP(AVG(LN(i.memory_mb))) AS geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON ism.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS iic ON i.uuid = iic.instance_uuid WHERE i.memory_mb > 0 AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.display_name ORDER BY geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC), Benchmark AS (SELECT EXP(AVG(LN(geometric_mean_memory_mb))) AS benchmark_geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.display_name, ic.geometric_mean_memory_mb, b.benchmark_geometric_mean_memory_mb AS benchmark, CASE WHEN ic.geometric_mean_memory_mb > b.benchmark_geometric_mean_memory_mb * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_MEMORY' WHEN ic.geometric_mean_memory_mb < b.benchmark_geometric_mean_memory_mb * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_MEMORY' ELSE 'AVERAGE_MEMORY' END AS memory_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN Benchmark AS b ORDER BY ic.geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC) SELECT q1.migration_type, q1.instance_uuid, q1.instance_display_name, q1.migration_status, q1.window_column AS running_sum_instance_memory_mb_within_migration_type, q2.geometric_mean_memory_mb AS display_name_geometric_mean_memory_mb, q2.benchmark AS benchmark_geometric_mean_memory_mb, q2.memory_category FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q2.display_name = q1.instance_display_name JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON i.UUID = q1.instance_uuid WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' ORDER BY q1.migration_type, q1.window_column, q1.instance_uuid
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT sg.NAME, sg.DESCRIPTION, sg.USER_ID, sg.PROJECT_ID, sgr.PROTOCOL, sgr.FROM_PORT, sgr.TO_PORT, sgr.CIDR, MIN(sgr.FROM_PORT) OVER (ORDER BY sgr.PARENT_GROUP_ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM SECURITY_GROUPS AS sg JOIN SECURITY_GROUP_RULES AS sgr ON sg.ID = sgr.PARENT_GROUP_ID WHERE sg.NAME = 'aurum-xenon'), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(FROM_PORT) AS avg_from_port FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.NAME, i.DESCRIPTION, i.USER_ID, i.PROJECT_ID, i.PROTOCOL, i.FROM_PORT, i.TO_PORT, i.CIDR, i.window_column, (i.FROM_PORT - a.avg_from_port) AS deviation_from_avg_from_port FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT sg.NAME AS security_group_name, sgr.PROTOCOL, sgr.FROM_PORT, SUM(sgr.FROM_PORT) OVER (PARTITION BY sgr.PROTOCOL ORDER BY sgr.FROM_PORT) AS window_column FROM SECURITY_GROUPS AS sg JOIN SECURITY_GROUP_RULES AS sgr ON sg.ID = sgr.PARENT_GROUP_ID WHERE sg.NAME = 'aurum-xenon'), ProtocolAverages AS (SELECT sgr.PROTOCOL, AVG(sgr.FROM_PORT * 1.0) AS avg_from_port FROM SECURITY_GROUPS AS sg JOIN SECURITY_GROUP_RULES AS sgr ON sg.ID = sgr.PARENT_GROUP_ID WHERE sg.NAME = 'aurum-xenon' GROUP BY sgr.PROTOCOL) SELECT i.security_group_name, i.PROTOCOL, i.FROM_PORT, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ProtocolAverages AS pa ON pa.PROTOCOL = i.PROTOCOL WHERE i.FROM_PORT > pa.avg_from_port) SELECT q1.NAME AS security_group_name, q1.DESCRIPTION, q1.USER_ID, q1.PROJECT_ID, q1.PROTOCOL, q1.FROM_PORT, q1.TO_PORT, q1.CIDR, q1.window_column AS min_from_port_window_3_preceding_1_following, q1.deviation_from_avg_from_port, q2.window_column AS running_sum_from_ports_within_protocol FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.NAME = q2.security_group_name AND q1.PROTOCOL = q2.PROTOCOL AND q1.FROM_PORT = q2.FROM_PORT ORDER BY q1.PROTOCOL, q1.FROM_PORT
WITH completed_migration_rank_by_zone AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE AS instance_availability_zone, m.STATUS AS migration_status, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed') SELECT instance_availability_zone, MAX(window_column) AS max_migration_rank_in_zone FROM inner_cte GROUP BY instance_availability_zone), max_total_vcpus_per_key_in_flare3 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name, i.key_name, COUNT(ism.id) AS metadata_entry_count, SUM(i.vcpus) AS total_vcpus FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3' GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, i.key_name) SELECT key_name, MAX(total_vcpus) AS max_total_vcpus_per_instance FROM inner_cte GROUP BY key_name) SELECT cmrz.max_migration_rank_in_zone, mtv.max_total_vcpus_per_instance FROM completed_migration_rank_by_zone AS cmrz CROSS JOIN (SELECT MAX(max_total_vcpus_per_instance) AS max_total_vcpus_per_instance FROM max_total_vcpus_per_key_in_flare3) AS mtv WHERE cmrz.instance_availability_zone = 'flare3'
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.ID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, c.NETWORK_INFO, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE ORDER BY i.ID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS c ON i.UUID = c.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded'), avg_ids AS (SELECT AVG(ID) AS avg_instance_id FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.ID, ic.DISPLAY_NAME, ic.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, ic.NETWORK_INFO, ic.window_column, (ic.ID - ai.avg_instance_id) AS deviation_from_avg_instance_id FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_ids AS ai), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, m.status, MAX(m.memory_total) AS max_memory_total, m.created_at FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.deleted = 0 GROUP BY i.display_name, m.status, m.created_at HAVING MAX(m.memory_total) > 0) SELECT inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.status, inner_cte.max_memory_total, inner_cte.created_at FROM inner_cte JOIN migrations AS m ON m.created_at = inner_cte.created_at AND m.status = inner_cte.status JOIN instances AS i ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON ism.instance_uuid = i.uuid WHERE m.memory_total > 0 AND (CAST(m.memory_processed * 1.0 AS REAL) / m.memory_total) > 0.75) SELECT q1.ID AS instance_id, q1.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, q1.AVAILABILITY_ZONE AS availability_zone, q1.NETWORK_INFO AS cached_network_info, q1.window_column AS rank_within_availability_zone_by_instance_id, q1.deviation_from_avg_instance_id, q2.status AS completed_migration_status, q2.max_memory_total AS completed_migration_max_memory_total, q2.created_at AS migration_created_at FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q2.display_name = q1.DISPLAY_NAME
WITH forge_instances AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID, i.HOSTNAME, c.NETWORK_INFO, SUM(i.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY i.VM_STATE ORDER BY i.ID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS c ON i.UUID = c.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge') SELECT inner_cte.UUID, inner_cte.HOSTNAME, inner_cte.NETWORK_INFO, inner_cte.window_column, state_counts.total_forge_instances_in_state, avg_state_counts.avg_forge_instances_per_state FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT i.VM_STATE, COUNT(*) AS total_forge_instances_in_state FROM INSTANCES AS i WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge' GROUP BY i.VM_STATE) AS state_counts ON state_counts.VM_STATE = (SELECT i2.VM_STATE FROM INSTANCES AS i2 WHERE i2.UUID = inner_cte.UUID), (SELECT AVG(state_total) AS avg_forge_instances_per_state FROM (SELECT COUNT(*) AS state_total FROM INSTANCES WHERE DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge' GROUP BY VM_STATE) AS per_state) AS avg_state_counts), completed_shelved_migrations AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS completed_migrations_for_shelved_instances FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded') SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.HOSTNAME AS instance_hostname, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE AS source_compute, m.DEST_COMPUTE AS dest_compute, (CAST(m.MEMORY_PROCESSED * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(m.MEMORY_TOTAL, 0)) AS memory_processed_ratio, inner_cte.completed_migrations_for_shelved_instances FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID CROSS JOIN inner_cte WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded' AND (CAST(m.MEMORY_PROCESSED * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(m.MEMORY_TOTAL, 0)) > 0.75) SELECT fi.UUID AS forge_instance_uuid, fi.HOSTNAME AS forge_instance_hostname, fi.NETWORK_INFO AS forge_cached_network_info_json, fi.window_column AS running_sum_instance_ids_within_vm_state, fi.total_forge_instances_in_state, fi.avg_forge_instances_per_state, csm.source_compute, csm.dest_compute, csm.memory_processed_ratio, csm.completed_migrations_for_shelved_instances FROM forge_instances AS fi CROSS JOIN completed_shelved_migrations AS csm
WITH migration_details AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.STATUS AS migration_status, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, m.DEST_HOST, i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.VM_STATE AS instance_vm_state, c.NETWORK_INFO AS cached_network_info, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS c ON i.UUID = c.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'confirmed' AND m.SOURCE_COMPUTE = 'ether-60') SELECT inner_cte.migration_id, inner_cte.migration_status, inner_cte.SOURCE_COMPUTE, inner_cte.DEST_COMPUTE, inner_cte.DEST_HOST, inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.instance_display_name, inner_cte.instance_vm_state, inner_cte.cached_network_info, inner_cte.window_column, COUNT(inner_cte.migration_id) OVER () AS total_confirmed_ether60_migrations, avg_by_dest.avg_migrations_per_dest_compute FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(migrations_per_dest) AS avg_migrations_per_dest_compute FROM (SELECT DEST_COMPUTE, COUNT(*) AS migrations_per_dest FROM inner_cte GROUP BY DEST_COMPUTE) AS per_dest) AS avg_by_dest ON 1 = 1 ORDER BY inner_cte.migration_id), high_memory_shelved_instances AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, SUM(i.MEMORY_MB) AS total_memory_mb FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME HAVING SUM(i.MEMORY_MB) > 50000) SELECT inner_cte.DISPLAY_NAME, inner_cte.total_memory_mb FROM inner_cte WHERE inner_cte.total_memory_mb > (SELECT AVG(inner_cte.total_memory_mb * 1.0) FROM inner_cte)) SELECT md.migration_id, md.migration_status, md.SOURCE_COMPUTE, md.DEST_COMPUTE, md.DEST_HOST, md.instance_uuid, md.instance_display_name, md.instance_vm_state, md.cached_network_info, md.window_column, md.total_confirmed_ether60_migrations, md.avg_migrations_per_dest_compute, hmsi.total_memory_mb AS shelved_offloaded_total_memory_mb_for_display_name FROM migration_details AS md JOIN high_memory_shelved_instances AS hmsi ON md.instance_display_name = hmsi.DISPLAY_NAME ORDER BY md.migration_id
WITH shelved_instances AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.ID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, c.NETWORK_INFO, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE ORDER BY i.ID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS c ON i.UUID = c.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded'), avg_ids AS (SELECT AVG(ID) AS avg_instance_id FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.ID, ic.DISPLAY_NAME, ic.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, ic.NETWORK_INFO, ic.window_column, (ic.ID - ai.avg_instance_id) AS deviation_from_avg_instance_id FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_ids AS ai), completed_migration_memory_by_display_name AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, MAX(m.memory_total) AS max_memory_total, m.status FROM instances AS i JOIN migrations AS m ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' GROUP BY i.display_name, m.status HAVING MAX(m.memory_total) > 0) SELECT display_name, MAX(max_memory_total) AS max_of_max_memory_total FROM inner_cte GROUP BY display_name) SELECT si.ID AS instance_id, si.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, si.AVAILABILITY_ZONE AS availability_zone, si.NETWORK_INFO AS cached_network_info, si.window_column AS rank_within_availability_zone_by_instance_id, si.deviation_from_avg_instance_id, cm.max_of_max_memory_total FROM shelved_instances AS si JOIN completed_migration_memory_by_display_name AS cm ON si.DISPLAY_NAME = cm.display_name ORDER BY si.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, si.ID
WITH top_vm_states AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name, i.vm_state, COUNT(ie.id) AS extra_record_count, SUM(ie.id) AS extra_id_sum FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3' GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, i.vm_state) SELECT inner_cte.vm_state, COUNT(DISTINCT inner_cte.instance_uuid) AS instance_count, SUM(inner_cte.extra_record_count) AS total_extra_record_count, SUM(inner_cte.extra_id_sum) AS total_extra_id_sum FROM inner_cte GROUP BY inner_cte.vm_state ORDER BY total_extra_record_count DESC LIMIT 10), instance_utilization_detail AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY i.VM_STATE ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3'), StateCounts AS (SELECT vm_state, COUNT(*) AS total_instances FROM inner_cte GROUP BY vm_state), AverageStateCounts AS (SELECT AVG(total_instances) AS avg_instances FROM StateCounts) SELECT ic.instance_uuid, ic.display_name, ic.vm_state, ic.memory_mb, ic.vcpus, ic.instance_extra_id, ic.window_column, sc.total_instances, ascnts.avg_instances AS benchmark, CASE WHEN sc.total_instances > ascnts.avg_instances * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_UTILIZATION' WHEN sc.total_instances < ascnts.avg_instances * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_UTILIZATION' ELSE 'AVERAGE_UTILIZATION' END AS utilization_category FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN StateCounts AS sc ON ic.vm_state = sc.vm_state CROSS JOIN AverageStateCounts AS ascnts) SELECT tvs.vm_state, tvs.instance_count, tvs.total_extra_record_count, tvs.total_extra_id_sum, iud.total_instances, iud.benchmark, iud.utilization_category FROM top_vm_states AS tvs JOIN instance_utilization_detail AS iud ON tvs.vm_state = iud.vm_state GROUP BY tvs.vm_state, tvs.instance_count, tvs.total_extra_record_count, tvs.total_extra_id_sum, iud.total_instances, iud.benchmark, iud.utilization_category ORDER BY tvs.total_extra_record_count DESC
WITH NonDeletedAboveAvgMemory AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, i.vm_state, i.memory_mb, c.network_info, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY i.project_id ORDER BY i.project_id) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON i.uuid = c.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0) SELECT display_name, vm_state, memory_mb, network_info, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE memory_mb > (SELECT AVG(memory_mb) FROM inner_cte)), CompletedMigrationsWithWindow AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.UUID, i.MEMORY_MB, m.STATUS, m.DEST_HOST, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY i.UUID ORDER BY m.DEST_HOST) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' ORDER BY m.DEST_HOST) SELECT CASE WHEN LAG(DEST_HOST) OVER (ORDER BY DEST_HOST, UUID) = DEST_HOST THEN NULL ELSE DEST_HOST END AS dest_host, DISPLAY_NAME, UUID, MEMORY_MB, STATUS, window_column FROM inner_cte ORDER BY DEST_HOST, UUID), CompletedMigrationCounts AS (SELECT m.instance_uuid AS uuid, COUNT(*) AS Completed_Migration_Count FROM migrations AS m WHERE m.status = 'completed' GROUP BY m.instance_uuid) SELECT cm.dest_host, cm.display_name, cm.uuid, cm.memory_mb, nd.network_info, cm.status, cm.window_column AS Avg_Memory_MB_Within_UUID_By_DestHost, cmc.Completed_Migration_Count FROM CompletedMigrationsWithWindow AS cm JOIN instances AS i ON i.uuid = cm.uuid JOIN NonDeletedAboveAvgMemory AS nd ON nd.display_name = i.display_name AND nd.vm_state = i.vm_state AND nd.memory_mb = i.memory_mb JOIN CompletedMigrationCounts AS cmc ON cmc.uuid = cm.uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND cmc.Completed_Migration_Count >= 2 ORDER BY cm.dest_host, cm.uuid
WITH Subquery1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.HOSTNAME AS hostname, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY i.UUID ORDER BY ism.KEY) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3') SELECT instance_uuid, hostname, instance_extra_id, metadata_key, metadata_value, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE window_column > (SELECT AVG(window_column) FROM inner_cte)), Subquery2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, COUNT(ism.id) AS metadata_entries FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3' GROUP BY i.display_name ORDER BY metadata_entries DESC LIMIT 10), AverageMetadata AS (SELECT AVG(metadata_entries) AS avg_metadata_entries FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.display_name, ic.metadata_entries, am.avg_metadata_entries, (ic.metadata_entries - am.avg_metadata_entries) AS deviation_from_avg FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageMetadata AS am) SELECT s1.instance_uuid, s1.hostname, i.display_name, s1.instance_extra_id, s1.metadata_key, s1.metadata_value, s1.window_column AS running_avg_instance_memory_mb, s2.metadata_entries, s2.avg_metadata_entries, s2.deviation_from_avg FROM Subquery1 AS s1 JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON i.uuid = s1.instance_uuid JOIN Subquery2 AS s2 ON s2.display_name = i.display_name
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME, COUNT(i.ID) AS instance_count, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) AS min_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(min_memory_mb) AS avg_min_memory_mb FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.VM_STATE, ic.DISPLAY_NAME, ic.instance_count, ic.min_memory_mb, (ic.min_memory_mb - ac.avg_min_memory_mb) AS deviation_from_avg_min_memory_mb FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS ac), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT MIN(i.ID) AS min_instance_id, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) AS avg_memory_mb FROM INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON i.UUID = iic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME) SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, inner_cte.min_instance_id, inner_cte.avg_memory_mb FROM inner_cte JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON i.ID = inner_cte.min_instance_id WHERE inner_cte.avg_memory_mb > (SELECT AVG(inner_cte2.avg_memory_mb) FROM inner_cte AS inner_cte2)) SELECT q1.VM_STATE, q1.DISPLAY_NAME, q1.instance_count, q1.min_memory_mb, q1.deviation_from_avg_min_memory_mb, q2.min_instance_id, q2.avg_memory_mb FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.DISPLAY_NAME = q2.DISPLAY_NAME
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.VM_STATE AS instance_vm_state, m.STATUS AS migration_status, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE AS migration_source_compute, m.DEST_COMPUTE AS migration_dest_compute, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded' AND m.STATUS = 'completed'), avg_window AS (SELECT AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.instance_uuid, ic.instance_display_name, ic.instance_vm_state, ic.migration_status, ic.migration_source_compute, ic.migration_dest_compute, ic.window_column, (ic.window_column - aw.avg_window_column) AS deviation_from_avg_row_number FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_window AS aw), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, COUNT(m.id) AS migrations FROM instances AS i JOIN migrations AS m ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' GROUP BY i.display_name ORDER BY migrations DESC LIMIT 10), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(migrations) AS avg_migrations FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.display_name, ic.migrations, (ic.migrations - ac.avg_migrations) AS deviation_from_avg FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS ac ORDER BY ic.migrations DESC) SELECT q1.instance_uuid, q1.instance_display_name, q1.instance_vm_state, q1.migration_status, q1.migration_source_compute, q1.migration_dest_compute, q1.window_column, q1.deviation_from_avg_row_number, q2.migrations AS completed_migrations_for_display_name_in_top10, q2.deviation_from_avg AS deviation_from_avg_completed_migrations_in_top10 FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.instance_display_name = q2.display_name
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, EXP(AVG(LN(i.memory_mb))) AS geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON ism.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS iic ON i.uuid = iic.instance_uuid WHERE i.memory_mb > 0 AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.display_name ORDER BY geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC) SELECT inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.geometric_mean_memory_mb, COUNT(DISTINCT i.uuid) AS contributing_instance_count FROM inner_cte JOIN instances AS i ON i.display_name = inner_cte.display_name JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON ism.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS iic ON i.uuid = iic.instance_uuid WHERE i.memory_mb > 0 AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.geometric_mean_memory_mb ORDER BY inner_cte.geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC LIMIT 10), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.uuid, i.display_name, i.vm_state, i.memory_mb, c.network_info, AVG(i.memory_mb) OVER (PARTITION BY i.vm_state ORDER BY i.memory_mb ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON i.uuid = c.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3' ORDER BY i.memory_mb) SELECT inner_cte.uuid, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.network_info, inner_cte.window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE inner_cte.memory_mb > (SELECT AVG(memory_mb) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY inner_cte.memory_mb) SELECT q2.uuid, q2.display_name, q2.vm_state, q2.memory_mb, q2.network_info, q2.window_column, q1.geometric_mean_memory_mb, q1.contributing_instance_count FROM q2 JOIN q1 ON q1.display_name = q2.display_name JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON ism.instance_uuid = q2.uuid WHERE ism.key = 'image_image_type' ORDER BY q1.geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC, q2.memory_mb DESC
WITH top_vmstate_az_by_image_type AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT ins.VM_STATE, ins.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, COUNT(ins.UUID) AS instance_count, MIN(ins.MEMORY_MB) AS min_memory_mb FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS ins ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = ins.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ins.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY ins.VM_STATE, ins.AVAILABILITY_ZONE) SELECT inner_cte.VM_STATE, inner_cte.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, inner_cte.instance_count, inner_cte.min_memory_mb FROM inner_cte ORDER BY inner_cte.instance_count DESC LIMIT 10), shelved_offloaded_completed_migration_stats AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) AS min_instance_memory_mb, SUM(m.MEMORY_PROCESSED) AS total_migration_memory_processed FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic ON i.UUID = ic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded') SELECT i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, inner_cte.min_instance_memory_mb, inner_cte.total_migration_memory_processed, CASE WHEN EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM MIGRATIONS AS m2 JOIN INSTANCES AS i2 ON m2.INSTANCE_UUID = i2.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic2 ON i2.UUID = ic2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m2.STATUS = 'completed' AND i2.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded' AND ic2.DELETED = 0) THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_network_info_cache FROM inner_cte JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded' GROUP BY i.VM_STATE, inner_cte.min_instance_memory_mb, inner_cte.total_migration_memory_processed) SELECT COUNT(*) AS top_vmstate_az_combinations_count, CASE WHEN EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM top_vmstate_az_by_image_type AS t JOIN shelved_offloaded_completed_migration_stats AS s ON s.vm_state = t.VM_STATE) THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS any_top_combination_matches_shelved_offloaded_migration_vm_state FROM top_vmstate_az_by_image_type
WITH MigrationMemoryStats AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, m.status, MAX(m.memory_total) AS max_memory_total, m.created_at FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.deleted = 0 GROUP BY i.display_name, m.status, m.created_at HAVING MAX(m.memory_total) > 0) SELECT inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.status, inner_cte.max_memory_total, inner_cte.created_at FROM inner_cte JOIN migrations AS m ON m.created_at = inner_cte.created_at AND m.status = inner_cte.status JOIN instances AS i ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON ism.instance_uuid = i.uuid WHERE m.memory_total > 0 AND (CAST(m.memory_processed * 1.0 AS REAL) / m.memory_total) > 0.75), Flare3HighVcpuMetadata AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3'), VmStateAverages AS (SELECT i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, AVG(i.VCPUS * 1.0) AS avg_vcpus FROM INSTANCES AS i WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' GROUP BY i.VM_STATE) SELECT ic.instance_uuid, ic.display_name, ic.vm_state, ic.memory_mb, ic.vcpus, ic.metadata_key, ic.metadata_value, ic.window_column FROM inner_cte AS ic WHERE ic.vcpus > (SELECT vsa.avg_vcpus FROM VmStateAverages AS vsa WHERE vsa.vm_state = ic.vm_state)) SELECT f.instance_uuid, f.display_name, f.vm_state, f.memory_mb, f.vcpus, f.metadata_key, f.metadata_value, f.window_column, mms.status AS migration_status, mms.max_memory_total, mms.created_at AS migration_created_at FROM Flare3HighVcpuMetadata AS f JOIN MigrationMemoryStats AS mms ON mms.display_name = f.display_name ORDER BY f.instance_uuid, f.metadata_key, f.window_column
WITH q1_inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, ie.CREATED_AT AS instance_extra_created_at, iic.NETWORK_INFO AS network_info, MIN(ie.ID) OVER (ORDER BY i.MEMORY_MB ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge'), q1 AS (SELECT CASE WHEN LAG(display_name) OVER (ORDER BY memory_mb, instance_uuid) = display_name THEN NULL ELSE display_name END AS display_name, instance_uuid, vm_state, memory_mb, vcpus, instance_extra_id, instance_extra_created_at, network_info, window_column FROM q1_inner_cte ORDER BY memory_mb, instance_uuid), q2_inner_cte AS (SELECT t0.UUID, t0.HOSTNAME, t0.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, t2.NETWORK_INFO, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY t0.AVAILABILITY_ZONE ORDER BY t0.ID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS t0 JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS t1 ON t1.INSTANCE_UUID = t0.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS t2 ON t2.INSTANCE_UUID = t0.UUID WHERE t0.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge'), q2_avg_ids AS (SELECT AVG(t0.ID) AS avg_instance_id FROM INSTANCES AS t0 WHERE t0.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge'), q2 AS (SELECT i.UUID, i.HOSTNAME, i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, i.NETWORK_INFO, i.window_column AS rank_within_az_by_id, (t0.ID - a.avg_instance_id) AS deviation_from_avg_instance_id FROM q2_inner_cte AS i JOIN INSTANCES AS t0 ON t0.UUID = i.UUID CROSS JOIN q2_avg_ids AS a) SELECT q2.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, COUNT(*) AS forge_instance_count, SUM(q1.vcpus) AS total_vcpus, ROUND(CAST(SUM(q1.vcpus) AS REAL) / NULLIF(SUM(q1.memory_mb), 0), 6) AS normalized_vcpu_per_mb_ratio, ROUND(AVG(q2.deviation_from_avg_instance_id), 2) AS avg_deviation_from_avg_instance_id FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q2.UUID = q1.instance_uuid GROUP BY q2.AVAILABILITY_ZONE ORDER BY normalized_vcpu_per_mb_ratio DESC
WITH image_type_above_avg AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT t1.UUID AS instance_uuid, t1.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, t0.VALUE AS metadata_value, t2.NETWORK_INFO AS network_info, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY t0.VALUE ORDER BY t1.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS t0 JOIN INSTANCES AS t1 ON t0.INSTANCE_UUID = t1.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS t2 ON t1.UUID = t2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE t0.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND t0.DELETED = 0), ValueCounts AS (SELECT metadata_value, COUNT(*) AS value_instance_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY metadata_value), AvgValueCount AS (SELECT AVG(value_instance_count * 1.0) AS avg_value_instance_count FROM ValueCounts) SELECT i.instance_uuid, i.instance_display_name, i.metadata_value, i.network_info FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ValueCounts AS vc ON i.metadata_value = vc.metadata_value WHERE vc.value_instance_count > (SELECT avg_value_instance_count FROM AvgValueCount)), top10_display_names_flare3 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, COUNT(iic.id) AS cache_records FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON i.uuid = iic.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3' GROUP BY i.display_name ORDER BY cache_records DESC LIMIT 10) SELECT inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.cache_records, SUM(i.vcpus) AS total_vcpus, SUM(i.memory_mb) AS total_memory_mb FROM inner_cte JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON i.display_name = inner_cte.display_name AND i.availability_zone = 'flare3' GROUP BY inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.cache_records ORDER BY inner_cte.cache_records DESC) SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT ita.instance_uuid) AS matching_instance_count, COUNT(DISTINCT t10.display_name) AS matching_display_name_count FROM image_type_above_avg AS ita JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON i.uuid = ita.instance_uuid JOIN top10_display_names_flare3 AS t10 ON t10.display_name = i.display_name WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3'
WITH az_mem AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, ie.FLAVOR AS flavor, iic.NETWORK_INFO AS network_info, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE ORDER BY i.MEMORY_MB) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' ORDER BY i.MEMORY_MB) SELECT instance_uuid, display_name, memory_mb, flavor, network_info, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE memory_mb > (SELECT AVG(memory_mb) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY memory_mb), forge_metadata AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, ism.key AS metadata_key, ism.value AS metadata_value, i.power_state, SUM(i.id) OVER (PARTITION BY i.power_state ORDER BY i.id) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ism.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON i.uuid = iic.instance_uuid WHERE i.display_name = 'forge') SELECT inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.metadata_key, inner_cte.metadata_value, inner_cte.power_state, inner_cte.window_column, stats.total_rows, stats.avg_window_column FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT COUNT(*) AS total_rows, AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte) AS stats ON 1 = 1) SELECT az_mem.instance_uuid, az_mem.display_name, az_mem.memory_mb, az_mem.flavor, az_mem.network_info, az_mem.window_column AS az_running_avg_memory_mb, forge_metadata.metadata_key, forge_metadata.metadata_value, forge_metadata.power_state, forge_metadata.window_column AS forge_running_sum_instance_ids, forge_metadata.total_rows AS forge_total_rows, forge_metadata.avg_window_column AS forge_avg_running_sum_instance_ids FROM az_mem JOIN forge_metadata ON az_mem.instance_uuid = forge_metadata.instance_uuid ORDER BY az_mem.memory_mb
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.POWER_STATE AS power_state, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, iic.NETWORK_INFO AS network_info, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded') SELECT CASE WHEN LAG(metadata_key) OVER (ORDER BY metadata_key, instance_uuid) = metadata_key THEN NULL ELSE metadata_key END AS metadata_key, instance_uuid, display_name, power_state, metadata_value, network_info, window_column, COUNT(*) OVER (PARTITION BY metadata_key) AS total_rows_per_key, AVG(power_state) OVER (PARTITION BY metadata_key) AS avg_power_state_per_key FROM inner_cte ORDER BY metadata_key, instance_uuid), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, i.vm_state, i.memory_mb, c.network_info, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY i.project_id ORDER BY i.project_id) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON i.uuid = c.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0) SELECT inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.network_info, inner_cte.window_column FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(i2.memory_mb) AS avg_memory_mb FROM instances AS i2 WHERE i2.deleted = 0) AS mem_avg ON 1 = 1 WHERE inner_cte.memory_mb > mem_avg.avg_memory_mb) SELECT q1.metadata_key, q1.instance_uuid, q1.display_name AS shelved_display_name, q1.power_state, q1.metadata_value, q1.network_info AS shelved_network_info, q1.window_column AS per_key_row_number, q1.total_rows_per_key, q1.avg_power_state_per_key, q2.display_name AS high_mem_display_name, q2.vm_state AS high_mem_vm_state, q2.memory_mb AS high_mem_memory_mb, q2.network_info AS high_mem_network_info, q2.window_column AS high_mem_dense_rank FROM q1 LEFT JOIN q2 ON q1.display_name = q2.display_name AND q1.network_info = q2.network_info ORDER BY q1.metadata_key, q1.instance_uuid
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.VM_STATE, ism.KEY, ism.VALUE, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT inner_cte.UUID, inner_cte.DISPLAY_NAME, inner_cte.VM_STATE, inner_cte.KEY, inner_cte.VALUE, inner_cte.window_column FROM inner_cte JOIN INSTANCES AS i2 ON i2.UUID = inner_cte.UUID WHERE i2.MEMORY_MB > (SELECT AVG(i3.MEMORY_MB * 1.0) FROM INSTANCES AS i3 WHERE i3.DELETED = 0 AND i3.HOST = 'blaze8-12')), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name, iic.network_info, COUNT(ie.id) AS extra_record_count, SUM(i.memory_mb) AS total_memory_mb FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON ie.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS iic ON iic.instance_uuid = i.uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND i.availability_zone = 'flare3' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, iic.network_info) SELECT inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.network_info, inner_cte.extra_record_count, inner_cte.total_memory_mb FROM inner_cte WHERE (CAST(inner_cte.total_memory_mb * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(inner_cte.extra_record_count, 0)) > 16384) SELECT q2.instance_uuid, q2.display_name, q2.network_info, q2.extra_record_count, q2.total_memory_mb, q1.VM_STATE, q1.KEY AS system_metadata_key, q1.VALUE AS system_metadata_value, q1.window_column AS metadata_rank_within_key FROM q2 JOIN q1 ON q1.UUID = q2.instance_uuid ORDER BY q2.instance_uuid, q1.KEY, q1.window_column
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, MIN(i.ID) AS min_instance_id, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) AS avg_memory_mb FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME) SELECT inner_cte.DISPLAY_NAME, inner_cte.min_instance_id, inner_cte.avg_memory_mb, overall_avg.avg_of_avg_memory_mb FROM inner_cte, (SELECT AVG(avg_memory_mb) AS avg_of_avg_memory_mb FROM inner_cte) AS overall_avg), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, SUM(i.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY i.VM_STATE ORDER BY i.ID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.window_column, COUNT(inner_cte.instance_uuid) OVER (PARTITION BY inner_cte.vm_state) AS total_instances_in_vm_state, avg_counts.avg_instance_count_per_vm_state FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(vm_state_count) AS avg_instance_count_per_vm_state FROM (SELECT vm_state, COUNT(*) AS vm_state_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY vm_state) AS sub) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1) SELECT q2.instance_uuid, q2.display_name, q2.vm_state, q2.window_column, q2.total_instances_in_vm_state, q2.avg_instance_count_per_vm_state, q1.min_instance_id, q1.avg_memory_mb, q1.avg_of_avg_memory_mb FROM q2 JOIN q1 ON q2.display_name = q1.DISPLAY_NAME
WITH migration_mem_window AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.uuid, i.display_name, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, m.status, MIN(i.memory_mb) OVER (ORDER BY m.id ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN migrations AS m ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12') SELECT uuid, display_name, source_compute, dest_compute, status, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE window_column > (SELECT AVG(window_column) FROM inner_cte)), instance_state_mem_stats AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY i.VM_STATE ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), avg_mem AS (SELECT AVG(memory_mb) AS avg_memory_mb FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.instance_uuid, ic.display_name, ic.vm_state, ic.memory_mb, ic.vcpus, ic.instance_extra_id, ic.window_column, (ic.memory_mb - am.avg_memory_mb) AS deviation_from_avg_memory_mb FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_mem AS am), counts AS (SELECT (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM migration_mem_window) AS migration_rows_count, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM instance_state_mem_stats) AS instance_rows_count) SELECT c.migration_rows_count, c.instance_rows_count FROM counts AS c
WITH metadata_cte AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.FLAVOR AS flavor, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.vcpus, inner_cte.flavor, inner_cte.metadata_key, inner_cte.metadata_value, inner_cte.window_column, key_counts.total_rows_for_key, avg_counts.avg_rows_per_key FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT metadata_key, COUNT(*) AS total_rows_for_key FROM inner_cte GROUP BY metadata_key) AS key_counts ON key_counts.metadata_key = inner_cte.metadata_key JOIN (SELECT AVG(rows_per_key) AS avg_rows_per_key FROM (SELECT metadata_key, COUNT(*) AS rows_per_key FROM inner_cte GROUP BY metadata_key) AS per_key) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1), vmstate_cte AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY
