i.VM_STATE ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT CASE WHEN LAG(vm_state) OVER (ORDER BY vm_state, instance_uuid) = vm_state THEN NULL ELSE vm_state END AS vm_state, instance_uuid, display_name, memory_mb, vcpus, instance_extra_id, window_column FROM inner_cte ORDER BY vm_state, instance_uuid) SELECT v.vm_state, v.instance_uuid, v.display_name, v.memory_mb, v.vcpus, v.instance_extra_id, v.window_column AS vm_state_row_number, m.flavor, m.metadata_key, m.metadata_value, m.window_column AS metadata_key_row_number, m.total_rows_for_key, m.avg_rows_per_key FROM vmstate_cte AS v JOIN metadata_cte AS m ON m.instance_uuid = v.instance_uuid ORDER BY v.vm_state, v.instance_uuid, m.metadata_key, m.window_column
WITH blaze_host_minmax AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.HOSTNAME AS instance_hostname, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE AS migration_source_compute, m.DEST_COMPUTE AS migration_dest_compute, m.DEST_HOST AS migration_dest_host, m.MIGRATION_TYPE AS migration_type, m.MEMORY_TOTAL AS memory_total, m.MEMORY_PROCESSED AS memory_processed, m.MEMORY_REMAINING AS memory_remaining, MIN(m.MEMORY_REMAINING) OVER (PARTITION BY m.INSTANCE_UUID ORDER BY m.INSTANCE_UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT i.HOST AS instance_host, MAX(inner_cte.window_column) AS max_of_instance_min_memory_remaining FROM inner_cte JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON i.UUID = inner_cte.instance_uuid GROUP BY i.HOST), ether_dest_compute_details AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.STATUS AS migration_status, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, m.DEST_HOST, m.INSTANCE_UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.VM_STATE AS instance_vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS instance_memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS instance_vcpus, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, m.MEMORY_REMAINING AS migration_memory_remaining, MIN(m.MEMORY_REMAINING) OVER (PARTITION BY m.DEST_COMPUTE ORDER BY m.DEST_COMPUTE) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'ether-18') SELECT CASE WHEN LAG(DEST_COMPUTE) OVER (ORDER BY DEST_COMPUTE, migration_id, metadata_key) = DEST_COMPUTE THEN NULL ELSE DEST_COMPUTE END AS dest_compute, migration_id, migration_status, SOURCE_COMPUTE, DEST_HOST, INSTANCE_UUID, instance_display_name, instance_vm_state, instance_memory_mb, instance_vcpus, metadata_key, metadata_value, migration_memory_remaining, window_column AS min_migration_memory_remaining_in_dest_compute, COUNT(*) OVER (PARTITION BY DEST_COMPUTE) AS total_rows_in_dest_compute FROM inner_cte ORDER BY DEST_COMPUTE, migration_id, metadata_key) SELECT ed.dest_compute, ed.total_rows_in_dest_compute, ed.min_migration_memory_remaining_in_dest_compute, bh.instance_host AS blaze_instance_host, bh.max_of_instance_min_memory_remaining, ROUND(CAST(ed.min_migration_memory_remaining_in_dest_compute AS REAL) / NULLIF(bh.max_of_instance_min_memory_remaining, 0), 4) AS min_dest_compute_memory_remaining_to_blaze_max_instance_min_ratio FROM ether_dest_compute_details AS ed CROSS JOIN blaze_host_minmax AS bh ORDER BY ed.dest_compute, ed.migration_id, ed.metadata_key
WITH metadata_rows AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.FLAVOR AS flavor, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.vcpus, inner_cte.flavor, inner_cte.metadata_key, inner_cte.metadata_value, inner_cte.window_column, key_counts.total_rows_for_key, avg_counts.avg_rows_per_key FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT metadata_key, COUNT(*) AS total_rows_for_key FROM inner_cte GROUP BY metadata_key) AS key_counts ON key_counts.metadata_key = inner_cte.metadata_key JOIN (SELECT AVG(rows_per_key) AS avg_rows_per_key FROM (SELECT metadata_key, COUNT(*) AS rows_per_key FROM inner_cte GROUP BY metadata_key) AS per_key) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1), vmstate_rows AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY i.VM_STATE ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT CASE WHEN LAG(vm_state) OVER (ORDER BY vm_state, instance_uuid) = vm_state THEN NULL ELSE vm_state END AS vm_state, instance_uuid, display_name, memory_mb, vcpus, instance_extra_id, window_column FROM inner_cte ORDER BY vm_state, instance_uuid) SELECT v.vm_state, v.instance_uuid, v.display_name, v.memory_mb, v.vcpus, v.instance_extra_id, v.window_column AS vm_state_row_number, m.flavor, m.metadata_key, m.metadata_value, m.window_column AS metadata_key_row_number, m.total_rows_for_key, m.avg_rows_per_key FROM vmstate_rows AS v JOIN metadata_rows AS m ON m.instance_uuid = v.instance_uuid ORDER BY v.vm_state, v.instance_uuid, m.metadata_key, m.window_column
WITH sq1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, MIN(i.ID) AS min_instance_id, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) AS avg_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME) SELECT ic.DISPLAY_NAME, ic.min_instance_id, ic.avg_memory_mb, CASE WHEN EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM INSTANCES AS i2 JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism2 ON i2.UUID = ism2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i2.DISPLAY_NAME = ic.DISPLAY_NAME AND i2.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i2.HOST = 'blaze8-12' AND ism2.KEY = 'image_image_location') THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_image_location_metadata FROM inner_cte AS ic ORDER BY ic.avg_memory_mb DESC LIMIT 10), sq2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.MEMORY_MB, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT inner_cte.migration_id, inner_cte.SOURCE_COMPUTE, inner_cte.DEST_COMPUTE, inner_cte.DISPLAY_NAME, inner_cte.MEMORY_MB, inner_cte.metadata_key, inner_cte.metadata_value, inner_cte.window_column FROM inner_cte CROSS JOIN (SELECT MAX(MEMORY_MB) AS max_memory_mb FROM inner_cte) AS mem_stats WHERE (CAST(inner_cte.MEMORY_MB * 1.0 AS REAL) / mem_stats.max_memory_mb) > 0.75) SELECT s1.DISPLAY_NAME, s1.min_instance_id, s1.avg_memory_mb, s1.has_image_location_metadata, COUNT(s2.migration_id) AS completed_migration_rows_over_75pct_max_mem FROM sq1 AS s1 LEFT JOIN sq2 AS s2 ON s2.DISPLAY_NAME = s1.DISPLAY_NAME GROUP BY s1.DISPLAY_NAME, s1.min_instance_id, s1.avg_memory_mb, s1.has_image_location_metadata ORDER BY s1.avg_memory_mb DESC
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.HOSTNAME, i.VM_STATE, i.MEMORY_MB, i.VCPUS, i.UUID, m.STATUS, MAX(m.ID) OVER (ORDER BY i.UUID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_migration_id_next2 FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12') SELECT inner_cte.DISPLAY_NAME, inner_cte.HOSTNAME, inner_cte.VM_STATE, inner_cte.MEMORY_MB, inner_cte.VCPUS, inner_cte.UUID, inner_cte.STATUS, inner_cte.max_migration_id_next2, host_stats.completed_migrations_on_host, avg_stats.avg_completed_migrations_per_host FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT i.host AS host, COUNT(*) AS completed_migrations_on_host FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' GROUP BY i.host) AS host_stats ON host_stats.host = 'blaze8-12', (SELECT AVG(completed_migration_count) AS avg_completed_migrations_per_host FROM (SELECT i.host AS host, COUNT(*) AS completed_migration_count FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' GROUP BY i.host) AS sub) AS avg_stats), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.STATUS AS migration_status, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, ic.NETWORK_INFO, AVG(m.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic ON i.UUID = ic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' ORDER BY m.ID) SELECT migration_id, migration_status, SOURCE_COMPUTE, DEST_COMPUTE, instance_display_name, NETWORK_INFO, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE migration_id > (SELECT AVG(migration_id) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY migration_id) SELECT q1.DISPLAY_NAME, q1.HOSTNAME, q1.VM_STATE, q1.MEMORY_MB, q1.VCPUS, q1.UUID, q1.STATUS, q1.max_migration_id_next2, q1.completed_migrations_on_host, q1.avg_completed_migrations_per_host, q2.migration_id, q2.migration_status, q2.SOURCE_COMPUTE, q2.DEST_COMPUTE, q2.instance_display_name, q2.NETWORK_INFO, q2.window_column FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.UUID = (SELECT m.INSTANCE_UUID FROM MIGRATIONS AS m WHERE m.ID = q2.migration_id) ORDER BY q2.migration_id
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.status, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, i.display_name, ie.flavor, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.status ORDER BY m.status) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12'), avg_rank AS (SELECT AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.status, ic.source_compute, ic.dest_compute, ic.display_name, ic.flavor, ic.window_column, ar.avg_window_column, (ic.window_column - ar.avg_window_column) AS deviation_from_avg_window_column FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_rank AS ar), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.uuid, i.display_name, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, m.status, MIN(i.memory_mb) OVER (ORDER BY m.id ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN migrations AS m ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12') SELECT inner_cte.uuid, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.source_compute, inner_cte.dest_compute, inner_cte.status, inner_cte.window_column, i.project_id, SUM(i_all.vcpus) AS project_total_vcpus FROM inner_cte JOIN instances AS i ON i.uuid = inner_cte.uuid JOIN instances AS i_all ON i_all.project_id = i.project_id AND i_all.deleted = 0 GROUP BY inner_cte.uuid, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.source_compute, inner_cte.dest_compute, inner_cte.status, inner_cte.window_column, i.project_id ORDER BY project_total_vcpus DESC, inner_cte.uuid) SELECT q2.uuid AS instance_uuid, q2.display_name AS instance_display_name, q2.status AS migration_status, q2.source_compute, q2.dest_compute, q1.flavor AS instance_flavor_json, q1.window_column AS dense_rank_value, q1.avg_window_column AS avg_dense_rank_value, q1.deviation_from_avg_window_column AS dense_rank_deviation_from_avg, q2.window_column AS min_instance_memory_mb_window_3_preceding_1_following, q2.project_id, q2.project_total_vcpus, ROUND(CAST(q2.project_total_vcpus AS REAL) / NULLIF(q1.avg_window_column, 0), 2) AS project_vcpus_per_avg_dense_rank FROM q2 JOIN q1 ON q1.status = q2.status AND q1.source_compute = q2.source_compute AND q1.dest_compute = q2.dest_compute AND q1.display_name = q2.display_name ORDER BY project_vcpus_per_avg_dense_rank DESC, q2.project_total_vcpus DESC, q2.uuid
WITH GeoMeanByAZ AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.availability_zone, EXP(AVG(LN(i.memory_mb))) AS geometric_mean FROM instance_extra AS ie JOIN instances AS i ON ie.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND i.memory_mb > 0 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.availability_zone ORDER BY geometric_mean DESC), OverallGeoMean AS (SELECT EXP(AVG(LN(i.memory_mb))) AS overall_geometric_mean FROM instance_extra AS ie JOIN instances AS i ON ie.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND i.memory_mb > 0 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12') SELECT inner_cte.availability_zone, inner_cte.geometric_mean FROM inner_cte WHERE inner_cte.geometric_mean > (SELECT overall_geometric_mean FROM OverallGeoMean) ORDER BY inner_cte.geometric_mean DESC), MaxNetworkCacheInstancesByAZ AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS flare3_instances_with_network_cache FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.DELETED = 0 AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), ZoneCounts AS (SELECT i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE AS availability_zone, COUNT(*) AS instances_with_network_cache_on_host FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE) SELECT availability_zone, MAX(instances_with_network_cache_on_host) AS max_instances_with_network_cache_on_host FROM ZoneCounts GROUP BY availability_zone) SELECT g.availability_zone, g.geometric_mean AS geometric_mean_instance_memory_mb, m.max_instances_with_network_cache_on_host FROM GeoMeanByAZ AS g JOIN MaxNetworkCacheInstancesByAZ AS m ON g.availability_zone = m.availability_zone ORDER BY g.geometric_mean DESC
WITH migration_details AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.status, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, i.display_name, ie.flavor, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.status ORDER BY m.status) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12'), avg_rank AS (SELECT AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.status, ic.source_compute, ic.dest_compute, ic.display_name, ic.flavor, ic.window_column, ar.avg_window_column, (ic.window_column - ar.avg_window_column) AS deviation_from_avg_window_column FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_rank AS ar), instance_details AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.uuid, i.display_name, i.vm_state, i.memory_mb, c.network_info, AVG(i.memory_mb) OVER (PARTITION BY i.vm_state ORDER BY i.memory_mb ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON i.uuid = c.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' ORDER BY i.memory_mb) SELECT inner_cte.uuid, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.network_info, inner_cte.window_column, state_counts.total_in_state, avg_state_counts.avg_instances_per_state FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT vm_state, COUNT(*) AS total_in_state FROM inner_cte GROUP BY vm_state) AS state_counts ON inner_cte.vm_state = state_counts.vm_state, (SELECT AVG(state_total) AS avg_instances_per_state FROM (SELECT COUNT(*) AS state_total FROM inner_cte GROUP BY vm_state) AS sub) AS avg_state_counts ORDER BY inner_cte.memory_mb) SELECT id.uuid, id.display_name, id.vm_state, id.memory_mb, id.network_info, id.window_column AS memory_mb_window_avg, id.total_in_state, id.avg_instances_per_state, md.status AS migration_status, md.source_compute, md.dest_compute, md.flavor, md.window_column AS migration_dense_rank, md.avg_window_column AS avg_migration_dense_rank, md.deviation_from_avg_window_column AS migration_dense_rank_deviation FROM instance_details AS id JOIN migration_details AS md ON id.display_name = md.display_name WHERE id.total_in_state > id.avg_instances_per_state ORDER BY id.memory_mb
WITH top_display_names_by_memory AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, MIN(i.ID) AS min_instance_id, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) AS avg_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME) SELECT ic.DISPLAY_NAME, ic.min_instance_id, ic.avg_memory_mb, CASE WHEN EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM INSTANCES AS i2 JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism2 ON i2.UUID = ism2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i2.DISPLAY_NAME = ic.DISPLAY_NAME AND i2.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i2.HOST = 'blaze8-12' AND ism2.KEY = 'image_image_location') THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_image_location_metadata FROM inner_cte AS ic ORDER BY ic.avg_memory_mb DESC LIMIT 10), vm_state_display_name_counts AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME, COUNT(i.UUID) AS instance_count, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) AS min_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.VCPUS > 8 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME) SELECT inner_cte.VM_STATE, inner_cte.DISPLAY_NAME, inner_cte.instance_count, inner_cte.min_memory_mb, avg_counts.avg_instance_count FROM inner_cte, (SELECT AVG(instance_count) AS avg_instance_count FROM inner_cte) AS avg_counts) SELECT tdn.DISPLAY_NAME, tdn.min_instance_id, tdn.avg_memory_mb, tdn.has_image_location_metadata, vsd.VM_STATE, vsd.instance_count, vsd.min_memory_mb, vsd.avg_instance_count FROM top_display_names_by_memory AS tdn JOIN vm_state_display_name_counts AS vsd ON vsd.DISPLAY_NAME = tdn.DISPLAY_NAME ORDER BY tdn.avg_memory_mb DESC, vsd.VM_STATE, vsd.instance_count DESC
WITH sq1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.FLAVOR AS flavor, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), HostAverages AS (SELECT i.HOST AS host, i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE AS availability_zone, AVG(i.VCPUS * 1.0) AS avg_vcpus FROM INSTANCES AS i WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.HOST, i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE) SELECT inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.vcpus, inner_cte.flavor, inner_cte.metadata_key, inner_cte.metadata_value, inner_cte.window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE inner_cte.vcpus > (SELECT HostAverages.avg_vcpus FROM HostAverages) ORDER BY inner_cte.metadata_key, inner_cte.instance_uuid), sq2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.availability_zone, EXP(AVG(LN(i.memory_mb))) AS geometric_mean FROM instance_extra AS ie JOIN instances AS i ON ie.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND i.memory_mb > 0 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.availability_zone ORDER BY geometric_mean DESC) SELECT availability_zone, MAX(geometric_mean) AS max_geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM inner_cte GROUP BY availability_zone ORDER BY max_geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC) SELECT sq1.instance_uuid, sq1.display_name, sq1.memory_mb, sq1.vcpus, sq1.flavor, sq1.metadata_key, sq1.metadata_value, sq1.window_column, sq2.availability_zone, sq2.max_geometric_mean_memory_mb, ROUND(CAST(sq1.memory_mb AS REAL) / NULLIF(sq2.max_geometric_mean_memory_mb, 0), 4) AS normalized_memory_vs_zone_geomean FROM sq1 JOIN sq2 ON sq2.availability_zone = 'flare3' ORDER BY normalized_memory_vs_zone_geomean DESC, sq1.metadata_key, sq1.instance_uuid
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.FLAVOR AS flavor, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.vcpus, inner_cte.flavor, inner_cte.metadata_key, inner_cte.metadata_value, inner_cte.window_column, key_counts.total_rows_for_key, avg_counts.avg_rows_per_key FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT metadata_key, COUNT(*) AS total_rows_for_key FROM inner_cte GROUP BY metadata_key) AS key_counts ON key_counts.metadata_key = inner_cte.metadata_key JOIN (SELECT AVG(rows_per_key) AS avg_rows_per_key FROM (SELECT metadata_key, COUNT(*) AS rows_per_key FROM inner_cte GROUP BY metadata_key) AS per_key) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY i.VM_STATE ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT CASE WHEN LAG(vm_state) OVER (ORDER BY vm_state, instance_uuid) = vm_state THEN NULL ELSE vm_state END AS vm_state, instance_uuid, display_name, memory_mb, vcpus, instance_extra_id, window_column FROM inner_cte ORDER BY vm_state, instance_uuid), vm_state_totals AS (SELECT i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, COUNT(*) AS total_instances_in_vm_state FROM INSTANCES AS i WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.VM_STATE) SELECT q2.vm_state, q2.instance_uuid, q2.display_name, q2.memory_mb, q2.vcpus, q2.instance_extra_id, q2.window_column AS vm_state_row_number, q1.metadata_key, q1.metadata_value, q1.window_column AS metadata_key_row_number, q1.total_rows_for_key, q1.avg_rows_per_key, vst.total_instances_in_vm_state, (CAST(vst.total_instances_in_vm_state * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(q1.total_rows_for_key, 0)) AS vm_state_to_metadata_key_row_ratio FROM q2 JOIN q1 ON q1.instance_uuid = q2.instance_uuid JOIN vm_state_totals AS vst ON vst.vm_state = q2.vm_state WHERE (CAST(vst.total_instances_in_vm_state * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(q1.total_rows_for_key, 0)) > 0.75 ORDER BY q2.vm_state, q2.instance_uuid, q1.metadata_key
WITH MaxMemoryGroups AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, MAX(i.MEMORY_MB) AS max_memory_mb, i.LAUNCHED_AT FROM INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON i.UUID = iic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME, ism.KEY, i.LAUNCHED_AT HAVING MAX(i.MEMORY_MB) > 16000), MaxMemoryAverages AS (SELECT AVG(inner_cte.max_memory_mb * 1.0) AS avg_of_group_max_memory_mb FROM inner_cte) SELECT inner_cte.DISPLAY_NAME, inner_cte.metadata_key, inner_cte.max_memory_mb, inner_cte.LAUNCHED_AT FROM inner_cte WHERE inner_cte.max_memory_mb > (SELECT avg_of_group_max_memory_mb FROM MaxMemoryAverages)), VcpuMinMemoryStats AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME, COUNT(i.UUID) AS instance_count, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) AS min_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.VCPUS > 8 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME), OverallMin AS (SELECT MIN(min_memory_mb) AS overall_min_memory_mb FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.VM_STATE, ic.DISPLAY_NAME, ic.instance_count, ic.min_memory_mb, (ic.min_memory_mb - om.overall_min_memory_mb) AS min_memory_deviation_from_overall_min FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN OverallMin AS om), CombinedInstanceInsights AS (SELECT mmg.DISPLAY_NAME, mmg.metadata_key, mmg.max_memory_mb, mmg.LAUNCHED_AT, NULL AS VM_STATE, NULL AS instance_count, NULL AS min_memory_mb, NULL AS min_memory_deviation_from_overall_min, 'High Max Memory Group' AS Reason FROM MaxMemoryGroups AS mmg UNION ALL SELECT vms.DISPLAY_NAME, NULL AS metadata_key, NULL AS max_memory_mb, NULL AS LAUNCHED_AT, vms.VM_STATE, vms.instance_count, vms.min_memory_mb, vms.min_memory_deviation_from_overall_min, 'VCPU>8 Min Memory Stats' AS Reason FROM VcpuMinMemoryStats AS vms) SELECT * FROM CombinedInstanceInsights ORDER BY DISPLAY_NAME, Reason
WITH migration_memory_analysis AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.MEMORY_MB AS instance_memory_mb, SUM(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), avg_window AS (SELECT AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.migration_id, ic.instance_display_name, ic.instance_memory_mb, ic.window_column AS running_sum_instance_memory_mb, (ic.window_column - aw.avg_window_column) AS deviation_from_avg_running_sum_instance_memory_mb FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_window AS aw), instance_memory_benchmark AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.POWER_STATE AS power_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, MAX(i.ID) OVER (ORDER BY i.ID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_instance_id_next_2 FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), AverageMemory AS (SELECT AVG(memory_mb) AS avg_memory_mb FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.instance_uuid, ic.display_name, ic.vm_state, ic.power_state, ic.memory_mb, ic.vcpus, ic.instance_extra_id, ic.max_instance_id_next_2, am.avg_memory_mb AS benchmark_avg_memory_mb, CASE WHEN ic.memory_mb > am.avg_memory_mb * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_MEMORY' WHEN ic.memory_mb < am.avg_memory_mb * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_MEMORY' ELSE 'AVERAGE_MEMORY' END AS memory_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageMemory AS am) SELECT mma.migration_id, mma.instance_display_name, mma.instance_memory_mb, mma.running_sum_instance_memory_mb, mma.deviation_from_avg_running_sum_instance_memory_mb, imb.instance_uuid, imb.display_name, imb.vm_state, imb.power_state, imb.memory_mb, imb.vcpus, imb.instance_extra_id, imb.max_instance_id_next_2, imb.benchmark_avg_memory_mb, imb.memory_category FROM migration_memory_analysis AS mma JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON i.DISPLAY_NAME = mma.instance_display_name JOIN instance_memory_benchmark AS imb ON imb.instance_uuid = i.UUID
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME, COUNT(i.UUID) AS instance_count, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) AS min_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.VCPUS > 8 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME) SELECT inner_cte.VM_STATE, inner_cte.DISPLAY_NAME, inner_cte.instance_count, inner_cte.min_memory_mb, (CAST(inner_cte.min_memory_mb * 1.0 AS REAL) / max_vals.max_min_memory_mb) AS min_memory_vs_max_ratio FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT MAX(min_memory_mb) AS max_min_memory_mb FROM inner_cte) AS max_vals ON 1 = 1 WHERE (CAST(inner_cte.min_memory_mb * 1.0 AS REAL) / max_vals.max_min_memory_mb) >= 0.75), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, iic.NETWORK_INFO AS network_info, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY ism.VALUE ORDER BY i.CREATED_AT ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON i.UUID = iic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' ORDER BY i.CREATED_AT) SELECT instance_uuid, instance_display_name, metadata_key, metadata_value, network_info, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE window_column > (SELECT AVG(window_column) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY window_column DESC, instance_uuid) SELECT q1.VM_STATE, q1.DISPLAY_NAME, q1.instance_count, q1.min_memory_mb, q1.min_memory_vs_max_ratio, COUNT(q2.instance_uuid) AS matching_metadata_rows FROM q1 LEFT JOIN q2 ON q1.DISPLAY_NAME = q2.instance_display_name GROUP BY q1.VM_STATE, q1.DISPLAY_NAME, q1.instance_count, q1.min_memory_mb, q1.min_memory_vs_max_ratio ORDER BY q1.min_memory_vs_max_ratio DESC, q1.VM_STATE, q1.DISPLAY_NAME
WITH max_mem_by_state AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.VM_STATE, i.MEMORY_MB, c.NETWORK_INFO, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY i.VM_STATE ORDER BY i.MEMORY_MB DESC) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS c ON i.UUID = c.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT inner_cte.VM_STATE, MAX(inner_cte.MEMORY_MB) AS max_memory_mb FROM inner_cte GROUP BY inner_cte.VM_STATE), instance_mem_deviation AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY i.VM_STATE ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), avg_mem AS (SELECT AVG(memory_mb) AS avg_memory_mb FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.instance_uuid, ic.display_name, ic.vm_state, ic.memory_mb, ic.vcpus, ic.instance_extra_id, ic.window_column, (ic.memory_mb - am.avg_memory_mb) AS deviation_from_avg_memory_mb FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_mem AS am) SELECT imd.vm_state, COUNT(*) AS instance_count, SUM(imd.vcpus) AS total_vcpus, mmbs.max_memory_mb, ROUND(CAST(mmbs.max_memory_mb AS REAL) / NULLIF(AVG(imd.memory_mb), 0), 2) AS max_to_avg_memory_ratio, ROUND(AVG(imd.deviation_from_avg_memory_mb), 2) AS avg_deviation_from_avg_memory_mb FROM instance_mem_deviation AS imd JOIN max_mem_by_state AS mmbs ON imd.vm_state = mmbs.VM_STATE GROUP BY imd.vm_state, mmbs.max_memory_mb ORDER BY max_to_avg_memory_ratio DESC
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT MIN(i.ID) AS min_instance_id, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) AS avg_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE (i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded' OR i.VCPUS >= 8) AND i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME) SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, inner_cte.min_instance_id, inner_cte.avg_memory_mb, (CAST(inner_cte.avg_memory_mb * 1.0 AS REAL) / MAX(i.MEMORY_MB)) AS avg_memory_utilization_ratio FROM inner_cte JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON i.ID = inner_cte.min_instance_id JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME, inner_cte.min_instance_id, inner_cte.avg_memory_mb), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.uuid, i.display_name, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, m.status, MIN(i.memory_mb) OVER (ORDER BY m.id ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN migrations AS m ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12') SELECT uuid, display_name, source_compute, dest_compute, status, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE window_column > (SELECT AVG(window_column) FROM inner_cte)) SELECT q1.display_name, q1.min_instance_id, q1.avg_memory_mb, q1.avg_memory_utilization_ratio, q2.uuid AS migration_instance_uuid, q2.source_compute, q2.dest_compute, q2.status AS migration_status, q2.window_column AS windowed_min_memory_mb FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q2.display_name = q1.display_name
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.MEMORY_MB AS instance_memory_mb, SUM(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT migration_id, instance_display_name, instance_memory_mb, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE instance_memory_mb > (SELECT AVG(instance_memory_mb) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY migration_id), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, COUNT(m.id) AS migrations FROM instances AS i JOIN migrations AS m ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.display_name ORDER BY migrations DESC LIMIT 10) SELECT display_name, migrations FROM inner_cte WHERE migrations > (SELECT AVG(migrations) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY migrations DESC) SELECT q1.migration_id, q1.instance_display_name, q1.instance_memory_mb, q1.window_column AS running_instance_memory_mb_within_status, q2.migrations AS completed_migrations_for_display_name FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.instance_display_name = q2.display_name ORDER BY q2.migrations DESC, q1.migration_id
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.vm_state, EXP(AVG(LN(i.memory_mb))) AS geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.memory_mb > 0 AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.vm_state ORDER BY geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC) SELECT inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.geometric_mean_memory_mb, COUNT(DISTINCT i.uuid) AS instance_count FROM inner_cte JOIN instances AS i ON i.vm_state = inner_cte.vm_state JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.memory_mb > 0 AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.geometric_mean_memory_mb ORDER BY inner_cte.geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME, COUNT(i.UUID) AS instance_count, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) AS min_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.VCPUS > 8 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME), OverallMin AS (SELECT MIN(min_memory_mb) AS overall_min_memory_mb FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.VM_STATE, ic.DISPLAY_NAME, ic.instance_count, ic.min_memory_mb, (ic.min_memory_mb - om.overall_min_memory_mb) AS min_memory_deviation_from_overall_min FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN OverallMin AS om) SELECT q2.VM_STATE, q2.DISPLAY_NAME, q1.geometric_mean_memory_mb, q1.instance_count AS image_image_type_instance_count, q2.instance_count AS high_vcpu_instance_count, q2.min_memory_mb AS high_vcpu_min_memory_mb, q2.min_memory_deviation_from_overall_min FROM q2 JOIN q1 ON q2.VM_STATE = q1.vm_state ORDER BY q2.VM_STATE, q2.DISPLAY_NAME
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.HOSTNAME, i.VM_STATE, i.MEMORY_MB, i.VCPUS, i.UUID, m.STATUS, MAX(m.ID) OVER (ORDER BY i.UUID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_migration_id_next2 FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12') SELECT inner_cte.DISPLAY_NAME, inner_cte.HOSTNAME, inner_cte.VM_STATE, inner_cte.MEMORY_MB, inner_cte.VCPUS, inner_cte.UUID, inner_cte.STATUS, inner_cte.max_migration_id_next2, host_stats.completed_migrations_on_host, avg_stats.avg_completed_migrations_per_host FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT i.host AS host, COUNT(*) AS completed_migrations_on_host FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' GROUP BY i.host) AS host_stats ON host_stats.host = 'blaze8-12', (SELECT AVG(completed_migration_count) AS avg_completed_migrations_per_host FROM (SELECT i.host AS host, COUNT(*) AS completed_migration_count FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' GROUP BY i.host) AS sub) AS avg_stats), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT t1.UUID AS instance_uuid, t2.VALUE AS metadata_value, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY t1.MEMORY_MB ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS t0 JOIN INSTANCES AS t1 ON t0.INSTANCE_UUID = t1.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS t2 ON t1.UUID = t2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE t1.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND t1.HOST = 'blaze8-12' AND t2.KEY = 'image_image_type'), avg_rank AS (SELECT AVG(inner_cte.window_column * 1.0) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte) SELECT inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.metadata_value, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, inner_cte.window_column FROM inner_cte JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON i.UUID = inner_cte.instance_uuid WHERE inner_cte.window_column > (SELECT avg_window_column FROM avg_rank)) SELECT q1.DISPLAY_NAME, q1.HOSTNAME, q1.VM_STATE, q1.MEMORY_MB, q1.VCPUS, q1.UUID, q1.STATUS, q1.max_migration_id_next2, q1.completed_migrations_on_host, q1.avg_completed_migrations_per_host, q2.metadata_value AS image_image_type, q2.window_column AS dense_rank_memory_window FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q2.instance_uuid = q1.UUID
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT t0.DISPLAY_NAME, t1.STATUS, t1.DEST_COMPUTE, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY t1.DEST_COMPUTE ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS t0 JOIN MIGRATIONS AS t1 ON t0.UUID = t1.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE t1.STATUS = 'completed' AND t0.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.STATUS, i.DEST_COMPUTE, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN (SELECT DEST_COMPUTE, COUNT(*) AS dest_compute_cnt FROM inner_cte GROUP BY DEST_COMPUTE) AS dc ON i.DEST_COMPUTE = dc.DEST_COMPUTE WHERE dc.dest_compute_cnt > 1), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, i.availability_zone, m.status, m.migration_type, i.uuid, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY i.uuid ORDER BY i.uuid) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN migrations AS m ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12'), ZoneVcpuAverages AS (SELECT i.availability_zone, AVG(i.vcpus * 1.0) AS avg_vcpus FROM instances AS i WHERE i.host = 'blaze8-12' AND i.deleted = 0 GROUP BY i.availability_zone) SELECT inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.availability_zone, inner_cte.status, inner_cte.migration_type, inner_cte.uuid, inner_cte.window_column FROM inner_cte JOIN instances AS i2 ON i2.uuid = inner_cte.uuid WHERE i2.vcpus > (SELECT avg_vcpus FROM ZoneVcpuAverages WHERE ZoneVcpuAverages.availability_zone = i2.availability_zone)) SELECT q2.display_name, q2.availability_zone, q2.status AS migration_status, q2.migration_type, q2.uuid AS instance_uuid, q2.window_column AS dense_rank_within_instance, q1.dest_compute, q1.window_column AS row_number_by_dest_compute FROM q2 JOIN q1 ON q1.display_name = q2.display_name AND q1.status = q2.status ORDER BY q2.availability_zone, q1.dest_compute, q2.display_name
WITH subq1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.FLAVOR AS flavor, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.vcpus, inner_cte.flavor, inner_cte.metadata_key, inner_cte.metadata_value, inner_cte.window_column, key_counts.total_rows_for_key, avg_counts.avg_rows_per_key FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT metadata_key, COUNT(*) AS total_rows_for_key FROM inner_cte GROUP BY metadata_key) AS key_counts ON key_counts.metadata_key = inner_cte.metadata_key JOIN (SELECT AVG(rows_per_key) AS avg_rows_per_key FROM (SELECT metadata_key, COUNT(*) AS rows_per_key FROM inner_cte GROUP BY metadata_key) AS per_key) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1), subq2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY i.VM_STATE ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT CASE WHEN LAG(vm_state) OVER (ORDER BY vm_state, instance_uuid) = vm_state THEN NULL ELSE vm_state END AS vm_state, instance_uuid, display_name, memory_mb, vcpus, instance_extra_id, window_column FROM inner_cte ORDER BY vm_state, instance_uuid) SELECT subq2.vm_state, subq2.instance_uuid, subq2.display_name, subq2.memory_mb, subq2.vcpus, subq2.instance_extra_id, subq2.window_column AS vm_state_row_number, subq1.flavor, subq1.metadata_key, subq1.metadata_value, subq1.window_column AS metadata_key_row_number, subq1.total_rows_for_key, subq1.avg_rows_per_key FROM subq2 JOIN subq1 ON subq1.instance_uuid = subq2.instance_uuid ORDER BY subq2.vm_state, subq2.instance_uuid, subq1.metadata_key, subq1.window_column
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, MIN(i.ID) AS min_instance_id, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) AS avg_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME) SELECT ic.DISPLAY_NAME, ic.min_instance_id, ic.avg_memory_mb, CASE WHEN EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM INSTANCES AS i2 JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism2 ON i2.UUID = ism2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i2.DISPLAY_NAME = ic.DISPLAY_NAME AND i2.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i2.HOST = 'blaze8-12' AND ism2.KEY = 'image_image_location') THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_image_location_metadata FROM inner_cte AS ic ORDER BY ic.avg_memory_mb DESC LIMIT 10), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME, COUNT(i.UUID) AS instance_count, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) AS min_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.VCPUS > 8 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME) SELECT inner_cte.VM_STATE, inner_cte.DISPLAY_NAME, inner_cte.instance_count, inner_cte.min_memory_mb, avg_counts.avg_instance_count FROM inner_cte, (SELECT AVG(instance_count) AS avg_instance_count FROM inner_cte) AS avg_counts) SELECT q1.DISPLAY_NAME, q1.min_instance_id, q1.avg_memory_mb, q1.has_image_location_metadata, q2.VM_STATE, q2.instance_count, q2.min_memory_mb, q2.avg_instance_count FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.DISPLAY_NAME = q2.DISPLAY_NAME ORDER BY q1.avg_memory_mb DESC, q2.instance_count DESC

instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12') SELECT inner_cte.uuid, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.key, inner_cte.value, meta_counts.total_metadata_rows DESC, inner_cte.uuid, inner_cte.key), instance_memory_benchmark AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.POWER_STATE AS power_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, MAX(i.ID) OVER (ORDER BY i.ID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_instance_id_next_2 FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), AverageMemory AS (SELECT AVG(memory_mb) AS avg_memory_mb FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.instance_uuid, ic.display_name, ic.vm_state, ic.power_state, ic.memory_mb, ic.vcpus, ic.instance_extra_id, ic.max_instance_id_next_2, am.avg_memory_mb AS benchmark_avg_memory_mb, CASE WHEN ic.memory_mb > am.avg_memory_mb * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_MEMORY' WHEN ic.memory_mb < am.avg_memory_mb * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_MEMORY' ELSE 'AVERAGE_MEMORY' END AS memory_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageMemory AS am) SELECT imb.instance_uuid, imb.display_name, imb.vm_state, imb.power_state, imb.memory_mb, imb.vcpus, imb.instance_extra_id, imb.max_instance_id_next_2, imb.benchmark_avg_memory_mb, imb.memory_category, mt.total_metadata_rows, mt.key AS system_metadata_key, mt.value AS system_metadata_value, mt.window_column AS metadata_dense_rank_within_instance FROM instance_memory_benchmark AS imb JOIN meta_top10 AS mt ON mt.uuid = imb.instance_uuid ORDER BY mt.total_metadata_rows DESC, imb.instance_uuid, mt.key
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, COUNT(m.id) AS migrations FROM instances AS i JOIN migrations AS m ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.display_name ORDER BY migrations DESC LIMIT 10) SELECT ic.display_name, ic.migrations AS completed_migrations, SUM(i.vcpus) AS total_vcpus, SUM(i.memory_mb) AS total_memory_mb, CASE WHEN EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM migrations AS m2 JOIN instances AS i2 ON m2.instance_uuid = i2.uuid WHERE i2.display_name = ic.display_name AND i2.host = 'blaze8-12' AND NOT m2.status IN ('completed', 'failed', 'error')) THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_active_migrations FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN instances AS i ON i.display_name = ic.display_name AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY ic.display_name, ic.migrations ORDER BY ic.migrations DESC), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY i.VM_STATE ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), avg_vcpu AS (SELECT AVG(vcpus) AS avg_vcpus FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.instance_uuid, ic.display_name, ic.vm_state, ic.memory_mb, ic.vcpus, ic.instance_extra_id, ic.window_column, av.avg_vcpus AS benchmark_avg_vcpus, CASE WHEN ic.vcpus > av.avg_vcpus * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_VCPU' WHEN ic.vcpus < av.avg_vcpus * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_VCPU' ELSE 'AVERAGE_VCPU' END AS vcpu_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_vcpu AS av) SELECT q1.display_name, q1.completed_migrations, q1.total_vcpus AS total_vcpus_for_display_name, q1.total_memory_mb AS total_memory_mb_for_display_name, q1.has_active_migrations, q2.instance_uuid, q2.vm_state, q2.memory_mb, q2.vcpus, q2.instance_extra_id, q2.window_column, q2.benchmark_avg_vcpus, q2.vcpu_category FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q2.display_name = q1.display_name ORDER BY q1.completed_migrations DESC, q1.display_name, q2.vm_state, q2.window_column, q2.instance_uuid
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.INSTANCE_UUID, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, AVG(m.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY m.INSTANCE_UUID ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' ORDER BY m.ID), instance_rollup AS (SELECT INSTANCE_UUID, COUNT(*) AS total_metadata_rows, AVG(window_column) AS avg_running_avg_migration_id FROM inner_cte GROUP BY INSTANCE_UUID) SELECT ic.migration_id, ic.INSTANCE_UUID, ic.SOURCE_COMPUTE, ic.DEST_COMPUTE, ic.instance_display_name, ic.metadata_key, ic.metadata_value, ic.window_column, ir.total_metadata_rows, ir.avg_running_avg_migration_id FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN instance_rollup AS ir ON ic.INSTANCE_UUID = ir.INSTANCE_UUID ORDER BY ic.migration_id), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.HOSTNAME AS hostname, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY i.UUID ORDER BY ism.KEY) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.hostname, inner_cte.instance_extra_id, inner_cte.metadata_key, inner_cte.metadata_value, inner_cte.window_column, CASE WHEN EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism2 JOIN INSTANCES AS i2 ON ism2.INSTANCE_UUID = i2.UUID WHERE ism2.INSTANCE_UUID = inner_cte.instance_uuid AND ism2.DELETED <> 0) THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_deleted_system_metadata FROM inner_cte ORDER BY inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.metadata_key) SELECT q2.hostname, q2.instance_uuid, q2.instance_extra_id, q2.metadata_key, q2.metadata_value, q2.window_column AS running_avg_instance_memory_mb, q2.has_deleted_system_metadata, q1.migration_id, q1.SOURCE_COMPUTE, q1.DEST_COMPUTE, q1.instance_display_name, q1.window_column AS running_avg_migration_id, q1.total_metadata_rows, q1.avg_running_avg_migration_id, ROUND(CAST(q1.avg_running_avg_migration_id AS REAL) / NULLIF(q2.window_column, 0), 6) AS normalized_avg_migration_id_per_running_avg_memory FROM q2 JOIN q1 ON q1.INSTANCE_UUID = q2.instance_uuid AND q1.metadata_key = q2.metadata_key AND q1.metadata_value = q2.metadata_value ORDER BY normalized_avg_migration_id_per_running_avg_memory DESC, q2.instance_uuid, q2.metadata_key, q1.migration_id
WITH CacheStats AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name, ism.value AS image_type_value, COUNT(iic.id) AS cache_record_count, SUM(i.memory_mb) AS total_memory_mb FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON ism.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS iic ON iic.instance_uuid = i.uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, ism.value), AverageCache AS (SELECT AVG(cache_record_count) AS avg_cache_record_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.instance_uuid, ic.display_name, ic.image_type_value, ic.cache_record_count, ic.total_memory_mb, ac.avg_cache_record_count AS benchmark_avg_cache_record_count, CASE WHEN ic.cache_record_count > ac.avg_cache_record_count * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_CACHE' WHEN ic.cache_record_count < ac.avg_cache_record_count * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_CACHE' ELSE 'AVERAGE_CACHE' END AS cache_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageCache AS ac), MaxVcpusByDisplayName AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name, i.key_name, COUNT(ism.id) AS metadata_entry_count, SUM(i.vcpus) AS total_vcpus FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, i.key_name) SELECT display_name, MAX(total_vcpus) AS max_total_vcpus_for_display_name FROM inner_cte GROUP BY display_name) SELECT cs.instance_uuid, cs.display_name, cs.image_type_value, cs.cache_record_count, cs.total_memory_mb, cs.benchmark_avg_cache_record_count, cs.cache_category, mv.max_total_vcpus_for_display_name FROM CacheStats AS cs LEFT JOIN MaxVcpusByDisplayName AS mv ON cs.display_name = mv.display_name ORDER BY cs.cache_record_count DESC, cs.display_name
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.FLAVOR AS flavor, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.vcpus, inner_cte.flavor, inner_cte.metadata_key, inner_cte.metadata_value, inner_cte.window_column, key_counts.total_rows_for_key, avg_counts.avg_rows_per_key FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT metadata_key, COUNT(*) AS total_rows_for_key FROM inner_cte GROUP BY metadata_key) AS key_counts ON key_counts.metadata_key = inner_cte.metadata_key JOIN (SELECT AVG(rows_per_key) AS avg_rows_per_key FROM (SELECT metadata_key, COUNT(*) AS rows_per_key FROM inner_cte GROUP BY metadata_key) AS per_key) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT ie.INSTANCE_UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, ie.FLAVOR, ism.KEY, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY ism.KEY) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE ie.DELETED = 0 AND ism.DELETED = 0 AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT inner_cte.INSTANCE_UUID, inner_cte.DISPLAY_NAME, inner_cte.FLAVOR, inner_cte.KEY, inner_cte.window_column, i.MEMORY_MB FROM inner_cte JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON inner_cte.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.MEMORY_MB > 16384) SELECT q1.instance_uuid, q1.display_name, q1.memory_mb, q1.vcpus, q1.flavor, q1.metadata_key, q1.metadata_value, q1.window_column AS row_number_within_key_by_uuid, q1.total_rows_for_key, q1.avg_rows_per_key, q2.window_column AS dense_rank_within_key, q2.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb_gt_16gb FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q2.INSTANCE_UUID = q1.instance_uuid AND q2.KEY = q1.metadata_key ORDER BY q1.metadata_key, q1.instance_uuid
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT t0.DISPLAY_NAME, t1.STATUS, t1.DEST_COMPUTE, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY t1.DEST_COMPUTE ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS t0 JOIN MIGRATIONS AS t1 ON t0.UUID = t1.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE t1.STATUS = 'completed' AND t0.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.STATUS, i.DEST_COMPUTE, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN (SELECT DEST_COMPUTE, COUNT(*) AS dest_compute_cnt FROM inner_cte GROUP BY DEST_COMPUTE) AS dc ON i.DEST_COMPUTE = dc.DEST_COMPUTE WHERE dc.dest_compute_cnt > 1), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, i.availability_zone, m.status, m.migration_type, i.uuid, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY i.uuid ORDER BY i.uuid) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN migrations AS m ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12'), ZoneVcpuAverages AS (SELECT i.availability_zone, AVG(i.vcpus * 1.0) AS avg_vcpus FROM instances AS i WHERE i.host = 'blaze8-12' AND i.deleted = 0 GROUP BY i.availability_zone) SELECT inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.availability_zone, inner_cte.status, inner_cte.migration_type, inner_cte.uuid, inner_cte.window_column FROM inner_cte JOIN instances AS i2 ON i2.uuid = inner_cte.uuid WHERE i2.vcpus > (SELECT avg_vcpus FROM ZoneVcpuAverages WHERE ZoneVcpuAverages.availability_zone = i2.availability_zone)) SELECT q2.availability_zone, COUNT(DISTINCT q2.uuid) AS Instance_Count FROM q2 JOIN q1 ON q1.display_name = q2.display_name AND q1.status = q2.status WHERE q2.status = 'completed' GROUP BY q2.availability_zone HAVING COUNT(*) >= 2 ORDER BY Instance_Count DESC
WITH GeomMeanByDisplayName AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, EXP(AVG(LN(i.memory_mb))) AS geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON ie.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS iic ON i.uuid = iic.instance_uuid WHERE iic.network_info <> '[]' AND i.memory_mb > 0 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.display_name ORDER BY geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC) SELECT inner_cte.display_name, MAX(inner_cte.geometric_mean_memory_mb) AS max_geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM inner_cte GROUP BY inner_cte.display_name ORDER BY max_geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC), MinMemoryGroups AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME, COUNT(i.UUID) AS instance_count, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) AS min_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.VCPUS > 8 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME) SELECT inner_cte.VM_STATE, inner_cte.DISPLAY_NAME, inner_cte.instance_count, inner_cte.min_memory_mb, (CAST(inner_cte.min_memory_mb * 1.0 AS REAL) / max_vals.max_min_memory_mb) AS min_memory_vs_max_ratio FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT MAX(min_memory_mb) AS max_min_memory_mb FROM inner_cte) AS max_vals ON 1 = 1 WHERE (CAST(inner_cte.min_memory_mb * 1.0 AS REAL) / max_vals.max_min_memory_mb) >= 0.75) SELECT mmg.VM_STATE, mmg.DISPLAY_NAME, mmg.instance_count, mmg.min_memory_mb, mmg.min_memory_vs_max_ratio, gmbdn.max_geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM MinMemoryGroups AS mmg JOIN GeomMeanByDisplayName AS gmbdn ON mmg.DISPLAY_NAME = gmbdn.display_name ORDER BY mmg.min_memory_vs_max_ratio DESC, gmbdn.max_geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC, mmg.instance_count DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, SUM(i.ROOT_GB) AS total_root_gb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME HAVING SUM(i.ROOT_GB) > 50), avg_totals AS (SELECT AVG(total_root_gb) AS avg_total_root_gb FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.DISPLAY_NAME, ic.total_root_gb, at.avg_total_root_gb, (ic.total_root_gb - at.avg_total_root_gb) AS deviation_from_avg_total_root_gb FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_totals AS at
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3') SELECT metadata_key, MAX(vcpus) AS max_vcpus_for_key FROM inner_cte GROUP BY metadata_key
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT sg.NAME AS security_group_name, SUM(sgr.TO_PORT - sgr.FROM_PORT + 1) AS total_ports_covered FROM SECURITY_GROUPS AS sg JOIN SECURITY_GROUP_RULES AS sgr ON sg.ID = sgr.PARENT_GROUP_ID WHERE sgr.PROTOCOL = 'tcp' GROUP BY sg.NAME HAVING SUM(sgr.TO_PORT - sgr.FROM_PORT + 1) > 100) SELECT security_group_name, total_ports_covered FROM inner_cte WHERE total_ports_covered > (SELECT AVG(total_ports_covered) FROM inner_cte)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME, COUNT(i.ID) AS instance_count, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) AS min_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(min_memory_mb) AS avg_min_memory_mb FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.VM_STATE, ic.DISPLAY_NAME, ic.instance_count, ic.min_memory_mb, (ic.min_memory_mb - ac.avg_min_memory_mb) AS deviation_from_avg_min_memory_mb FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS ac
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) AS min_instance_memory_mb, SUM(m.MEMORY_REMAINING) AS total_migration_memory_remaining FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded' AND m.STATUS = 'completed') SELECT min_instance_memory_mb, total_migration_memory_remaining FROM inner_cte WHERE total_migration_memory_remaining > (SELECT AVG(m2.MEMORY_REMAINING) FROM INSTANCES AS i2 JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m2 ON i2.UUID = m2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i2.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded' AND m2.STATUS = 'completed')
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.STATUS AS migration_status, ins.VM_STATE AS instance_vm_state, COUNT(m.ID) AS migration_count, MIN(m.CREATED_AT) AS earliest_migration_created_at FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS ins ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = ins.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ins.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE ins.VCPUS >= 8 GROUP BY m.STATUS, ins.VM_STATE) SELECT inner_cte.migration_status, inner_cte.instance_vm_state, inner_cte.migration_count, inner_cte.earliest_migration_created_at FROM inner_cte WHERE inner_cte.earliest_migration_created_at > '2015-01-01T00:00:00'
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.STATUS AS migration_status, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, m.INSTANCE_UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.MEMORY_MB AS instance_memory_mb, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (ORDER BY m.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed') SELECT inner_cte.migration_id, inner_cte.migration_status, inner_cte.SOURCE_COMPUTE, inner_cte.DEST_COMPUTE, inner_cte.INSTANCE_UUID, inner_cte.instance_display_name, inner_cte.instance_memory_mb, inner_cte.instance_extra_id, inner_cte.window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE inner_cte.instance_memory_mb > (SELECT AVG(inner_cte.instance_memory_mb * 1.0) FROM inner_cte)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.vm_state, EXP(AVG(LN(i.memory_mb))) AS geometric_mean FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.memory_mb > 0 GROUP BY i.vm_state ORDER BY geometric_mean DESC), overall_cte AS (SELECT EXP(AVG(LN(geometric_mean))) AS overall_geometric_mean FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.vm_state, ic.geometric_mean, (ic.geometric_mean - oc.overall_geometric_mean) AS deviation_from_overall_geometric_mean FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN overall_cte AS oc ORDER BY ic.geometric_mean DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.status, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, i.display_name, ie.flavor, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.status ORDER BY m.status) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed'), status_summary AS (SELECT status, COUNT(*) AS total_completed_migrations, COUNT(DISTINCT display_name) AS distinct_instances_involved FROM inner_cte GROUP BY status) SELECT i.status, i.source_compute, i.dest_compute, i.display_name, i.flavor, i.window_column, s.total_completed_migrations, s.distinct_instances_involved FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN status_summary AS s ON i.status = s.status
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, EXP(AVG(LN(i.memory_mb))) AS geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON ism.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS iic ON i.uuid = iic.instance_uuid WHERE i.memory_mb > 0 AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.display_name ORDER BY geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC) SELECT inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.geometric_mean_memory_mb, COUNT(DISTINCT i.uuid) AS contributing_instance_count FROM inner_cte JOIN instances AS i ON i.display_name = inner_cte.display_name JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON ism.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS iic ON i.uuid = iic.instance_uuid WHERE i.memory_mb > 0 AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.geometric_mean_memory_mb ORDER BY inner_cte.geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.uuid, i.display_name, i.vm_state, i.memory_mb, c.network_info, AVG(i.memory_mb) OVER (PARTITION BY i.vm_state ORDER BY i.memory_mb ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON i.uuid = c.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3' ORDER BY i.memory_mb) SELECT inner_cte.uuid, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.network_info, inner_cte.window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE inner_cte.memory_mb > (SELECT AVG(memory_mb) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY inner_cte.memory_mb
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.STATUS AS migration_status, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.MEMORY_MB AS instance_memory_mb, c.NETWORK_INFO, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (ORDER BY m.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS c ON i.UUID = c.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed') SELECT inner_cte.project_id AS project_id, SUM(inner_cte.instance_memory_mb) AS total_migrated_memory_mb, CASE WHEN EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM migrations AS m2 JOIN instances AS i2 ON m2.instance_uuid = i2.uuid WHERE i2.project_id = inner_cte.project_id AND m2.deleted = 0 AND NOT m2.status IN ('completed', 'failed', 'error')) THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_active_migrations FROM (SELECT i.project_id AS project_id, inner_cte.instance_memory_mb AS instance_memory_mb FROM inner_cte JOIN instances AS i ON i.display_name = inner_cte.instance_display_name) AS inner_cte GROUP BY inner_cte.project_id ORDER BY total_migrated_memory_mb DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.VM_STATE, SUM(i.MEMORY_MB) AS total_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic ON i.UUID = ic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' GROUP BY i.VM_STATE HAVING SUM(i.MEMORY_MB) > 50000) SELECT inner_cte.VM_STATE, inner_cte.total_memory_mb, COUNT(DISTINCT i.UUID) AS instance_count FROM inner_cte JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON i.VM_STATE = inner_cte.VM_STATE AND i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic ON i.UUID = ic.INSTANCE_UUID GROUP BY inner_cte.VM_STATE, inner_cte.total_memory_mb ORDER BY inner_cte.total_memory_mb DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT MIN(m.ID) AS min_migration_id, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) AS avg_instance_memory_mb FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS c ON i.UUID = c.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND c.DELETED = 0 GROUP BY i.VM_STATE) SELECT inner_cte.min_migration_id, inner_cte.avg_instance_memory_mb, overall_avg.overall_avg_instance_memory_mb FROM inner_cte, (SELECT AVG(avg_instance_memory_mb) AS overall_avg_instance_memory_mb FROM inner_cte) AS overall_avg
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT MIN(i.ID) AS min_instance_id, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) AS avg_memory_mb FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME) SELECT MAX(avg_memory_mb) AS max_avg_memory_mb_across_display_names FROM inner_cte
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.ID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, c.NETWORK_INFO, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE ORDER BY i.ID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS c ON i.UUID = c.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded') SELECT inner_cte.AVAILABILITY_ZONE AS availability_zone, COUNT(inner_cte.ID) AS total_shelved_offloaded_instances, avg_az.avg_instance_count AS avg_shelved_offloaded_instances_per_az FROM inner_cte, (SELECT AVG(instance_count) AS avg_instance_count FROM (SELECT COUNT(ID) AS instance_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY AVAILABILITY_ZONE) AS sub) AS avg_az GROUP BY inner_cte.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, avg_az.avg_instance_count
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.ID AS instance_id, i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.USER_ID, i.PROJECT_ID, i.HOSTNAME, i.VM_STATE, i.POWER_STATE, i.MEMORY_MB, i.VCPUS, i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, c.ID AS info_cache_id, c.NETWORK_INFO, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY i.PROJECT_ID ORDER BY i.PROJECT_ID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS c ON i.UUID = c.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge') SELECT inner_cte.instance_id, inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.user_id, inner_cte.project_id, inner_cte.hostname, inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.power_state, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.vcpus, inner_cte.availability_zone, inner_cte.info_cache_id, inner_cte.network_info, inner_cte.window_column AS min_memory_mb_in_project, COUNT(inner_cte.instance_id) OVER (PARTITION BY inner_cte.project_id) AS total_forge_instances_in_project, avg_counts.avg_forge_instances_per_project FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(forge_instance_count) AS avg_forge_instances_per_project FROM (SELECT project_id, COUNT(*) AS forge_instance_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY project_id) AS per_project_counts) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.STATUS AS migration_status, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, m.DEST_HOST, i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.VM_STATE AS instance_vm_state, c.NETWORK_INFO AS cached_network_info, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS c ON i.UUID = c.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'confirmed' AND m.SOURCE_COMPUTE = 'ether-60') SELECT inner_cte.migration_id, inner_cte.migration_status, inner_cte.SOURCE_COMPUTE, inner_cte.DEST_COMPUTE, inner_cte.DEST_HOST, inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.instance_display_name, inner_cte.instance_vm_state, inner_cte.cached_network_info, inner_cte.window_column, COUNT(inner_cte.migration_id) OVER () AS total_confirmed_ether60_migrations, avg_by_dest.avg_migrations_per_dest_compute FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(migrations_per_dest) AS avg_migrations_per_dest_compute FROM (SELECT DEST_COMPUTE, COUNT(*) AS migrations_per_dest FROM inner_cte GROUP BY DEST_COMPUTE) AS per_dest) AS avg_by_dest ON 1 = 1 ORDER BY inner_cte.migration_id
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS shelved_offloaded_instances_with_cache FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic ON i.UUID = ic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded' AND i.DELETED = 0 AND ic.DELETED = 0) SELECT inner_cte.shelved_offloaded_instances_with_cache, SUM(i.vcpus) AS total_vcpus, SUM(i.memory_mb) AS total_memory_mb FROM inner_cte JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON 1 = 1 JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic ON i.UUID = ic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded' AND i.DELETED = 0 AND ic.DELETED = 0 GROUP BY inner_cte.shelved_offloaded_instances_with_cache
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT MIN(i.ID) AS min_instance_id, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) AS avg_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME), AverageMemory AS (SELECT AVG(avg_memory_mb) AS benchmark_avg_memory_mb FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.min_instance_id, ic.avg_memory_mb, am.benchmark_avg_memory_mb AS benchmark, CASE WHEN ic.avg_memory_mb > am.benchmark_avg_memory_mb * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_MEMORY' WHEN ic.avg_memory_mb < am.benchmark_avg_memory_mb * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_MEMORY' ELSE 'AVERAGE_MEMORY' END AS memory_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageMemory AS am
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID, ism.KEY, ism.VALUE, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ism.KEY ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge'), ForgeMetaCounts AS (SELECT i.UUID, COUNT(*) AS meta_row_count FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge' GROUP BY i.UUID) SELECT inner_cte.UUID, inner_cte.KEY, inner_cte.VALUE, inner_cte.window_column FROM inner_cte JOIN ForgeMetaCounts AS fmc ON inner_cte.UUID = fmc.UUID WHERE fmc.meta_row_count > (SELECT AVG(meta_row_count) FROM ForgeMetaCounts)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS completed_migrations_for_shelved_instances FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded') SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.HOSTNAME AS instance_hostname, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE AS source_compute, m.DEST_COMPUTE AS dest_compute, (CAST(m.MEMORY_PROCESSED * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(m.MEMORY_TOTAL, 0)) AS memory_processed_ratio, inner_cte.completed_migrations_for_shelved_instances FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID CROSS JOIN inner_cte WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded' AND (CAST(m.MEMORY_PROCESSED * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(m.MEMORY_TOTAL, 0)) > 0.75
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, m.STATUS AS migration_status, COUNT(i.ID) AS instance_count, MIN(m.MEMORY_TOTAL) AS min_memory_total FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.HIDDEN = 0 GROUP BY i.VM_STATE, m.STATUS), AverageInstanceCount AS (SELECT AVG(instance_count) AS avg_instance_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.vm_state, ic.migration_status, ic.instance_count, ic.min_memory_total, aic.avg_instance_count AS benchmark, CASE WHEN ic.instance_count > aic.avg_instance_count * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_VOLUME' WHEN ic.instance_count < aic.avg_instance_count * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_VOLUME' ELSE 'AVERAGE_VOLUME' END AS volume_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageInstanceCount AS aic
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT q.ID AS quota_usage_id, q.PROJECT_ID, q.RESOURCE, q.IN_USE, q.RESERVED, r.UUID AS reservation_uuid, MAX(r.DELTA) OVER (ORDER BY r.ID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_delta_next_2 FROM QUOTA_USAGES AS q JOIN RESERVATIONS AS r ON q.ID = r.USAGE_ID WHERE q.DELETED = 0), ResourceInUseAverages AS (SELECT q2.RESOURCE, AVG(q2.IN_USE * 1.0) AS avg_in_use FROM QUOTA_USAGES AS q2 WHERE q2.DELETED = 0 GROUP BY q2.RESOURCE) SELECT i.quota_usage_id, i.PROJECT_ID, i.RESOURCE, i.IN_USE, i.RESERVED, i.reservation_uuid, i.max_delta_next_2 FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ResourceInUseAverages AS a ON a.RESOURCE = i.RESOURCE WHERE i.IN_USE > (SELECT a2.avg_in_use FROM ResourceInUseAverages AS a2 WHERE a2.RESOURCE = i.RESOURCE)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.POWER_STATE AS power_state, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, iic.NETWORK_INFO AS network_info, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded') SELECT CASE WHEN LAG(metadata_key) OVER (ORDER BY metadata_key, instance_uuid) = metadata_key THEN NULL ELSE metadata_key END AS metadata_key, instance_uuid, display_name, power_state, metadata_value, network_info, window_column, COUNT(*) OVER (PARTITION BY metadata_key) AS total_rows_per_key, AVG(power_state) OVER (PARTITION BY metadata_key) AS avg_power_state_per_key FROM inner_cte ORDER BY metadata_key, instance_uuid
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, COUNT(ism.ID) AS metadata_entries FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME ORDER BY metadata_entries DESC LIMIT 10) SELECT inner_cte.DISPLAY_NAME, inner_cte.metadata_entries, MAX(i2.VCPUS) AS max_vcpus_in_display_name FROM inner_cte JOIN INSTANCES AS i2 ON i2.DISPLAY_NAME = inner_cte.DISPLAY_NAME AND i2.DELETED = 0 GROUP BY inner_cte.DISPLAY_NAME, inner_cte.metadata_entries ORDER BY inner_cte.metadata_entries DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, i.availability_zone, m.status, m.migration_type, i.uuid, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY i.uuid ORDER BY i.uuid) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN migrations AS m ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed') SELECT inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.availability_zone, inner_cte.status, inner_cte.migration_type, inner_cte.uuid, inner_cte.window_column, (CAST(m.memory_processed * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(m.memory_total, 0)) AS memory_processed_ratio FROM inner_cte JOIN migrations AS m ON m.instance_uuid = inner_cte.uuid AND m.status = inner_cte.status AND m.migration_type = inner_cte.migration_type WHERE (CAST(m.memory_processed * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(m.memory_total, 0)) > 0.75
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.FLAVOR AS flavor, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3') SELECT inner_cte.metadata_key AS metadata_key, COUNT(*) AS metadata_row_count, COUNT(DISTINCT inner_cte.instance_uuid) AS distinct_instance_count, SUM(inner_cte.vcpus) AS total_vcpus, SUM(inner_cte.memory_mb) AS total_memory_mb, CASE WHEN EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie2 WHERE ie2.INSTANCE_UUID IN (SELECT DISTINCT inner_cte2.instance_uuid FROM inner_cte AS inner_cte2 WHERE inner_cte2.metadata_key = inner_cte.metadata_key) AND NOT ie2.MIGRATION_CONTEXT IS NULL) THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_any_migration_context FROM inner_cte GROUP BY inner_cte.metadata_key ORDER BY metadata_row_count DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.uuid, ie.flavor, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY i.memory_mb ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE i.display_name = 'forge') SELECT uuid, flavor, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE window_column > (SELECT AVG(window_column) FROM inner_cte)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) AS min_memory_mb, SUM(i.VCPUS) AS total_vcpus FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3') SELECT inner_cte.min_memory_mb, inner_cte.total_vcpus, (CAST(SUM(CASE WHEN i.MEMORY_MB = inner_cte.min_memory_mb THEN i.VCPUS ELSE 0 END) * 1.0 AS REAL) / inner_cte.total_vcpus) AS pct_vcpus_at_min_memory FROM inner_cte JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID GROUP BY inner_cte.min_memory_mb, inner_cte.total_vcpus
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.status, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, i.display_name, ic.network_info, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.status ORDER BY m.status) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic ON i.uuid = ic.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'confirmed'), avg_rank AS (SELECT AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte) SELECT c.status, c.source_compute, c.dest_compute, c.display_name, c.network_info, c.window_column, a.avg_window_column, (c.window_column - a.avg_window_column) AS deviation_from_avg_dense_rank FROM inner_cte AS c CROSS JOIN avg_rank AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, COUNT(ism.ID) AS metadata_entries FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME ORDER BY metadata_entries DESC LIMIT 10), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(metadata_entries) AS avg_metadata_entries FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.DISPLAY_NAME, ic.metadata_entries, ac.avg_metadata_entries, (ic.metadata_entries - ac.avg_metadata_entries) AS deviation_from_avg FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS ac ORDER BY ic.metadata_entries DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) AS min_instance_memory_mb, SUM(m.MEMORY_PROCESSED) AS total_migration_memory_processed FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic ON i.UUID = ic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded') SELECT min_instance_memory_mb, total_migration_memory_processed FROM inner_cte WHERE total_migration_memory_processed > (SELECT AVG(m2.MEMORY_PROCESSED) FROM MIGRATIONS AS m2 JOIN INSTANCES AS i2 ON m2.INSTANCE_UUID = i2.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic2 ON i2.UUID = ic2.INSTANCE_UUID)
