WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.POWER_STATE AS power_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, MAX(ie.ID) OVER (ORDER BY i.UUID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_instance_extra_id_next_2 FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND ism.DELETED = 0) SELECT inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.power_state, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.vcpus, inner_cte.metadata_key, inner_cte.metadata_value, inner_cte.max_instance_extra_id_next_2 FROM inner_cte WHERE (CAST(inner_cte.memory_mb * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(inner_cte.vcpus, 0)) > 2048
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT t1.UUID AS instance_uuid, t2.VALUE AS metadata_value, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY t1.MEMORY_MB ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS t0 JOIN INSTANCES AS t1 ON t0.INSTANCE_UUID = t1.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS t2 ON t1.UUID = t2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE t1.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND t2.KEY = 'image_image_type'), AverageRank AS (SELECT AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_rank FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.instance_uuid, ic.metadata_value, ic.window_column, ar.avg_window_rank AS benchmark, CASE WHEN ic.window_column > ar.avg_window_rank * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_RANK' WHEN ic.window_column < ar.avg_window_rank * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_RANK' ELSE 'AVERAGE_RANK' END AS rank_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageRank AS ar
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT sg.NAME AS security_group_name, EXP(AVG(LN(sgr.TO_PORT - sgr.FROM_PORT + 1))) AS geometric_mean_port_range_size FROM SECURITY_GROUPS AS sg JOIN SECURITY_GROUP_RULES AS sgr ON sg.ID = sgr.PARENT_GROUP_ID WHERE sgr.TO_PORT >= sgr.FROM_PORT GROUP BY sg.NAME ORDER BY geometric_mean_port_range_size DESC), Benchmark AS (SELECT AVG(geometric_mean_port_range_size) AS avg_geometric_mean_port_range_size FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.security_group_name, i.geometric_mean_port_range_size, b.avg_geometric_mean_port_range_size AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.geometric_mean_port_range_size > b.avg_geometric_mean_port_range_size * 1.5 THEN 'WIDE_RANGES' WHEN i.geometric_mean_port_range_size < b.avg_geometric_mean_port_range_size * 0.5 THEN 'NARROW_RANGES' ELSE 'TYPICAL_RANGES' END AS range_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN Benchmark AS b
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.status, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, i.display_name, ie.flavor, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.status ORDER BY m.status) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed') SELECT inner_cte.status, inner_cte.source_compute, inner_cte.dest_compute, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.flavor, inner_cte.window_column, COUNT(*) AS total_completed_migrations_from_source, avg_sources.avg_completed_migrations_per_source FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(source_count) AS avg_completed_migrations_per_source FROM (SELECT source_compute, COUNT(*) AS source_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY source_compute) AS sub) AS avg_sources ON 1 = 1 GROUP BY inner_cte.status, inner_cte.source_compute, inner_cte.dest_compute, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.flavor, inner_cte.window_column, avg_sources.avg_completed_migrations_per_source
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT q.RESOURCE AS resource, EXP(AVG(LN(r.DELTA))) AS geometric_mean FROM QUOTA_USAGES AS q JOIN RESERVATIONS AS r ON q.ID = r.USAGE_ID WHERE r.DELTA > 0 GROUP BY q.RESOURCE ORDER BY geometric_mean DESC) SELECT resource, geometric_mean FROM inner_cte WHERE geometric_mean > (SELECT AVG(geometric_mean) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY geometric_mean DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME, COUNT(i.UUID) AS instance_count, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) AS min_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.VCPUS > 8 GROUP BY i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME) SELECT VM_STATE, MAX(instance_count) AS max_instance_count_per_display_name FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VM_STATE
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, COUNT(m.id) AS migrations FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.display_name ORDER BY migrations DESC LIMIT 10) SELECT inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.migrations AS completed_migrations, SUM(i.vcpus) AS total_vcpus, SUM(i.memory_mb) AS total_memory_mb, CASE WHEN EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM migrations AS m2 JOIN instances AS i2 ON m2.instance_uuid = i2.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism2 ON i2.uuid = ism2.instance_uuid WHERE i2.display_name = inner_cte.display_name AND ism2.key = 'image_image_type' AND m2.status <> 'completed') THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_non_completed_migrations FROM inner_cte JOIN instances AS i ON i.display_name = inner_cte.display_name JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.migrations ORDER BY inner_cte.migrations DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) AS min_instance_memory_mb, SUM(m.MEMORY_REMAINING) AS total_migration_memory_remaining FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded' AND m.STATUS = 'completed') SELECT inner_cte.min_instance_memory_mb, inner_cte.total_migration_memory_remaining, COUNT(DISTINCT i.UUID) AS distinct_instance_count, COUNT(m.ID) AS completed_migration_count FROM inner_cte JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON 1 = 1 JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded' AND m.STATUS = 'completed' GROUP BY inner_cte.min_instance_memory_mb, inner_cte.total_migration_memory_remaining
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY i.DISPLAY_NAME ORDER BY i.MEMORY_MB) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge' ORDER BY i.MEMORY_MB), ForgeAverages AS (SELECT display_name, AVG(memory_mb * 1.0) AS avg_memory_mb FROM inner_cte GROUP BY display_name) SELECT inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.metadata_key, inner_cte.metadata_value, inner_cte.window_column FROM inner_cte JOIN ForgeAverages ON ForgeAverages.display_name = inner_cte.display_name WHERE inner_cte.memory_mb > (SELECT avg_memory_mb FROM ForgeAverages WHERE ForgeAverages.display_name = inner_cte.display_name) ORDER BY inner_cte.memory_mb
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.STATUS AS migration_status, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, m.INSTANCE_UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.MEMORY_MB AS instance_memory_mb, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (ORDER BY m.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed') SELECT DEST_COMPUTE, MAX(window_column) AS max_window_min_instance_memory_mb FROM inner_cte GROUP BY DEST_COMPUTE
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.uuid, ie.flavor, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY i.memory_mb ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE i.display_name = 'forge'), RankAverages AS (SELECT AVG(inner_cte.window_column * 1.0) AS avg_window_rank FROM inner_cte) SELECT inner_cte.uuid, inner_cte.flavor, inner_cte.window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE inner_cte.window_column > (SELECT avg_window_rank FROM RankAverages)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT t1.UUID AS instance_uuid, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY t1.MEMORY_MB ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS t0 JOIN INSTANCES AS t1 ON t0.INSTANCE_UUID = t1.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS t2 ON t1.UUID = t2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE t0.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND t1.DELETED = 0), AvgMem AS (SELECT AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) AS avg_memory_mb FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON i.UUID = ic.instance_uuid) SELECT ic.instance_uuid, ic.window_column, (i.MEMORY_MB - am.avg_memory_mb) AS deviation_from_avg_memory_mb FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON i.UUID = ic.instance_uuid CROSS JOIN AvgMem AS am
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT MIN(q.ID) AS min_usage_id, AVG(r.DELTA) AS avg_reservation_delta FROM QUOTA_USAGES AS q JOIN RESERVATIONS AS r ON q.ID = r.USAGE_ID WHERE q.DELETED = 0 AND r.RESOURCE IN ('cores', 'instances') GROUP BY q.RESOURCE), overall_avg AS (SELECT AVG(r.DELTA) AS overall_avg_reservation_delta FROM QUOTA_USAGES AS q JOIN RESERVATIONS AS r ON q.ID = r.USAGE_ID WHERE q.DELETED = 0 AND r.RESOURCE IN ('cores', 'instances')) SELECT i.min_usage_id, i.avg_reservation_delta, (i.avg_reservation_delta - oa.overall_avg_reservation_delta) AS deviation_from_overall_avg FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN overall_avg AS oa
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, i.availability_zone, m.status, m.migration_type, i.uuid, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY i.uuid ORDER BY i.uuid) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN migrations AS m ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed') SELECT DISTINCT inner_cte.availability_zone AS availability_zone, COUNT(*) AS total_completed_migrations, avg_migrations.avg_migration_count FROM inner_cte, (SELECT AVG(migration_count) AS avg_migration_count FROM (SELECT COUNT(*) AS migration_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY availability_zone) AS sub) AS avg_migrations GROUP BY inner_cte.availability_zone, avg_migrations.avg_migration_count
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.INSTANCE_UUID AS instance_uuid, m.STATUS AS migration_status, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE AS source_compute, m.DEST_COMPUTE AS dest_compute, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.VM_STATE AS instance_vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS instance_memory_mb, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'confirmed' AND m.SOURCE_COMPUTE = 'ether-60') SELECT inner_cte.migration_id, inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.migration_status, inner_cte.source_compute, inner_cte.dest_compute, inner_cte.instance_display_name, inner_cte.instance_vm_state, inner_cte.instance_memory_mb, inner_cte.window_column, i.project_id AS project_id, CASE WHEN EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM migrations AS m2 JOIN instances AS i2 ON m2.instance_uuid = i2.uuid WHERE i2.project_id = i.project_id AND m2.deleted = 0 AND NOT m2.status IN ('completed', 'failed', 'error') AND m2.id <> inner_cte.migration_id) THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS project_has_other_active_migrations FROM inner_cte JOIN instances AS i ON inner_cte.instance_uuid = i.uuid ORDER BY inner_cte.migration_status, inner_cte.migration_id
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, m.status, MAX(m.memory_total) AS max_memory_total, m.created_at FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.deleted = 0 GROUP BY i.display_name, m.status, m.created_at HAVING MAX(m.memory_total) > 0) SELECT inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.status, inner_cte.max_memory_total, inner_cte.created_at, CASE WHEN EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM instances AS i2 JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism2 ON i2.uuid = ism2.instance_uuid WHERE i2.display_name = inner_cte.display_name AND i2.deleted = 0 AND ism2.deleted = 0) THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_system_metadata FROM inner_cte ORDER BY inner_cte.max_memory_total DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, SUM(i.memory_mb) AS total_memory_mb FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON ie.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS iic ON i.uuid = iic.instance_uuid WHERE i.vm_state = 'shelved_offloaded' GROUP BY i.display_name HAVING SUM(i.memory_mb) > 20000), AvgTotals AS (SELECT AVG(inner_cte.total_memory_mb * 1.0) AS avg_total_memory_mb FROM inner_cte) SELECT inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.total_memory_mb FROM inner_cte WHERE inner_cte.total_memory_mb > (SELECT avg_total_memory_mb FROM AvgTotals)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.status AS migration_status, i.vm_state AS instance_vm_state, COUNT(m.id) AS migration_count, MIN(i.memory_mb) AS min_instance_memory_mb FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE ism.key = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY m.status, i.vm_state), GroupAverages AS (SELECT AVG(inner_cte.migration_count * 1.0) AS avg_migration_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT inner_cte.migration_status, inner_cte.instance_vm_state, inner_cte.migration_count, inner_cte.min_instance_memory_mb FROM inner_cte WHERE inner_cte.migration_count > (SELECT avg_migration_count FROM GroupAverages)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, COUNT(m.id) AS migrations FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.display_name ORDER BY migrations DESC LIMIT 10) SELECT display_name, migrations FROM inner_cte WHERE migrations > (SELECT AVG(migrations) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY migrations DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID, ism.KEY, ism.VALUE, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ism.KEY ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge') SELECT inner_cte.UUID, inner_cte.KEY, inner_cte.VALUE, inner_cte.window_column, i.MEMORY_MB FROM inner_cte JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON i.UUID = inner_cte.UUID WHERE i.MEMORY_MB > 16000
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY i.VM_STATE ORDER BY i.MEMORY_MB ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge') SELECT inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.instance_extra_id, inner_cte.window_column FROM inner_cte CROSS JOIN (SELECT MAX(memory_mb) AS max_memory_mb FROM inner_cte) AS m WHERE (CAST(inner_cte.memory_mb * 1.0 AS REAL) / m.max_memory_mb) > 0.75
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID, i.HOSTNAME, i.VM_STATE, i.MEMORY_MB, c.NETWORK_INFO, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (ORDER BY i.ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS c ON i.UUID = c.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge') SELECT CASE WHEN LAG(vm_state) OVER (ORDER BY uuid) = vm_state THEN NULL ELSE vm_state END AS vm_state, uuid, hostname, memory_mb, network_info, window_column, COUNT(*) OVER () AS total_forge_instances, AVG(memory_mb) OVER () AS avg_forge_memory_mb FROM inner_cte ORDER BY uuid
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, COUNT(m.id) AS migrations FROM instances AS i JOIN migrations AS m ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' GROUP BY i.display_name ORDER BY migrations DESC LIMIT 10) SELECT inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.migrations, MAX(i.memory_mb) AS memory_mb, MAX(i.vcpus) AS vcpus FROM inner_cte JOIN instances AS i ON i.display_name = inner_cte.display_name GROUP BY inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.migrations ORDER BY inner_cte.migrations DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT ins.VM_STATE, ins.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, COUNT(ins.UUID) AS instance_count, MIN(ins.MEMORY_MB) AS min_memory_mb FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS ins ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = ins.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ins.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY ins.VM_STATE, ins.AVAILABILITY_ZONE) SELECT inner_cte.VM_STATE, inner_cte.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, inner_cte.instance_count, inner_cte.min_memory_mb FROM inner_cte ORDER BY inner_cte.instance_count DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, m.DEST_HOST, m.STATUS AS migration_status, i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.VM_STATE AS instance_vm_state, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'confirmed' AND m.DEST_COMPUTE = 'glint-94'), StatusBenchmarks AS (SELECT migration_status, COUNT(*) AS total_rows_in_status, AVG(migration_id) AS avg_migration_id_in_status FROM inner_cte GROUP BY migration_status) SELECT ic.migration_id, ic.SOURCE_COMPUTE, ic.DEST_COMPUTE, ic.DEST_HOST, ic.migration_status, ic.instance_uuid, ic.instance_display_name, ic.instance_vm_state, ic.metadata_key, ic.metadata_value, ic.window_column, sb.total_rows_in_status, sb.avg_migration_id_in_status AS benchmark_avg_migration_id, CASE WHEN ic.migration_id > sb.avg_migration_id_in_status THEN 'ABOVE_AVG_ID' WHEN ic.migration_id < sb.avg_migration_id_in_status THEN 'BELOW_AVG_ID' ELSE 'AT_AVG_ID' END AS id_vs_benchmark_category FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN StatusBenchmarks AS sb ON ic.migration_status = sb.migration_status
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.status, EXP(AVG(LN(i.memory_mb))) AS geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE i.memory_mb > 0 GROUP BY m.status ORDER BY geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC) SELECT m.status, inner_cte.geometric_mean_memory_mb, EXP(AVG(LN(i.vcpus))) AS geometric_mean_vcpus FROM inner_cte JOIN migrations AS m ON m.status = inner_cte.status JOIN instances AS i ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE i.memory_mb > 0 AND i.vcpus > 0 GROUP BY m.status, inner_cte.geometric_mean_memory_mb ORDER BY inner_cte.geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.uuid, i.display_name, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, m.status, MIN(i.memory_mb) OVER (ORDER BY m.id ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN migrations AS m ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed') SELECT inner_cte.source_compute, MAX(inner_cte.window_column) AS max_window_min_memory_mb FROM inner_cte GROUP BY inner_cte.source_compute
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS flare3_instances_with_network_cache FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.DELETED = 0), ZoneCounts AS (SELECT i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, COUNT(*) AS instances_with_network_cache FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 GROUP BY i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE), ZoneAverages AS (SELECT AVG(instances_with_network_cache * 1.0) AS avg_instances_with_network_cache FROM ZoneCounts) SELECT inner_cte.flare3_instances_with_network_cache, CASE WHEN inner_cte.flare3_instances_with_network_cache > (SELECT avg_instances_with_network_cache FROM ZoneAverages) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS flare3_above_zone_average FROM inner_cte
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY i.DISPLAY_NAME ORDER BY i.MEMORY_MB) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge' ORDER BY i.MEMORY_MB) SELECT instance_uuid, display_name, memory_mb, metadata_key, metadata_value, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE memory_mb > (SELECT AVG(memory_mb) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY memory_mb
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, COUNT(iic.id) AS cache_records FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON i.uuid = iic.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3' GROUP BY i.display_name ORDER BY cache_records DESC LIMIT 10), AverageCache AS (SELECT AVG(cache_records) AS avg_cache_records FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.display_name, ic.cache_records, ac.avg_cache_records AS benchmark, CASE WHEN ic.cache_records > ac.avg_cache_records * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_CACHE' WHEN ic.cache_records < ac.avg_cache_records * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_CACHE' ELSE 'AVERAGE_CACHE' END AS cache_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageCache AS ac
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, m.STATUS AS migration_status, COUNT(i.ID) AS instance_count, MIN(m.MEMORY_TOTAL) AS min_memory_total FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.HIDDEN = 0 AND NOT m.STATUS IN ('accepted', 'confirmed', 'reverted', 'error', 'failed', 'completed') GROUP BY i.VM_STATE, m.STATUS) SELECT vm_state, MAX(min_memory_total) AS max_of_min_memory_total FROM inner_cte WHERE instance_count >= 2 GROUP BY vm_state
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT t1.UUID, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY t1.MEMORY_MB ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS t0 JOIN INSTANCES AS t1 ON t0.INSTANCE_UUID = t1.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS t2 ON t1.UUID = t2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE t1.DELETED = 0 AND t1.host = 'blaze8-12') SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, inner_cte.window_column AS dense_rank_by_memory_window, i.hostname AS instance_hostname, CASE WHEN EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic2 WHERE iic2.instance_uuid = i.uuid AND iic2.deleted = 0) THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_instance_info_cache FROM inner_cte JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON i.uuid = inner_cte.uuid
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.MEMORY_MB, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT CASE WHEN LAG(m.STATUS) OVER (ORDER BY inner_cte.migration_id) = m.STATUS THEN NULL ELSE m.STATUS END AS migration_status, inner_cte.migration_id, inner_cte.SOURCE_COMPUTE, inner_cte.DEST_COMPUTE, inner_cte.DISPLAY_NAME, inner_cte.MEMORY_MB, inner_cte.metadata_key, inner_cte.metadata_value, inner_cte.window_column FROM inner_cte JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON m.ID = inner_cte.migration_id ORDER BY inner_cte.migration_id
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.VM_STATE, SUM(i.MEMORY_MB) AS total_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic ON i.UUID = ic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.VM_STATE HAVING SUM(i.MEMORY_MB) > 50000) SELECT 'flare3' AS availability_zone, MAX(inner_cte.total_memory_mb) AS max_total_memory_mb_across_vm_states FROM inner_cte
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.VM_STATE, ism.KEY, ism.VALUE, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT inner_cte.UUID, inner_cte.DISPLAY_NAME, inner_cte.VM_STATE, inner_cte.KEY, inner_cte.VALUE, inner_cte.window_column FROM inner_cte JOIN INSTANCES AS i2 ON i2.UUID = inner_cte.UUID WHERE i2.MEMORY_MB > (SELECT AVG(i3.MEMORY_MB * 1.0) FROM INSTANCES AS i3 WHERE i3.DELETED = 0 AND i3.HOST = 'blaze8-12')
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name AS instance_display_name, m.status AS migration_status, COUNT(m.id) AS migration_count, SUM(m.memory_processed) AS total_memory_processed FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE ism.key = 'image_image_type' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, m.status) SELECT inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.instance_display_name, inner_cte.migration_status, inner_cte.migration_count, inner_cte.total_memory_processed FROM inner_cte JOIN migrations AS m2 ON m2.instance_uuid = inner_cte.instance_uuid AND m2.status = inner_cte.migration_status GROUP BY inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.instance_display_name, inner_cte.migration_status, inner_cte.migration_count, inner_cte.total_memory_processed HAVING (CAST(inner_cte.total_memory_processed * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(SUM(m2.memory_total), 0)) > 0.75
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, COUNT(ie.id) AS extra_records FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.display_name ORDER BY extra_records DESC LIMIT 10) SELECT i.availability_zone, MAX(inner_cte.extra_records) AS max_extra_records_per_display_name FROM inner_cte JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON i.display_name = inner_cte.display_name WHERE i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.availability_zone
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT t1.UUID, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY t0.IN_USE ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM QUOTA_USAGES AS t0 JOIN RESERVATIONS AS t1 ON t0.ID = t1.USAGE_ID WHERE t1.RESOURCE = 'cores' AND t1.EXPIRE < '2021-05-13 16:53:07') SELECT r.PROJECT_ID AS project_id, SUM(r.DELTA) AS total_reserved_delta, MAX(i.window_column) AS max_dense_rank_in_project, CASE WHEN EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM RESERVATIONS AS r2 JOIN QUOTA_USAGES AS q2 ON q2.ID = r2.USAGE_ID WHERE r2.PROJECT_ID = r.PROJECT_ID AND r2.RESOURCE = 'cores' AND r2.EXPIRE < '2021-05-13 16:53:07' AND q2.RESERVED > 0) THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_any_positive_reserved_quota FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN RESERVATIONS AS r ON r.UUID = i.UUID JOIN QUOTA_USAGES AS q ON q.ID = r.USAGE_ID WHERE r.RESOURCE = 'cores' AND r.EXPIRE < '2021-05-13 16:53:07' GROUP BY r.PROJECT_ID ORDER BY total_reserved_delta DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS flare3_instances_with_network_cache FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.DELETED = 0 AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), HostZoneCounts AS (SELECT i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, i.HOST, COUNT(*) AS instances_with_network_cache FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 GROUP BY i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, i.HOST), AverageCount AS (SELECT AVG(instances_with_network_cache) AS avg_instances_with_network_cache FROM HostZoneCounts) SELECT ic.flare3_instances_with_network_cache, (ic.flare3_instances_with_network_cache - ac.avg_instances_with_network_cache) AS deviation_from_avg FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageCount AS ac
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.status, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, i.display_name, ic.network_info, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.status ORDER BY m.status) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic ON i.uuid = ic.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'confirmed' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12') SELECT inner_cte.status, inner_cte.source_compute, inner_cte.dest_compute, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.network_info, inner_cte.window_column, i.project_id, CASE WHEN NOT i.project_id IS NULL AND i.project_id <> '' THEN 'HAS_PROJECT' ELSE 'NO_PROJECT' END AS project_presence_category FROM inner_cte JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON i.display_name = inner_cte.display_name
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.HOSTNAME AS instance_hostname, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE AS migration_source_compute, m.DEST_COMPUTE AS migration_dest_compute, m.DEST_HOST AS migration_dest_host, m.MIGRATION_TYPE AS migration_type, m.MEMORY_TOTAL AS memory_total, m.MEMORY_PROCESSED AS memory_processed, m.MEMORY_REMAINING AS memory_remaining, MIN(m.MEMORY_REMAINING) OVER (PARTITION BY m.INSTANCE_UUID ORDER BY m.INSTANCE_UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT i.HOST AS instance_host, MAX(inner_cte.window_column) AS max_of_instance_min_memory_remaining FROM inner_cte JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON i.UUID = inner_cte.instance_uuid GROUP BY i.HOST
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, COUNT(ie.id) AS extra_records FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.display_name ORDER BY extra_records DESC LIMIT 10), totals AS (SELECT COUNT(ie.id) AS total_extra_records FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12') SELECT ic.display_name, ic.extra_records, (CAST(ic.extra_records * 1.0 AS REAL) / t.total_extra_records) AS pct_of_total_extra_records FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN totals AS t ORDER BY ic.extra_records DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.INSTANCE_UUID, m.MIGRATION_TYPE, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, m.DEST_HOST, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.VM_STATE, i.MEMORY_MB, i.VCPUS, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, m.MEMORY_TOTAL, MIN(m.MEMORY_TOTAL) OVER (PARTITION BY m.MIGRATION_TYPE ORDER BY m.MIGRATION_TYPE) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), avg_by_type AS (SELECT MIGRATION_TYPE, AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_min_memory_total FROM inner_cte GROUP BY MIGRATION_TYPE) SELECT ic.migration_id, ic.INSTANCE_UUID, ic.MIGRATION_TYPE, ic.SOURCE_COMPUTE, ic.DEST_COMPUTE, ic.DEST_HOST, ic.DISPLAY_NAME, ic.VM_STATE, ic.MEMORY_MB, ic.VCPUS, ic.instance_extra_id, ic.MEMORY_TOTAL, ic.window_column, abt.avg_window_min_memory_total, (ic.MEMORY_TOTAL - abt.avg_window_min_memory_total) AS deviation_from_type_avg_window_min FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN avg_by_type AS abt ON ic.MIGRATION_TYPE = abt.MIGRATION_TYPE
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT ins.VM_STATE, ins.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, COUNT(ins.UUID) AS instance_count, MIN(ins.MEMORY_MB) AS min_memory_mb FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS ins ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = ins.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ins.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND ins.HOST = 'prime-35' GROUP BY ins.VM_STATE, ins.AVAILABILITY_ZONE), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(inner_cte.instance_count * 1.0) AS avg_instance_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT inner_cte.VM_STATE, inner_cte.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, inner_cte.instance_count, inner_cte.min_memory_mb FROM inner_cte WHERE inner_cte.instance_count > (SELECT avg_instance_count FROM avg_cte)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.status, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, i.display_name, ism.key, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.instance_uuid ORDER BY m.instance_uuid) AS window_column FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12') SELECT inner_cte.status, inner_cte.source_compute, inner_cte.dest_compute, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.key, inner_cte.window_column, COUNT(*) AS total_migrations_for_dest_compute, avg_migrations.avg_migration_count FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(migration_count) AS avg_migration_count FROM (SELECT dest_compute, COUNT(*) AS migration_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY dest_compute) AS sub) AS avg_migrations ON 1 = 1 GROUP BY inner_cte.status, inner_cte.source_compute, inner_cte.dest_compute, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.key, inner_cte.window_column, avg_migrations.avg_migration_count
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.vm_state, EXP(AVG(LN(i.memory_mb))) AS geometric_mean FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON i.uuid = c.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.vm_state ORDER BY geometric_mean DESC), overall_cte AS (SELECT EXP(AVG(LN(i.memory_mb))) AS overall_geometric_mean FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON i.uuid = c.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12') SELECT ic.vm_state, ic.geometric_mean, (ic.geometric_mean - oc.overall_geometric_mean) AS deviation_from_overall_geometric_mean FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN overall_cte AS oc ORDER BY ic.geometric_mean DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.STATUS AS migration_status, ins.VM_STATE AS instance_vm_state, COUNT(m.ID) AS migration_count, MIN(m.CREATED_AT) AS earliest_migration_created_at FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS ins ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = ins.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ins.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE ins.VCPUS >= 8 AND NOT m.STATUS IN ('accepted', 'confirmed', 'reverted', 'error', 'failed', 'completed') GROUP BY m.STATUS, ins.VM_STATE), AverageMigrations AS (SELECT AVG(migration_count) AS avg_migration_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.migration_status, ic.instance_vm_state, ic.migration_count, ic.earliest_migration_created_at, am.avg_migration_count AS benchmark, CASE WHEN ic.migration_count > am.avg_migration_count * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_MIGRATION_ACTIVITY' WHEN ic.migration_count < am.avg_migration_count * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_MIGRATION_ACTIVITY' ELSE 'AVERAGE_MIGRATION_ACTIVITY' END AS migration_activity_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageMigrations AS am
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT sg.ID AS security_group_id, sg.NAME AS security_group_name, sgr.ID AS rule_id, sgr.CIDR AS rule_cidr, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sg.ID ORDER BY sgr.ID) AS window_column FROM SECURITY_GROUPS AS sg JOIN SECURITY_GROUP_RULES AS sgr ON sg.ID = sgr.PARENT_GROUP_ID JOIN services AS s ON s.binary = 'nova-scheduler' WHERE sgr.PROTOCOL = 'tcp') SELECT security_group_id, security_group_name, rule_id, rule_cidr, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE rule_id > (SELECT AVG(rule_id) FROM inner_cte)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.STATUS AS migration_status, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, ic.NETWORK_INFO, AVG(m.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic ON i.UUID = ic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' ORDER BY m.ID) SELECT migration_id, migration_status, SOURCE_COMPUTE, DEST_COMPUTE, instance_display_name, NETWORK_INFO, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE migration_id > (SELECT AVG(migration_id) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY migration_id
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.VM_STATE, EXP(AVG(LN(m.MEMORY_TOTAL))) AS geometric_mean_memory_total FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.MEMORY_TOTAL > 0 AND i.DELETED = 0 AND NOT m.STATUS IN ('accepted', 'confirmed', 'reverted', 'error', 'failed', 'completed') GROUP BY i.VM_STATE ORDER BY geometric_mean_memory_total DESC) SELECT inner_cte.VM_STATE, inner_cte.geometric_mean_memory_total FROM inner_cte WHERE inner_cte.geometric_mean_memory_total > 10000000000 ORDER BY inner_cte.geometric_mean_memory_total DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT ins.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, ins.VM_STATE, COUNT(ins.UUID) AS instance_count, MIN(ins.CREATED_AT) AS earliest_created_at FROM INSTANCES AS ins JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = ins.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = ins.UUID WHERE ins.DELETED = 0 AND ins.HOST = 'prime-35' GROUP BY ins.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, ins.VM_STATE) SELECT AVAILABILITY_ZONE, MAX(instance_count) AS max_instance_count_across_vm_states FROM inner_cte GROUP BY AVAILABILITY_ZONE
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT MIN(i.ID) AS min_instance_id, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) AS avg_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE (i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded' OR i.VCPUS >= 8) AND i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME) SELECT i.display_name, inner_cte.min_instance_id, inner_cte.avg_memory_mb, CASE WHEN EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM instances AS i2 JOIN instance_extra AS ie2 ON i2.uuid = ie2.instance_uuid WHERE i2.display_name = i.display_name AND NOT ie2.migration_context IS NULL AND ie2.migration_context <> '') THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_migration_context FROM inner_cte JOIN instances AS i ON i.id = inner_cte.min_instance_id ORDER BY inner_cte.avg_memory_mb DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.HOSTNAME AS hostname, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY i.UUID ORDER BY ism.KEY) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.hostname, inner_cte.instance_extra_id, inner_cte.metadata_key, inner_cte.metadata_value, inner_cte.window_column FROM inner_cte JOIN INSTANCES AS i2 ON i2.UUID = inner_cte.instance_uuid WHERE (CAST(i2.MEMORY_MB * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(i2.ROOT_GB, 0)) > 0.75
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.INSTANCE_UUID, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, AVG(m.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY m.INSTANCE_UUID ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' ORDER BY m.ID), instance_rollup AS (SELECT INSTANCE_UUID, COUNT(*) AS total_metadata_rows, AVG(window_column) AS avg_running_avg_migration_id FROM inner_cte GROUP BY INSTANCE_UUID) SELECT ic.migration_id, ic.INSTANCE_UUID, ic.SOURCE_COMPUTE, ic.DEST_COMPUTE, ic.instance_display_name, ic.metadata_key, ic.metadata_value, ic.window_column, ir.total_metadata_rows, ir.avg_running_avg_migration_id FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN instance_rollup AS ir ON ic.INSTANCE_UUID = ir.INSTANCE_UUID ORDER BY ic.migration_id
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.status, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, i.display_name, ie.flavor, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.status ORDER BY m.status) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12'), avg_rank AS (SELECT AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.status, ic.source_compute, ic.dest_compute, ic.display_name, ic.flavor, ic.window_column, ar.avg_window_column, (ic.window_column - ar.avg_window_column) AS deviation_from_avg_window_column FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_rank AS ar
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT MIN(i.memory_mb) AS min_instance_memory_mb, SUM(m.memory_processed) AS total_migration_memory_processed FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12'), HostAgg AS (SELECT i.host, MIN(i.memory_mb) AS min_instance_memory_mb, SUM(m.memory_processed) AS total_migration_memory_processed FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' GROUP BY i.host) SELECT ha.host, ha.min_instance_memory_mb, ha.total_migration_memory_processed FROM HostAgg AS ha CROSS JOIN inner_cte AS ic WHERE ha.host = 'blaze8-12' AND ha.total_migration_memory_processed > (SELECT AVG(total_migration_memory_processed) FROM HostAgg)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.MEMORY_MB AS instance_memory_mb, SUM(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), avg_window AS (SELECT AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.migration_id, ic.instance_display_name, ic.instance_memory_mb, ic.window_column AS running_sum_instance_memory_mb, (ic.window_column - aw.avg_window_column) AS deviation_from_avg_running_sum_instance_memory_mb FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_window AS aw
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.STATUS AS migration_status, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, m.DEST_HOST, m.INSTANCE_UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.VM_STATE AS instance_vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS instance_memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS instance_vcpus, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, m.MEMORY_REMAINING AS migration_memory_remaining, MIN(m.MEMORY_REMAINING) OVER (PARTITION BY m.DEST_COMPUTE ORDER BY m.DEST_COMPUTE) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'ether-18') SELECT DEST_COMPUTE, MAX(instance_vcpus) AS max_instance_vcpus FROM inner_cte GROUP BY DEST_COMPUTE ORDER BY DEST_COMPUTE
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY i.MEMORY_MB ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT inner_cte.migration_id, inner_cte.window_column, avg_window.avg_window_column FROM inner_cte, (SELECT AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte) AS avg_window
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name, ism.value AS image_type_value, COUNT(iic.id) AS cache_record_count, SUM(i.memory_mb) AS total_memory_mb FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON ism.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS iic ON iic.instance_uuid = i.uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, ism.value), MaxMemoryPerImageType AS (SELECT image_type_value, MAX(total_memory_mb) AS max_total_memory_mb FROM inner_cte GROUP BY image_type_value) SELECT m.image_type_value, m.max_total_memory_mb, COUNT(*) AS instance_count_at_max FROM MaxMemoryPerImageType AS m JOIN inner_cte AS ic ON ic.image_type_value = m.image_type_value AND ic.total_memory_mb = m.max_total_memory_mb GROUP BY m.image_type_value, m.max_total_memory_mb
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.FLAVOR AS flavor, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), HostAverages AS (SELECT i.HOST AS host, i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE AS availability_zone, AVG(i.VCPUS * 1.0) AS avg_vcpus FROM INSTANCES AS i WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.HOST, i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE) SELECT inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.vcpus, inner_cte.flavor, inner_cte.metadata_key, inner_cte.metadata_value, inner_cte.window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE inner_cte.vcpus > (SELECT HostAverages.avg_vcpus FROM HostAverages) ORDER BY inner_cte.metadata_key, inner_cte.instance_uuid
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, MAX(i.MEMORY_MB) AS max_memory_mb, i.LAUNCHED_AT FROM INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON i.UUID = iic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME, ism.KEY, i.LAUNCHED_AT HAVING MAX(i.MEMORY_MB) > 16000), MaxMemoryAverages AS (SELECT AVG(inner_cte.max_memory_mb * 1.0) AS avg_of_group_max_memory_mb FROM inner_cte) SELECT inner_cte.DISPLAY_NAME, inner_cte.metadata_key, inner_cte.max_memory_mb, inner_cte.LAUNCHED_AT FROM inner_cte WHERE inner_cte.max_memory_mb > (SELECT avg_of_group_max_memory_mb FROM MaxMemoryAverages)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT MIN(i.ID) AS min_instance_id, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) AS avg_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic ON ic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.POWER_STATE = 4 AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME) SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, inner_cte.min_instance_id, inner_cte.avg_memory_mb, CASE WHEN EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM INSTANCES AS i2 JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie2 ON ie2.INSTANCE_UUID = i2.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic2 ON ic2.INSTANCE_UUID = i2.UUID WHERE i2.DELETED = 0 AND i2.POWER_STATE = 4 AND i2.HOST = 'blaze8-12' AND i2.DISPLAY_NAME = i.DISPLAY_NAME AND NOT ie2.MIGRATION_CONTEXT IS NULL AND ie2.MIGRATION_CONTEXT <> '') THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_migration_context FROM inner_cte JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON i.ID = inner_cte.min_instance_id ORDER BY inner_cte.avg_memory_mb DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.VM_STATE, i.MEMORY_MB, i.VCPUS, i.KEY_NAME, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY i.VCPUS ORDER BY i.VCPUS) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), VcpuAverages AS (SELECT i2.VCPUS, AVG(i2.MEMORY_MB * 1.0) AS avg_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i2 WHERE i2.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i2.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i2.VCPUS) SELECT inner_cte.UUID, inner_cte.DISPLAY_NAME, inner_cte.VM_STATE, inner_cte.MEMORY_MB, inner_cte.VCPUS, inner_cte.KEY_NAME, inner_cte.instance_extra_id, inner_cte.window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE inner_cte.MEMORY_MB > (SELECT VcpuAverages.avg_memory_mb FROM VcpuAverages WHERE VcpuAverages.VCPUS = inner_cte.VCPUS)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS completed_migrations_for_shelved_instances FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, COUNT(*) AS completed_migrations_for_state, avg_counts.avg_completed_migrations_per_state, inner_cte.completed_migrations_for_shelved_instances FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID JOIN inner_cte ON 1 = 1 JOIN (SELECT AVG(state_migration_count) AS avg_completed_migrations_per_state FROM (SELECT i2.VM_STATE, COUNT(*) AS state_migration_count FROM INSTANCES AS i2 JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m2 ON i2.UUID = m2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m2.STATUS = 'completed' AND i2.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i2.VM_STATE) AS sub) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1 WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.VM_STATE, avg_counts.avg_completed_migrations_per_state, inner_cte.completed_migrations_for_shelved_instances
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.vm_state, EXP(AVG(LN(i.memory_mb))) AS geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.memory_mb > 0 AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.vm_state ORDER BY geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC), ProjectVmStateGeoMean AS (SELECT i.project_id, inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM inner_cte JOIN instances AS i ON i.vm_state = inner_cte.vm_state JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.memory_mb > 0 AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.project_id, inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.geometric_mean_memory_mb) SELECT vm_state, MAX(geometric_mean_memory_mb) AS max_geometric_mean_memory_mb_across_projects FROM ProjectVmStateGeoMean GROUP BY vm_state ORDER BY max_geometric_mean_memory_mb_across_projects DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT t1.UUID AS instance_uuid, t2.VALUE AS metadata_value, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY t1.MEMORY_MB ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS t0 JOIN INSTANCES AS t1 ON t0.INSTANCE_UUID = t1.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS t2 ON t1.UUID = t2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE t1.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND t1.HOST = 'blaze8-12' AND t2.KEY = 'image_image_type'), avg_rank AS (SELECT AVG(window_column) AS avg_dense_rank FROM inner_cte) SELECT ar.avg_dense_rank, CASE WHEN ar.avg_dense_rank >= 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_RANK' WHEN ar.avg_dense_rank <= 0.5 THEN 'LOW_RANK' ELSE 'AVERAGE_RANK' END AS rank_category FROM avg_rank AS ar
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.STATUS AS migration_status, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, m.DEST_HOST, m.INSTANCE_UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.VM_STATE AS instance_vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS instance_memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS instance_vcpus, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, m.MEMORY_REMAINING AS migration_memory_remaining, MIN(m.MEMORY_REMAINING) OVER (PARTITION BY m.DEST_COMPUTE ORDER BY m.DEST_COMPUTE) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'ether-18') SELECT CASE WHEN LAG(DEST_COMPUTE) OVER (ORDER BY DEST_COMPUTE, migration_id, metadata_key) = DEST_COMPUTE THEN NULL ELSE DEST_COMPUTE END AS dest_compute, migration_id, migration_status, SOURCE_COMPUTE, DEST_HOST, INSTANCE_UUID, instance_display_name, instance_vm_state, instance_memory_mb, instance_vcpus, metadata_key, metadata_value, migration_memory_remaining, window_column AS min_migration_memory_remaining_in_dest_compute, COUNT(*) OVER (PARTITION BY DEST_COMPUTE) AS total_rows_in_dest_compute FROM inner_cte ORDER BY DEST_COMPUTE, migration_id, metadata_key
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, c.ID AS instance_info_cache_id, SUM(m.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY i.INSTANCE_TYPE_ID ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS c ON i.UUID = c.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT migration_id, instance_display_name, instance_info_cache_id, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE window_column > (SELECT AVG(window_column) FROM inner_cte)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY i.VM_STATE ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), avg_mem AS (SELECT AVG(memory_mb) AS avg_memory_mb FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.instance_uuid, ic.display_name, ic.vm_state, ic.memory_mb, ic.vcpus, ic.instance_extra_id, ic.window_column, (ic.memory_mb - am.avg_memory_mb) AS deviation_from_avg_memory_mb FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_mem AS am
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.status, SUM(m.memory_processed) AS total_memory_processed FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE ism.key = 'image_image_type' AND NOT m.status IN ('accepted', 'confirmed', 'reverted', 'error', 'failed', 'completed') GROUP BY m.status HAVING SUM(m.memory_processed) > 0), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(total_memory_processed) AS avg_total_memory_processed FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.status, ic.total_memory_processed, a.avg_total_memory_processed AS benchmark, CASE WHEN ic.total_memory_processed > a.avg_total_memory_processed * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_MEMORY' WHEN ic.total_memory_processed < a.avg_total_memory_processed * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_MEMORY' ELSE 'AVERAGE_MEMORY' END AS memory_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.INSTANCE_UUID, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, AVG(m.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY m.INSTANCE_UUID ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' ORDER BY m.ID), ProjectMigrations AS (SELECT i.PROJECT_ID AS project_id, COUNT(DISTINCT ic.migration_id) AS total_migrations FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID GROUP BY i.PROJECT_ID), AverageMigrations AS (SELECT AVG(total_migrations) AS avg_migrations FROM ProjectMigrations) SELECT ic.migration_id, ic.INSTANCE_UUID, ic.SOURCE_COMPUTE, ic.DEST_COMPUTE, ic.instance_display_name, ic.metadata_key, ic.metadata_value, ic.window_column, i.PROJECT_ID AS project_id, pm.total_migrations, am.avg_migrations AS benchmark, CASE WHEN pm.total_migrations > am.avg_migrations * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_UTILIZATION' WHEN pm.total_migrations < am.avg_migrations * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_UTILIZATION' ELSE 'AVERAGE_UTILIZATION' END AS utilization_category FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN ProjectMigrations AS pm ON pm.project_id = i.PROJECT_ID CROSS JOIN AverageMigrations AS am ORDER BY ic.migration_id
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT t1.UUID, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY t0.IN_USE ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM QUOTA_USAGES AS t0 JOIN RESERVATIONS AS t1 ON t0.ID = t1.USAGE_ID WHERE t1.RESOURCE = 'cores' AND t1.EXPIRE < '2021-05-13 16:53:07') SELECT r.PROJECT_ID, MAX(inner_cte.window_column) AS max_dense_rank FROM inner_cte JOIN RESERVATIONS AS r ON r.UUID = inner_cte.UUID GROUP BY r.PROJECT_ID
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, COUNT(m.id) AS migrations FROM instances AS i JOIN migrations AS m ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.display_name ORDER BY migrations DESC LIMIT 10) SELECT ic.display_name, ic.migrations AS completed_migrations, SUM(i.vcpus) AS total_vcpus, SUM(i.memory_mb) AS total_memory_mb, CASE WHEN EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM migrations AS m2 JOIN instances AS i2 ON m2.instance_uuid = i2.uuid WHERE i2.display_name = ic.display_name AND i2.host = 'blaze8-12' AND NOT m2.status IN ('completed', 'failed', 'error')) THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_active_migrations FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN instances AS i ON i.display_name = ic.display_name AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY ic.display_name, ic.migrations ORDER BY ic.migrations DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.VM_STATE, EXP(AVG(LN(m.MEMORY_TOTAL))) AS geometric_mean_memory_total FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.MEMORY_TOTAL > 0 AND i.DELETED = 0 AND NOT m.STATUS IN ('accepted', 'confirmed', 'reverted', 'error', 'failed', 'completed') GROUP BY i.VM_STATE ORDER BY geometric_mean_memory_total DESC) SELECT inner_cte.VM_STATE, inner_cte.geometric_mean_memory_total, overall_avg.avg_geometric_mean_memory_total FROM inner_cte, (SELECT AVG(geometric_mean_memory_total) AS avg_geometric_mean_memory_total FROM inner_cte) AS overall_avg ORDER BY inner_cte.geometric_mean_memory_total DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT ie.INSTANCE_UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, ie.FLAVOR, ism.KEY, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY ism.KEY) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE ie.DELETED = 0 AND ism.DELETED = 0 AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT inner_cte.INSTANCE_UUID, inner_cte.DISPLAY_NAME, inner_cte.FLAVOR, inner_cte.KEY, inner_cte.window_column, i.MEMORY_MB FROM inner_cte JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON inner_cte.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.MEMORY_MB > 16384
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT m.status, EXP(AVG(LN(i.memory_mb))) AS geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE i.memory_mb > 0 AND NOT m.status IN ('accepted', 'confirmed', 'reverted', 'error', 'failed', 'completed') GROUP BY m.status ORDER BY geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC), Benchmark AS (SELECT EXP(AVG(LN(i.memory_mb))) AS benchmark_geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE i.memory_mb > 0 AND NOT m.status IN ('accepted', 'confirmed', 'reverted', 'error', 'failed', 'completed')) SELECT ic.status, ic.geometric_mean_memory_mb, b.benchmark_geometric_mean_memory_mb AS benchmark, CASE WHEN ic.geometric_mean_memory_mb > b.benchmark_geometric_mean_memory_mb * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_MEMORY' WHEN ic.geometric_mean_memory_mb < b.benchmark_geometric_mean_memory_mb * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_MEMORY' ELSE 'AVERAGE_MEMORY' END AS memory_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN Benchmark AS b
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT i.display_name, COUNT(m.id) AS migrations FROM instances AS i JOIN migrations AS m ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.display_name ORDER BY migrations DESC LIMIT 10) SELECT inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.migrations, avg_migrations.avg_migrations_per_instance FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(migration_count) AS avg_migrations_per_instance FROM (SELECT i.uuid, COUNT(m.id) AS migration_count FROM instances AS i JOIN migrations AS m ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.uuid) AS per_instance) AS avg_migrations ON 1 = 1 ORDER BY inner_cte.migrations DESC

SELECT i.display_name, i.vm_state, i.memory_mb, c.network_info, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY i.project_id ORDER BY i.project_id) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON i.uuid = c.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12'

SELECT COUNT(*) AS qemu_compute_nodes_with_resource_provider FROM COMPUTE_NODES AS cn JOIN RESOURCE_PROVIDERS AS rp ON cn.UUID = rp.UUID JOIN AGGREGATE_HOSTS AS ah ON ah.HOST = cn.HOST WHERE cn.HYPERVISOR_TYPE = 'QEMU' AND ah.AGGREGATE_ID = '1'
SELECT i.vm_state, EXP(AVG(LN(i.memory_mb))) AS geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.vm_state ORDER BY geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC
SELECT MIN(i.ID) AS min_instance_id, AVG(m.ID) AS avg_migration_id FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE (i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded' OR m.STATUS = 'completed') AND i.HOST = 'prime-35' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME
SELECT m.status, SUM(m.memory_processed) AS total_memory_processed FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE ism.key = 'image_image_type' AND NOT m.status IN ('accepted', 'confirmed', 'reverted', 'error', 'failed', 'completed') GROUP BY m.status HAVING SUM(m.memory_processed) > 0
SELECT MIN(i.ID) AS min_instance_id, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) AS avg_memory_mb FROM INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON i.UUID = iic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME
SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.UUID, i.MEMORY_MB, m.STATUS, m.DEST_HOST, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY i.UUID ORDER BY m.DEST_HOST) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' ORDER BY m.DEST_HOST
SELECT m.STATUS AS migration_status, ins.VM_STATE AS instance_vm_state, COUNT(m.ID) AS migration_count, MIN(m.CREATED_AT) AS earliest_migration_created_at FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS ins ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = ins.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ins.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE ins.VCPUS >= 8 AND NOT m.STATUS IN ('accepted', 'confirmed', 'reverted', 'error', 'failed', 'completed') GROUP BY m.STATUS, ins.VM_STATE
SELECT COUNT(*) AS instance_count FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON i.UUID = iic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type'
SELECT MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) AS min_memory_mb, SUM(i.VCPUS) AS total_vcpus FROM INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON i.UUID = iic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'
SELECT COUNT(*) AS completed_migrations_for_shelved_instances_with_image_type_metadata FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded' AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'
SELECT m.status, EXP(AVG(LN(i.memory_mb))) AS geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE i.memory_mb > 0 AND NOT m.status IN ('accepted', 'confirmed', 'reverted', 'error', 'failed', 'completed') GROUP BY m.status ORDER BY geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC
SELECT COUNT(*) AS completed_migrations_for_shelved_instances FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'
SELECT i.VM_STATE, EXP(AVG(LN(m.MEMORY_TOTAL))) AS geometric_mean_memory_total FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.MEMORY_TOTAL > 0 AND i.DELETED = 0 AND NOT m.STATUS IN ('accepted', 'confirmed', 'reverted', 'error', 'failed', 'completed') GROUP BY i.VM_STATE ORDER BY geometric_mean_memory_total DESC
SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.FLAVOR AS flavor, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY i.MEMORY_MB ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'
SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, MIN(i.ID) AS min_instance_id, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) AS avg_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME
SELECT i.uuid, i.display_name, ism.key, ism.value, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY i.uuid ORDER BY i.uuid) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12'
SELECT i.vm_state, EXP(AVG(LN(i.memory_mb))) AS geometric_mean FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.memory_mb > 0 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.vm_state ORDER BY geometric_mean DESC
SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name, i.availability_zone, COUNT(ism.id) AS metadata_entry_count, SUM(i.vcpus) AS total_vcpus FROM instance_extra AS ie JOIN instances AS i ON ie.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, i.availability_zone
SELECT m.status AS migration_status, i.vm_state AS instance_vm_state, COUNT(m.id) AS migration_count, MIN(i.memory_mb) AS min_instance_memory_mb FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE ism.key = 'image_image_type' AND NOT m.status IN ('accepted', 'confirmed', 'reverted', 'error', 'failed', 'completed') GROUP BY m.status, i.vm_state
SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.HOSTNAME AS instance_hostname, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE AS migration_source_compute, m.DEST_COMPUTE AS migration_dest_compute, m.DEST_HOST AS migration_dest_host, m.MIGRATION_TYPE AS migration_type, m.MEMORY_TOTAL AS memory_total, m.MEMORY_PROCESSED AS memory_processed, m.MEMORY_REMAINING AS memory_remaining, MIN(m.MEMORY_REMAINING) OVER (PARTITION BY m.INSTANCE_UUID ORDER BY m.INSTANCE_UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'
SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name, i.key_name, COUNT(ism.id) AS metadata_entry_count, SUM(i.vcpus) AS total_vcpus FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, i.key_name
SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY i.MEMORY_MB ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'
SELECT t1.UUID AS instance_uuid, t2.VALUE AS metadata_value, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY t1.MEMORY_MB ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS t0 JOIN INSTANCES AS t1 ON t0.INSTANCE_UUID = t1.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS t2 ON t1.UUID = t2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE t1.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND t1.HOST = 'blaze8-12' AND t2.KEY = 'image_image_type'
SELECT i.vm_state, EXP(AVG(LN(i.memory_mb))) AS geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.memory_mb > 0 AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.vm_state ORDER BY geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC
SELECT i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, m.STATUS AS migration_status, COUNT(i.ID) AS instance_count, MIN(m.MEMORY_TOTAL) AS min_memory_total FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.HIDDEN = 0 AND NOT m.STATUS IN ('accepted', 'confirmed', 'reverted', 'error', 'failed', 'completed') GROUP BY i.VM_STATE, m.STATUS
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH InstanceDetails AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.POWER_STATE AS power_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, MAX(i.ID) OVER (ORDER BY i.ID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_instance_id_next_2 FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3'), TopCompletedMigrations AS (SELECT i.display_name, COUNT(m.id) AS completed_migrations FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND NOT i.key_name IS NULL GROUP BY i.display_name ORDER BY completed_migrations DESC LIMIT 10) SELECT id.instance_uuid, id.display_name, id.vm_state, id.power_state, id.memory_mb, id.vcpus, id.instance_extra_id, id.max_instance_id_next_2, tcm.completed_migrations, ROUND(CAST(tcm.completed_migrations AS REAL) / NULLIF(id.vcpus, 0), 2) AS completed_migrations_per_vcpu FROM InstanceDetails AS id JOIN TopCompletedMigrations AS tcm ON tcm.display_name = id.display_name ORDER BY completed_migrations_per_vcpu DESC, tcm.completed_migrations DESC), OverallAvg AS (SELECT AVG(CAST(inner_cte.completed_migrations * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(inner_cte.vcpus, 0)) AS avg_completed_migrations_per_vcpu FROM inner_cte) SELECT inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.power_state, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.vcpus, inner_cte.instance_extra_id, inner_cte.max_instance_id_next_2, inner_cte.completed_migrations, inner_cte.completed_migrations_per_vcpu FROM inner_cte WHERE (CAST(inner_cte.completed_migrations * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(inner_cte.vcpus, 0)) > (SELECT avg_completed_migrations_per_vcpu FROM OverallAvg) ORDER BY inner_cte.completed_migrations_per_vcpu DESC, inner_cte.completed_migrations DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH instance_zone_metadata_rank AS (SELECT t1.UUID AS instance_uuid, t2.VALUE AS metadata_value, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY t1.MEMORY_MB ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS t0 JOIN INSTANCES AS t1 ON t0.INSTANCE_UUID = t1.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS t2 ON t1.UUID = t2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE t1.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND t2.KEY = 'image_image_type'), completed_migrations_memory AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.HOSTNAME AS instance_hostname, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE AS migration_source_compute, m.DEST_COMPUTE AS migration_dest_compute, m.DEST_HOST AS migration_dest_host, m.MIGRATION_TYPE AS migration_type, m.MEMORY_TOTAL AS memory_total, m.MEMORY_PROCESSED AS memory_processed, m.MEMORY_REMAINING AS memory_remaining, MIN(m.MEMORY_REMAINING) OVER (PARTITION BY m.INSTANCE_UUID ORDER BY m.INSTANCE_UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed'), flare3_completed_migrations_with_image_type AS (SELECT cmm.instance_uuid, cmm.instance_display_name, cmm.instance_hostname, cmm.migration_source_compute, cmm.migration_dest_compute, cmm.migration_dest_host, cmm.migration_type, cmm.memory_total, cmm.memory_processed, cmm.memory_remaining, cmm.window_column AS min_memory_remaining_within_instance_uuid FROM completed_migrations_memory AS cmm JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON i.UUID = cmm.instance_uuid JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type') SELECT COUNT(*) AS flare3_completed_migrations_with_image_type_count FROM flare3_completed_migrations_with_image_type), flare3_migrations_detail AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.HOSTNAME AS instance_hostname, m.MIGRATION_TYPE AS migration_type, m.MEMORY_REMAINING AS memory_remaining FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND m.STATUS = 'completed' AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type'), flare3_avg_memory_remaining AS (SELECT AVG(fmd.memory_remaining * 1.0) AS avg_memory_remaining FROM flare3_migrations_detail AS fmd) SELECT fmd.instance_uuid, fmd.instance_display_name, fmd.instance_hostname, fmd.migration_type, fmd.memory_remaining FROM flare3_migrations_detail AS fmd WHERE fmd.memory_remaining > (SELECT avg_memory_remaining FROM flare3_avg_memory_remaining)
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH InstanceMetadataSummary AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name, i.key_name, COUNT(ism.id) AS metadata_entry_count, SUM(i.vcpus) AS total_vcpus FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3' GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, i.key_name), InstanceNetworkWindow AS (SELECT t1.DISPLAY_NAME, t1.UUID AS instance_uuid, t2.NETWORK_INFO, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY t1.DISPLAY_NAME ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS t0 JOIN INSTANCES AS t1 ON t0.INSTANCE_UUID = t1.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS t2 ON t1.UUID = t2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE t1.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3') SELECT ims.instance_uuid, ims.display_name, ims.key_name, ims.metadata_entry_count, ims.total_vcpus, inw.NETWORK_INFO, inw.window_column FROM InstanceMetadataSummary AS ims JOIN InstanceNetworkWindow AS inw ON ims.instance_uuid = inw.instance_uuid ORDER BY ims.display_name, ims.instance_uuid) SELECT key_name, MAX(total_vcpus) AS max_total_vcpus_per_instance_for_key FROM inner_cte GROUP BY key_name
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH ShelvedOffloadedInstancesWithMetadata AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.PROJECT_ID AS project_id, i.POWER_STATE AS power_state, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, MAX(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (ORDER BY i.UUID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_memory_mb_next_2 FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded'), ForgeInstancesWithNetworkInfo AS (SELECT i.UUID, i.HOSTNAME, i.VM_STATE, i.MEMORY_MB, c.NETWORK_INFO, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (ORDER BY i.ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS c ON i.UUID = c.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge') SELECT f.UUID AS instance_uuid, f.HOSTNAME AS hostname, s.display_name, s.project_id, s.power_state, s.metadata_key, s.metadata_value, f.VM_STATE AS vm_state, f.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, f.NETWORK_INFO AS network_info, s.max_memory_mb_next_2, f.window_column AS min_memory_mb_window_2_pre_2_fol FROM ForgeInstancesWithNetworkInfo AS f JOIN ShelvedOffloadedInstancesWithMetadata AS s ON s.instance_uuid = f.UUID ORDER BY f.UUID, s.metadata_key), InstanceVcpu AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.VCPUS AS vcpus FROM INSTANCES AS i), AverageVcpu AS (SELECT AVG(v.vcpus) AS avg_vcpus FROM InstanceVcpu AS v JOIN inner_cte AS ic ON ic.instance_uuid = v.instance_uuid) SELECT ic.instance_uuid, ic.hostname, ic.display_name, ic.project_id, ic.power_state, ic.metadata_key, ic.metadata_value, ic.vm_state, ic.memory_mb, ic.network_info, ic.max_memory_mb_next_2, ic.min_memory_mb_window_2_pre_2_fol, v.vcpus, (v.vcpus - av.avg_vcpus) AS deviation_from_avg_vcpus FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN InstanceVcpu AS v ON v.instance_uuid = ic.instance_uuid CROSS JOIN AverageVcpu AS av ORDER BY ic.instance_uuid, ic.metadata_key
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH top_metadata AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, COUNT(ism.ID) AS metadata_entries FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME ORDER BY metadata_entries DESC LIMIT 10), completed_migrations AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE AS instance_availability_zone, m.STATUS AS migration_status, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed') SELECT cm.migration_id, cm.instance_display_name, cm.instance_availability_zone, cm.migration_status, cm.window_column, tm.metadata_entries FROM completed_migrations AS cm JOIN top_metadata AS tm ON tm.DISPLAY_NAME = cm.instance_display_name ORDER BY tm.metadata_entries DESC, cm.migration_id) SELECT migration_id, instance_display_name, instance_availability_zone, migration_status, window_column, metadata_entries FROM inner_cte WHERE metadata_entries > (SELECT AVG(metadata_entries) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY metadata_entries DESC, migration_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH sq1 AS (SELECT i.UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.VM_STATE, i.MEMORY_MB, i.VCPUS, i.KEY_NAME, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY i.VCPUS ORDER BY i.VCPUS) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3'), sq2 AS (SELECT i.display_name, COUNT(m.id) AS completed_migrations FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND NOT i.key_name IS NULL GROUP BY i.display_name ORDER BY completed_migrations DESC LIMIT 10) SELECT sq2.display_name, sq2.completed_migrations, sq1.uuid, sq1.vm_state, sq1.memory_mb, sq1.vcpus, sq1.key_name, sq1.instance_extra_id, sq1.window_column AS min_memory_mb_in_vcpu_group FROM sq2 JOIN sq1 ON sq2.display_name = sq1.display_name ORDER BY sq2.completed_migrations DESC) SELECT inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.completed_migrations, inner_cte.uuid, inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.vcpus, inner_cte.key_name, inner_cte.instance_extra_id, inner_cte.min_memory_mb_in_vcpu_group, CASE WHEN EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM migrations AS m2 JOIN instances AS i2 ON m2.instance_uuid = i2.uuid WHERE i2.uuid = inner_cte.uuid AND m2.deleted = 0 AND NOT m2.status IN ('completed', 'failed', 'error')) THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_active_migrations FROM inner_cte ORDER BY inner_cte.completed_migrations DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH sq1 AS (SELECT i.display_name, i.availability_zone, i.vm_state, i.memory_mb, i.vcpus, i.uuid, MAX(ism.id) OVER (ORDER BY i.uuid ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_metadata_id_next_2 FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3'), sq2 AS (SELECT m.status, EXP(AVG(LN(i.memory_mb))) AS geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE i.memory_mb > 0 GROUP BY m.status ORDER BY geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC) SELECT sq1.display_name, sq1.availability_zone, sq1.vm_state, sq1.memory_mb, sq1.vcpus, sq1.uuid, sq1.max_metadata_id_next_2, sq2.status AS migration_status, sq2.geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM sq1 JOIN migrations AS m ON m.instance_uuid = sq1.uuid JOIN sq2 ON sq2.status = m.status JOIN instances AS i2 ON i2.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE i2.availability_zone = 'flare3' AND i2.memory_mb > 0 ORDER BY sq2.geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC, sq1.uuid) SELECT inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.availability_zone, inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.vcpus, inner_cte.uuid, inner_cte.max_metadata_id_next_2, inner_cte.migration_status, inner_cte.geometric_mean_memory_mb, m2.source_compute, m2.dest_compute, MAX(m2.id) OVER (ORDER BY inner_cte.uuid ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_migration_id_next_2 FROM inner_cte JOIN migrations AS m2 ON m2.instance_uuid = inner_cte.uuid JOIN instances AS i3 ON i3.uuid = inner_cte.uuid WHERE i3.availability_zone = 'flare3' AND i3.memory_mb > 0 ORDER BY inner_cte.geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC, inner_cte.uuid
