WITH inner_cte AS (WITH ShelvedOffloadedInstances AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.PROJECT_ID AS project_id, i.POWER_STATE AS power_state, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, MAX(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (ORDER BY i.UUID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_memory_mb_next_2 FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded'), CompletedMigrations AS (SELECT i.uuid, i.display_name, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, m.status, MIN(i.memory_mb) OVER (ORDER BY m.id ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN migrations AS m ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed') SELECT soi.instance_uuid, soi.display_name AS shelved_display_name, soi.project_id, soi.power_state, soi.metadata_key, soi.metadata_value, soi.max_memory_mb_next_2, cm.source_compute, cm.dest_compute, cm.status AS migration_status, cm.window_column AS min_memory_mb_window_3_preceding_1_following FROM ShelvedOffloadedInstances AS soi JOIN CompletedMigrations AS cm ON cm.uuid = soi.instance_uuid ORDER BY soi.instance_uuid) SELECT inner_cte.project_id, MAX(inner_cte.max_memory_mb_next_2) AS max_of_instance_max_memory_mb_next_2, MAX(inner_cte.min_memory_mb_window_3_preceding_1_following) AS max_of_migration_min_memory_mb_window FROM inner_cte GROUP BY inner_cte.project_id ORDER BY inner_cte.project_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH InstanceSystemMetadataRanks AS (SELECT i.UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.VM_STATE, ism.KEY, ism.VALUE, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0), ForgeInstanceMemoryWindows AS (SELECT i.UUID, i.HOSTNAME, i.VM_STATE, i.MEMORY_MB, c.NETWORK_INFO, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (ORDER BY i.ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS c ON i.UUID = c.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge') SELECT f.UUID, f.HOSTNAME, f.VM_STATE, f.MEMORY_MB, f.NETWORK_INFO, f.window_column AS Min_Memory_MB_Window_2_Preceding_2_Following, ism.KEY AS System_Metadata_Key, ism.VALUE AS System_Metadata_Value, ism.window_column AS Metadata_Rank_Within_Key_By_UUID FROM ForgeInstanceMemoryWindows AS f JOIN InstanceSystemMetadataRanks AS ism ON f.UUID = ism.UUID WHERE ism.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge' ORDER BY f.UUID, ism.KEY, ism.window_column) SELECT i.display_name AS display_name, SUM(i.vcpus) AS total_vcpus, SUM(i.memory_mb) AS total_memory_mb, CASE WHEN EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM inner_cte AS d JOIN instances AS i2 ON i2.uuid = d.uuid WHERE i2.display_name = i.display_name AND i2.deleted = 0) THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_any_instances_in_forge_detail_report FROM instances AS i WHERE i.deleted = 0 GROUP BY i.display_name ORDER BY total_vcpus DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH InstanceWindowExtras AS (SELECT i.uuid, i.display_name, i.vm_state, i.power_state, i.memory_mb, i.vcpus, MAX(ie.id) OVER (ORDER BY i.uuid ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_instance_extra_id_next2 FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0), AZInstanceNetworkWindow AS (SELECT t1.DISPLAY_NAME, t1.UUID AS instance_uuid, t2.NETWORK_INFO, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY t1.DISPLAY_NAME ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS t0 JOIN INSTANCES AS t1 ON t0.INSTANCE_UUID = t1.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS t2 ON t1.UUID = t2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE t1.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3') SELECT iwe.uuid AS instance_uuid, iwe.display_name, iwe.vm_state, iwe.power_state, iwe.memory_mb, iwe.vcpus, iwe.max_instance_extra_id_next2, az.NETWORK_INFO, az.window_column, ROUND(CAST(iwe.vcpus AS REAL) / NULLIF(iwe.memory_mb, 0), 6) AS vcpus_per_mb_ratio FROM InstanceWindowExtras AS iwe JOIN AZInstanceNetworkWindow AS az ON az.instance_uuid = iwe.uuid ORDER BY vcpus_per_mb_ratio DESC, iwe.uuid) SELECT inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.power_state, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.vcpus, inner_cte.max_instance_extra_id_next2, inner_cte.network_info, inner_cte.window_column, inner_cte.vcpus_per_mb_ratio FROM inner_cte WHERE inner_cte.vcpus_per_mb_ratio > 0.0005 ORDER BY inner_cte.vcpus_per_mb_ratio DESC, inner_cte.instance_uuid
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH VmStateGeometricMeanMemory AS (SELECT i.vm_state, EXP(AVG(LN(i.memory_mb))) AS geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.memory_mb > 0 AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.vm_state ORDER BY geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC), Flare3NonDeletedInstances AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.POWER_STATE AS power_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, MAX(i.ID) OVER (ORDER BY i.ID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_instance_id_next_2 FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3') SELECT f.instance_uuid, f.display_name, f.vm_state, f.power_state, f.memory_mb, f.vcpus, f.instance_extra_id, f.max_instance_id_next_2, v.geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM Flare3NonDeletedInstances AS f JOIN VmStateGeometricMeanMemory AS v ON f.vm_state = v.vm_state WHERE v.geometric_mean_memory_mb > 0 ORDER BY v.geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC, f.instance_uuid) SELECT i.vm_state, COUNT(i.instance_uuid) AS total_instances_in_flare3_vm_state, avg_counts.avg_instance_count_per_vm_state FROM inner_cte AS i, (SELECT AVG(vm_state_count) AS avg_instance_count_per_vm_state FROM (SELECT vm_state, COUNT(*) AS vm_state_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY vm_state) AS sub) AS avg_counts GROUP BY i.vm_state, avg_counts.avg_instance_count_per_vm_state ORDER BY total_instances_in_flare3_vm_state DESC, i.vm_state
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH NotDeletedInstancesWindow AS (SELECT t1.UUID, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY t1.MEMORY_MB ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS t0 JOIN INSTANCES AS t1 ON t0.INSTANCE_UUID = t1.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS t2 ON t1.UUID = t2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE t1.DELETED = 0), ForgeInstancesWindow AS (SELECT i.uuid, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY i.memory_mb ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS ic ON i.uuid = ic.instance_uuid WHERE i.display_name = 'forge') SELECT COUNT(*) AS Total_Not_Deleted_Instances, COUNT(f.uuid) AS Total_Forge_Instances, ROUND(CAST(COUNT(f.uuid) AS REAL) / NULLIF(COUNT(*), 0), 2) AS Forge_Share_Of_Not_Deleted FROM NotDeletedInstancesWindow AS nd LEFT JOIN ForgeInstancesWindow AS f ON nd.UUID = f.uuid), ZoneAverages AS (SELECT i.availability_zone, AVG(i.memory_mb * 1.0) AS avg_zone_memory_mb FROM instances AS i WHERE i.deleted = 0 GROUP BY i.availability_zone) SELECT i.uuid, i.display_name, i.availability_zone, i.memory_mb, za.avg_zone_memory_mb FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON ie.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS ic ON ic.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN ZoneAverages AS za ON za.availability_zone = i.availability_zone WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND i.memory_mb > (SELECT avg_zone_memory_mb FROM ZoneAverages WHERE ZoneAverages.availability_zone = i.availability_zone)
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH InstanceStateStats AS (SELECT i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME, COUNT(i.ID) AS instance_count, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) AS min_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME), CompletedMigrations AS (SELECT m.status, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, i.display_name, ie.flavor, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.status ORDER BY m.status) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed') SELECT cm.status AS migration_status, cm.source_compute, cm.dest_compute, cm.display_name, cm.flavor, cm.window_column, iss.VM_STATE, iss.instance_count, iss.min_memory_mb FROM CompletedMigrations AS cm JOIN InstanceStateStats AS iss ON cm.display_name = iss.DISPLAY_NAME) SELECT inner_cte.migration_status, inner_cte.source_compute, inner_cte.dest_compute, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.flavor, inner_cte.window_column, inner_cte.VM_STATE, inner_cte.instance_count, inner_cte.min_memory_mb, avg_min_mem.avg_min_memory_mb_across_display_names FROM inner_cte, (SELECT AVG(min_memory_mb) AS avg_min_memory_mb_across_display_names FROM (SELECT MIN(min_memory_mb) AS min_memory_mb FROM inner_cte GROUP BY display_name) AS per_display_name) AS avg_min_mem
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH HighMemoryByMetadata AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, MAX(i.MEMORY_MB) AS max_memory_mb, i.LAUNCHED_AT FROM INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON i.UUID = iic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME, ism.KEY, i.LAUNCHED_AT HAVING MAX(i.MEMORY_MB) > 16000), TopCacheRecordInstances AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, COUNT(iic.ID) AS cache_records FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON i.UUID = iic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME ORDER BY cache_records DESC LIMIT 10) SELECT hmbm.DISPLAY_NAME, hmbm.metadata_key, hmbm.max_memory_mb, hmbm.LAUNCHED_AT, tcri.cache_records FROM HighMemoryByMetadata AS hmbm JOIN TopCacheRecordInstances AS tcri ON hmbm.DISPLAY_NAME = tcri.DISPLAY_NAME ORDER BY tcri.cache_records DESC, hmbm.max_memory_mb DESC) SELECT DISPLAY_NAME, metadata_key, max_memory_mb, LAUNCHED_AT, cache_records FROM inner_cte WHERE max_memory_mb > (SELECT AVG(max_memory_mb) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY cache_records DESC, max_memory_mb DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH ForgeInstances AS (SELECT i.ID AS instance_id, i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.USER_ID, i.PROJECT_ID, i.HOSTNAME, i.VM_STATE, i.POWER_STATE, i.MEMORY_MB, i.VCPUS, i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, c.ID AS info_cache_id, c.NETWORK_INFO, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY i.PROJECT_ID ORDER BY i.PROJECT_ID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS c ON i.UUID = c.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge'), MigrationStats AS (SELECT m.status AS migration_status, i.vm_state AS instance_vm_state, COUNT(m.id) AS migration_count, MIN(i.memory_mb) AS min_instance_memory_mb FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE ism.key = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY m.status, i.vm_state), Combined AS (SELECT fi.instance_id, fi.instance_uuid, fi.user_id, fi.project_id, fi.hostname, fi.vm_state, fi.power_state, fi.memory_mb, fi.vcpus, fi.availability_zone, fi.info_cache_id, fi.network_info, fi.window_column, ms.migration_status, ms.migration_count, ms.min_instance_memory_mb FROM ForgeInstances AS fi LEFT JOIN MigrationStats AS ms ON fi.vm_state = ms.instance_vm_state) SELECT * FROM Combined ORDER BY instance_id, migration_status), AverageMemory AS (SELECT AVG(memory_mb) AS avg_memory_mb FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.instance_id, ic.instance_uuid, ic.user_id, ic.project_id, ic.hostname, ic.vm_state, ic.power_state, ic.memory_mb, ic.vcpus, ic.availability_zone, ic.info_cache_id, ic.network_info, ic.window_column, ic.migration_status, ic.migration_count, ic.min_instance_memory_mb, (ic.memory_mb - am.avg_memory_mb) AS deviation_from_avg_memory_mb FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageMemory AS am ORDER BY ic.instance_id, ic.migration_status
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH MetadataStats AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name, i.key_name, COUNT(ism.id) AS metadata_entry_count, SUM(i.vcpus) AS total_vcpus FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3' GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, i.key_name), NetworkWindow AS (SELECT t1.DISPLAY_NAME, t1.UUID AS instance_uuid, t2.NETWORK_INFO, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY t1.DISPLAY_NAME ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS t0 JOIN INSTANCES AS t1 ON t0.INSTANCE_UUID = t1.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS t2 ON t1.UUID = t2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE t1.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3'), CombinedInstanceDetails AS (SELECT ms.instance_uuid, ms.display_name, ms.key_name, ms.metadata_entry_count, ms.total_vcpus, nw.NETWORK_INFO, nw.window_column FROM MetadataStats AS ms JOIN NetworkWindow AS nw ON ms.instance_uuid = nw.instance_uuid) SELECT * FROM CombinedInstanceDetails ORDER BY display_name, instance_uuid) SELECT instance_uuid, display_name, key_name, metadata_entry_count, total_vcpus, network_info, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE metadata_entry_count > (SELECT AVG(metadata_entry_count) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY display_name, instance_uuid
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH ShelvedOffloadedNonDeletedInstances AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.POWER_STATE AS power_state, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, iic.NETWORK_INFO AS network_info, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded'), MaxMetadataIdNext2InFlare3 AS (SELECT i.display_name, i.availability_zone, i.vm_state, i.memory_mb, i.vcpus, i.uuid, MAX(ism.id) OVER (ORDER BY i.uuid ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_metadata_id_next_2 FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3') SELECT s.instance_uuid, s.display_name, m.availability_zone, m.vm_state, s.power_state, m.memory_mb, m.vcpus, s.metadata_key, s.metadata_value, s.network_info, s.window_column, m.max_metadata_id_next_2 FROM ShelvedOffloadedNonDeletedInstances AS s JOIN MaxMetadataIdNext2InFlare3 AS m ON m.uuid = s.instance_uuid) SELECT inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.availability_zone, inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.power_state, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.vcpus, inner_cte.metadata_key, inner_cte.metadata_value, inner_cte.network_info, inner_cte.window_column, inner_cte.max_metadata_id_next_2 FROM inner_cte WHERE (CAST(inner_cte.memory_mb * 1.0 AS REAL) / (inner_cte.memory_mb + inner_cte.vcpus * 1024)) > 0.75
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH MaxMemoryByInstanceStatus AS (SELECT i.display_name, MAX(m.memory_total) AS max_memory_total, m.status FROM instances AS i JOIN migrations AS m ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' GROUP BY i.display_name, m.status HAVING MAX(m.memory_total) > 0), CompletedMigrationsRanked AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic ON i.UUID = ic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND ic.DELETED = 0) SELECT cmr.migration_id, cmr.SOURCE_COMPUTE, cmr.DEST_COMPUTE, cmr.instance_display_name, mm.max_memory_total, cmr.window_column AS migration_rank_within_completed_status, ROUND(CAST(mm.max_memory_total AS REAL) / NULLIF(cmr.window_column, 0), 2) AS max_memory_per_rank_ratio FROM CompletedMigrationsRanked AS cmr JOIN MaxMemoryByInstanceStatus AS mm ON cmr.instance_display_name = mm.display_name ORDER BY max_memory_per_rank_ratio DESC, cmr.migration_id), project_rollup AS (SELECT i.project_id, SUM(inner_cte.max_memory_per_rank_ratio) AS total_max_memory_per_rank_ratio, COUNT(DISTINCT i.uuid) AS distinct_instances_contributing FROM inner_cte JOIN instances AS i ON i.display_name = inner_cte.instance_display_name AND i.deleted = 0 GROUP BY i.project_id), ranked_projects AS (SELECT pr.project_id, pr.total_max_memory_per_rank_ratio, pr.distinct_instances_contributing, RANK() OVER (ORDER BY pr.total_max_memory_per_rank_ratio DESC) AS project_rank FROM project_rollup AS pr) SELECT rp.project_id, rp.total_max_memory_per_rank_ratio, rp.distinct_instances_contributing, CASE WHEN EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM migrations AS m2 JOIN instances AS i2 ON m2.instance_uuid = i2.uuid WHERE i2.project_id = rp.project_id AND m2.deleted = 0 AND NOT m2.status IN ('completed', 'failed', 'error')) THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_active_migrations FROM ranked_projects AS rp WHERE rp.project_rank <= 10 ORDER BY rp.total_max_memory_per_rank_ratio DESC, rp.project_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH MigrationMetadata AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, SUM(m.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed'), InstanceMetadataDetails AS (SELECT i.ID AS instance_id, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.USER_ID AS user_id, i.PROJECT_ID AS project_id, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.POWER_STATE AS power_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE AS availability_zone, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, ism.ID AS metadata_id, MIN(ism.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY ism.KEY) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.UUID = '0003c04d-211b-4847-8916-102efabca5bc') SELECT imd.instance_id, imd.display_name, imd.user_id, imd.project_id, imd.vm_state, imd.power_state, imd.memory_mb, imd.vcpus, imd.availability_zone, imd.metadata_key, imd.metadata_value, imd.metadata_id, imd.window_column AS min_metadata_id_in_key_group, mm.migration_id, mm.instance_display_name AS migration_instance_display_name, mm.window_column AS running_sum_migration_ids_in_key_group, ROUND(CAST(mm.window_column AS REAL) / NULLIF(imd.window_column, 0), 2) AS running_sum_to_min_metadata_id_ratio FROM InstanceMetadataDetails AS imd JOIN MigrationMetadata AS mm ON mm.metadata_key = imd.metadata_key AND mm.instance_display_name = imd.display_name ORDER BY running_sum_to_min_metadata_id_ratio DESC, mm.migration_id), AverageRatio AS (SELECT AVG(running_sum_to_min_metadata_id_ratio) AS avg_ratio FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.instance_id, ic.display_name, ic.user_id, ic.project_id, ic.vm_state, ic.power_state, ic.memory_mb, ic.vcpus, ic.availability_zone, ic.metadata_key, ic.metadata_value, ic.metadata_id, ic.min_metadata_id_in_key_group, ic.migration_id, ic.migration_instance_display_name, ic.running_sum_migration_ids_in_key_group, ic.running_sum_to_min_metadata_id_ratio, ar.avg_ratio AS benchmark_avg_ratio, CASE WHEN ic.running_sum_to_min_metadata_id_ratio > ar.avg_ratio * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_RATIO' WHEN ic.running_sum_to_min_metadata_id_ratio < ar.avg_ratio * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_RATIO' ELSE 'AVERAGE_RATIO' END AS ratio_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageRatio AS ar ORDER BY ratio_category, ic.running_sum_to_min_metadata_id_ratio DESC, ic.migration_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH vm_state_geom_mean_migrated_mem AS (SELECT i.vm_state, EXP(AVG(LN(m.memory_total))) AS geometric_mean_memory_total FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON i.uuid = c.instance_uuid WHERE m.memory_total > 0 AND c.network_info <> '' GROUP BY i.vm_state ORDER BY geometric_mean_memory_total DESC), flare3_instances_ranked_by_mem AS (SELECT i.UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.VM_STATE, i.MEMORY_MB, c.NETWORK_INFO, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY i.VM_STATE ORDER BY i.MEMORY_MB DESC) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS c ON i.UUID = c.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3') SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT fir.UUID) AS flare3_instance_count_with_geom_mean_migration_mem, AVG(vsgmmm.geometric_mean_memory_total) AS avg_geometric_mean_memory_total_across_matching_vm_states FROM flare3_instances_ranked_by_mem AS fir JOIN vm_state_geom_mean_migrated_mem AS vsgmmm ON fir.VM_STATE = vsgmmm.vm_state WHERE fir.NETWORK_INFO <> ''), flare3_matching_instances AS (SELECT i.project_id, i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.vcpus, i.memory_mb, i.vm_state, vsgmmm.geometric_mean_memory_total FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON c.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN (SELECT i2.vm_state, EXP(AVG(LN(m.memory_total))) AS geometric_mean_memory_total FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i2 ON m.instance_uuid = i2.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS c2 ON i2.uuid = c2.instance_uuid WHERE m.memory_total > 0 AND c2.network_info <> '' GROUP BY i2.vm_state) AS vsgmmm ON vsgmmm.vm_state = i.vm_state WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3' AND c.network_info <> '' AND i.deleted = 0) SELECT fmi.project_id, SUM(fmi.vcpus) AS total_vcpus, SUM(fmi.memory_mb) AS total_memory, CASE WHEN EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i2 ON m.instance_uuid = i2.uuid WHERE i2.project_id = fmi.project_id AND m.deleted = 0 AND NOT m.status IN ('completed', 'failed', 'error')) THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_active_migrations, AVG(fmi.geometric_mean_memory_total) AS avg_geom_mean_migrated_memory_total_across_matching_vm_states, inner_cte.flare3_instance_count_with_geom_mean_migration_mem AS flare3_instance_count_with_geom_mean_migration_mem_overall, inner_cte.avg_geometric_mean_memory_total_across_matching_vm_states AS avg_geometric_mean_memory_total_across_matching_vm_states_overall FROM flare3_matching_instances AS fmi CROSS JOIN inner_cte GROUP BY fmi.project_id, inner_cte.flare3_instance_count_with_geom_mean_migration_mem, inner_cte.avg_geometric_mean_memory_total_across_matching_vm_states ORDER BY total_vcpus DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH InstanceStateAZStats AS (SELECT ins.VM_STATE, ins.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, COUNT(ins.UUID) AS instance_count, MIN(ins.MEMORY_MB) AS min_memory_mb FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS ins ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = ins.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ins.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY ins.VM_STATE, ins.AVAILABILITY_ZONE), CompletedMigrationsWithMetadata AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.STATUS AS migration_status, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, m.DEST_HOST, m.INSTANCE_UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.VM_STATE AS instance_vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS instance_memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS instance_vcpus, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, m.MEMORY_REMAINING AS migration_memory_remaining, MIN(m.MEMORY_REMAINING) OVER (PARTITION BY m.DEST_COMPUTE ORDER BY m.DEST_COMPUTE) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed'), CompletedMigrationCountsByStateAZ AS (SELECT i.VM_STATE, i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, COUNT(m.ID) AS completed_migration_count FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.VM_STATE, i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE) SELECT isaz.VM_STATE, isaz.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, isaz.instance_count, isaz.min_memory_mb, cmc.completed_migration_count FROM InstanceStateAZStats AS isaz JOIN CompletedMigrationCountsByStateAZ AS cmc ON isaz.VM_STATE = cmc.VM_STATE AND isaz.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = cmc.AVAILABILITY_ZONE WHERE (CAST(cmc.completed_migration_count * 1.0 AS REAL) / isaz.instance_count) > 0.75), avg_ratio AS (SELECT AVG(CAST(completed_migration_count * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(instance_count, 0)) AS avg_completed_migration_ratio FROM inner_cte) SELECT inner_cte.VM_STATE, inner_cte.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, inner_cte.instance_count, inner_cte.min_memory_mb, inner_cte.completed_migration_count, avg_ratio.avg_completed_migration_ratio FROM inner_cte CROSS JOIN avg_ratio
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH MaxMemoryByInstanceStatus AS (SELECT i.display_name, MAX(m.memory_total) AS max_memory_total, m.status FROM instances AS i JOIN migrations AS m ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' GROUP BY i.display_name, m.status HAVING MAX(m.memory_total) > 0), CompletedMigrationsRanked AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic ON i.UUID = ic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND ic.DELETED = 0) SELECT cmr.migration_id, cmr.SOURCE_COMPUTE, cmr.DEST_COMPUTE, cmr.instance_display_name, mm.max_memory_total, cmr.window_column AS migration_rank_within_completed_status, ROUND(CAST(mm.max_memory_total AS REAL) / NULLIF(cmr.window_column, 0), 2) AS max_memory_per_rank_ratio FROM CompletedMigrationsRanked AS cmr JOIN MaxMemoryByInstanceStatus AS mm ON cmr.instance_display_name = mm.display_name ORDER BY max_memory_per_rank_ratio DESC, cmr.migration_id) SELECT inner_cte.migration_id, inner_cte.SOURCE_COMPUTE, inner_cte.DEST_COMPUTE, inner_cte.instance_display_name, inner_cte.max_memory_total, inner_cte.migration_rank_within_completed_status, inner_cte.max_memory_per_rank_ratio FROM inner_cte WHERE (inner_cte.max_memory_per_rank_ratio * 1.0) > 0.75 ORDER BY inner_cte.max_memory_per_rank_ratio DESC, inner_cte.migration_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH ForgeInstanceMetadata AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.HOSTNAME AS hostname, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, SUM(i.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY i.ID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge'), Flare3InstanceDetails AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY i.VM_STATE ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3') SELECT fim.instance_uuid, fim.hostname, fim.metadata_key, fim.window_column AS running_sum_instance_ids_by_metadata_key, fid.display_name, fid.vm_state, fid.memory_mb, fid.vcpus, fid.instance_extra_id, fid.window_column AS row_number_within_vm_state FROM ForgeInstanceMetadata AS fim JOIN Flare3InstanceDetails AS fid ON fim.instance_uuid = fid.instance_uuid ORDER BY fim.metadata_key, fim.instance_uuid) SELECT inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.hostname, inner_cte.metadata_key, inner_cte.running_sum_instance_ids_by_metadata_key, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.vcpus, inner_cte.instance_extra_id, inner_cte.row_number_within_vm_state, COUNT(inner_cte.instance_uuid) OVER (PARTITION BY inner_cte.metadata_key) AS total_instances_for_metadata_key, avg_keys.avg_instance_count AS avg_instances_per_metadata_key FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(instance_count) AS avg_instance_count FROM (SELECT COUNT(*) AS instance_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY metadata_key) AS sub) AS avg_keys ON 1 = 1 ORDER BY inner_cte.metadata_key, inner_cte.instance_uuid
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH MigrationCompletedFlare3 AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, m.STATUS AS migration_status, SUM(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY m.MIGRATION_TYPE ORDER BY i.MEMORY_MB) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND m.STATUS = 'completed'), ShelvedOffloadedInstances AS (SELECT i.ID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, c.NETWORK_INFO, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE ORDER BY i.ID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS c ON i.UUID = c.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded') SELECT mcf.instance_uuid, mcf.instance_display_name, mcf.migration_status, mcf.window_column AS migration_type_memory_running_sum, soi.ID AS instance_id, soi.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, soi.NETWORK_INFO, soi.window_column AS az_instance_id_rank FROM MigrationCompletedFlare3 AS mcf JOIN ShelvedOffloadedInstances AS soi ON soi.DISPLAY_NAME = mcf.instance_display_name WHERE soi.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' ORDER BY soi.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, soi.ID, mcf.instance_uuid) SELECT i.instance_uuid, i.instance_display_name, i.migration_status, i.migration_type_memory_running_sum, i.instance_id, i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, i.NETWORK_INFO, i.az_instance_id_rank, avg_stats.avg_running_sum AS avg_migration_type_memory_running_sum_across_matches FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN (SELECT AVG(migration_type_memory_running_sum) AS avg_running_sum FROM inner_cte) AS avg_stats ON 1 = 1 ORDER BY i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, i.instance_id, i.instance_uuid
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH ForgeInstancesWindow AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.HOSTNAME AS hostname, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (ORDER BY ie.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge'), CompletedMigrationsWindow AS (SELECT t0.STATUS AS migration_status, t1.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, t2.FLAVOR AS instance_flavor_json, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY t1.DISPLAY_NAME ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS t0 JOIN INSTANCES AS t1 ON t0.INSTANCE_UUID = t1.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS t2 ON t1.UUID = t2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE t0.STATUS = 'completed'), ForgeCompletedMigrationCounts AS (SELECT m.INSTANCE_UUID AS instance_uuid, COUNT(*) AS completed_migration_count FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge' GROUP BY m.INSTANCE_UUID) SELECT fiw.instance_uuid, fiw.hostname, fiw.vm_state, fiw.memory_mb, fiw.instance_extra_id, fiw.window_column AS forge_min_memory_mb_window, cmw.migration_status, cmw.instance_flavor_json, cmw.window_column AS completed_migrations_row_number_window, fcmc.completed_migration_count FROM ForgeInstancesWindow AS fiw JOIN ForgeCompletedMigrationCounts AS fcmc ON fcmc.instance_uuid = fiw.instance_uuid JOIN CompletedMigrationsWindow AS cmw ON cmw.instance_display_name = 'forge' WHERE fcmc.completed_migration_count >= 1 ORDER BY fiw.instance_extra_id) SELECT inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.hostname, inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.instance_extra_id, inner_cte.forge_min_memory_mb_window, inner_cte.migration_status, inner_cte.instance_flavor_json, inner_cte.completed_migrations_row_number_window, inner_cte.completed_migration_count, AVG(CASE WHEN NOT m.memory_total IS NULL AND m.memory_total <> 0 THEN (CAST(m.memory_remaining * 1.0 AS REAL) / m.memory_total) ELSE NULL END) AS completed_migrations_memory_remaining_pct FROM inner_cte JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON m.instance_uuid = inner_cte.instance_uuid AND m.status = 'completed' JOIN INSTANCES AS i2 ON i2.uuid = m.instance_uuid AND i2.display_name = 'forge' GROUP BY inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.hostname, inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.instance_extra_id, inner_cte.forge_min_memory_mb_window, inner_cte.migration_status, inner_cte.instance_flavor_json, inner_cte.completed_migrations_row_number_window, inner_cte.completed_migration_count ORDER BY inner_cte.instance_extra_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH CompletedMigrationsWithMetadata AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.STATUS AS migration_status, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, m.DEST_HOST, m.INSTANCE_UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.VM_STATE AS instance_vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS instance_memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS instance_vcpus, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, m.MEMORY_REMAINING AS migration_memory_remaining, MIN(m.MEMORY_REMAINING) OVER (PARTITION BY m.DEST_COMPUTE ORDER BY m.DEST_COMPUTE) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed'), ShelvedOffloadedHighMemoryDisplayNames AS (SELECT i.display_name, SUM(i.memory_mb) AS total_memory_mb FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.vm_state = 'shelved_offloaded' AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.display_name HAVING SUM(i.memory_mb) > 20000) SELECT cmwm.DEST_COMPUTE, COUNT(DISTINCT cmwm.migration_id) AS completed_migration_count, MIN(cmwm.window_column) AS min_migration_memory_remaining_in_dest_compute, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT sohdn.display_name) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS Has_Shelved_Offloaded_High_Memory_DisplayName FROM CompletedMigrationsWithMetadata AS cmwm LEFT JOIN ShelvedOffloadedHighMemoryDisplayNames AS sohdn ON cmwm.instance_display_name = sohdn.display_name GROUP BY cmwm.DEST_COMPUTE HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT cmwm.migration_id) >= 2 ORDER BY completed_migration_count DESC, cmwm.DEST_COMPUTE), DestComputeAgg AS (SELECT DEST_COMPUTE, completed_migration_count, min_migration_memory_remaining_in_dest_compute, Has_Shelved_Offloaded_High_Memory_DisplayName FROM inner_cte) SELECT DEST_COMPUTE, completed_migration_count, min_migration_memory_remaining_in_dest_compute, Has_Shelved_Offloaded_High_Memory_DisplayName FROM DestComputeAgg WHERE completed_migration_count > (SELECT AVG(completed_migration_count) FROM DestComputeAgg) ORDER BY completed_migration_count DESC, DEST_COMPUTE
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH ForgeInstanceMetadata AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, ism.key AS metadata_key, ism.value AS metadata_value, i.power_state, SUM(i.id) OVER (PARTITION BY i.power_state ORDER BY i.id) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ism.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON i.uuid = iic.instance_uuid WHERE i.display_name = 'forge'), CompletedMigrations AS (SELECT i.uuid, i.display_name, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, m.status, MIN(i.memory_mb) OVER (ORDER BY m.id ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN migrations AS m ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed') SELECT fim.instance_uuid, fim.metadata_key, fim.metadata_value, fim.power_state, fim.window_column AS running_sum_instance_ids_within_power_state, cm.source_compute, cm.dest_compute, cm.status AS migration_status, cm.window_column AS min_instance_memory_mb_window FROM ForgeInstanceMetadata AS fim JOIN CompletedMigrations AS cm ON cm.uuid = fim.instance_uuid WHERE cm.display_name = 'forge' ORDER BY fim.instance_uuid, fim.metadata_key), avg_window_mem AS (SELECT AVG(min_instance_memory_mb_window) AS avg_min_instance_memory_mb_window FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.instance_uuid, ic.metadata_key, ic.metadata_value, ic.power_state, ic.running_sum_instance_ids_within_power_state, ic.source_compute, ic.dest_compute, ic.migration_status, ic.min_instance_memory_mb_window, (ic.min_instance_memory_mb_window - awm.avg_min_instance_memory_mb_window) AS deviation_from_avg_min_instance_memory_mb_window FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_window_mem AS awm ORDER BY ic.instance_uuid, ic.metadata_key
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH MetaKeyDenseRank AS (SELECT i.UUID, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY ism.KEY ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge'), MemoryDenseRank AS (SELECT i.uuid, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY i.memory_mb ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS ic ON i.uuid = ic.instance_uuid WHERE i.display_name = 'forge') SELECT m.UUID AS instance_uuid, m.window_column AS dense_rank_by_system_metadata_key, r.window_column AS dense_rank_by_memory_mb FROM MetaKeyDenseRank AS m JOIN MemoryDenseRank AS r ON m.UUID = r.uuid), AvgMetaKeyRank AS (SELECT AVG(inner_cte.dense_rank_by_system_metadata_key * 1.0) AS avg_dense_rank_by_system_metadata_key FROM inner_cte) SELECT inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.dense_rank_by_system_metadata_key, inner_cte.dense_rank_by_memory_mb FROM inner_cte WHERE inner_cte.dense_rank_by_system_metadata_key > (SELECT avg_dense_rank_by_system_metadata_key FROM AvgMetaKeyRank)
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH CompletedMigrationsByInstance AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name AS instance_display_name, i.availability_zone AS availability_zone, COUNT(m.id) AS completed_migration_count, SUM(m.memory_processed) AS total_memory_processed FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND m.memory_processed > 0 GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, i.availability_zone), AZFlare3InstanceNetworkRank AS (SELECT i.UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.VM_STATE, i.MEMORY_MB, c.NETWORK_INFO, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY i.VM_STATE ORDER BY i.MEMORY_MB DESC) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS c ON i.UUID = c.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3') SELECT az.UUID AS instance_uuid, az.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, az.VM_STATE, az.MEMORY_MB, az.NETWORK_INFO, az.window_column AS vm_state_memory_rank, cm.completed_migration_count, cm.total_memory_processed FROM AZFlare3InstanceNetworkRank AS az JOIN CompletedMigrationsByInstance AS cm ON az.UUID = cm.instance_uuid WHERE cm.availability_zone = 'flare3') SELECT inner_cte.VM_STATE AS vm_state, MAX(inner_cte.total_memory_processed) AS max_total_memory_processed_bytes FROM inner_cte GROUP BY inner_cte.VM_STATE
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH ForgeInstances AS (SELECT i.ID AS instance_id, i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.USER_ID, i.PROJECT_ID, i.HOSTNAME, i.VM_STATE, i.POWER_STATE, i.MEMORY_MB, i.VCPUS, i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, c.ID AS info_cache_id, c.NETWORK_INFO, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY i.PROJECT_ID ORDER BY i.PROJECT_ID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS c ON i.UUID = c.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge'), MigrationStats AS (SELECT m.status AS migration_status, i.vm_state AS instance_vm_state, COUNT(m.id) AS migration_count, MIN(i.memory_mb) AS min_instance_memory_mb FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE ism.key = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY m.status, i.vm_state), Combined AS (SELECT fi.instance_id, fi.instance_uuid, fi.user_id, fi.project_id, fi.hostname, fi.vm_state, fi.power_state, fi.memory_mb, fi.vcpus, fi.availability_zone, fi.info_cache_id, fi.network_info, fi.window_column, ms.migration_status, ms.migration_count, ms.min_instance_memory_mb FROM ForgeInstances AS fi LEFT JOIN MigrationStats AS ms ON fi.vm_state = ms.instance_vm_state) SELECT * FROM Combined ORDER BY instance_id, migration_status), ProjectVmStateTotals AS (SELECT ic.project_id, ic.vm_state, SUM(ic.vcpus) AS total_vcpus_in_vm_state FROM inner_cte AS ic GROUP BY ic.project_id, ic.vm_state) SELECT project_id, MAX(total_vcpus_in_vm_state) AS max_total_vcpus_by_vm_state FROM ProjectVmStateTotals GROUP BY project_id ORDER BY project_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH CompletedMigrationsWithMetadata AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ism.KEY ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed'), Flare3InstancesMetadataRunningSum AS (SELECT i.UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, ism.KEY, SUM(i.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY i.ID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3') SELECT c.migration_id, f.UUID AS instance_uuid, c.instance_display_name, c.metadata_key, c.metadata_value, c.window_column AS completed_migrations_row_number_window, f.window_column AS flare3_instance_id_running_sum_window FROM CompletedMigrationsWithMetadata AS c JOIN Flare3InstancesMetadataRunningSum AS f ON c.instance_display_name = f.DISPLAY_NAME AND c.metadata_key = f.KEY ORDER BY c.migration_id, c.metadata_key) SELECT migration_id, MAX(flare3_instance_id_running_sum_window) AS max_flare3_instance_id_running_sum_window_per_migration FROM inner_cte GROUP BY migration_id ORDER BY migration_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH ImageTypeMetadataInstances AS (SELECT t1.DISPLAY_NAME, t0.VALUE AS metadata_value, t2.NETWORK_INFO, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY t0.VALUE ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS t0 JOIN INSTANCES AS t1 ON t0.INSTANCE_UUID = t1.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS t2 ON t1.UUID = t2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE t0.KEY = 'image_image_type'), ForgeInstanceDetails AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.HOSTNAME AS hostname, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (ORDER BY ie.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge') SELECT f.instance_uuid, f.hostname, f.vm_state, f.memory_mb, f.instance_extra_id, f.window_column AS forge_min_memory_window, im.metadata_value, im.NETWORK_INFO, im.window_column AS image_type_row_number_window FROM ForgeInstanceDetails AS f JOIN ImageTypeMetadataInstances AS im ON im.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge' ORDER BY f.instance_extra_id, f.instance_uuid), ForgeAvgMemory AS (SELECT AVG(i.MEMORY_MB * 1.0) AS avg_forge_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge') SELECT ic.instance_uuid, ic.hostname, ic.vm_state, ic.memory_mb, ic.instance_extra_id, ic.forge_min_memory_window, ic.metadata_value, ic.NETWORK_INFO, ic.image_type_row_number_window FROM inner_cte AS ic WHERE ic.memory_mb > (SELECT avg_forge_memory_mb FROM ForgeAvgMemory) ORDER BY ic.instance_extra_id, ic.instance_uuid
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH VMStateMemory AS (SELECT i.VM_STATE, SUM(i.MEMORY_MB) AS total_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic ON i.UUID = ic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' GROUP BY i.VM_STATE HAVING SUM(i.MEMORY_MB) > 50000), InstanceMetadataWindow AS (SELECT i.display_name, i.availability_zone, i.vm_state, i.memory_mb, i.vcpus, i.uuid, MAX(ism.id) OVER (ORDER BY i.uuid ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_metadata_id_next_2 FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3') SELECT imw.display_name, imw.availability_zone, imw.vm_state, vsm.total_memory_mb, imw.memory_mb, imw.vcpus, imw.uuid, imw.max_metadata_id_next_2 FROM InstanceMetadataWindow AS imw JOIN VMStateMemory AS vsm ON imw.vm_state = vsm.VM_STATE) SELECT DISTINCT inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.availability_zone, inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.total_memory_mb, avg_totals.avg_total_memory_mb_across_states, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.vcpus, inner_cte.uuid, inner_cte.max_metadata_id_next_2, ic.id AS instance_info_cache_id FROM inner_cte JOIN instances AS i ON i.uuid = inner_cte.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS ic ON ic.instance_uuid = i.uuid, (SELECT AVG(state_totals.total_memory_mb) AS avg_total_memory_mb_across_states FROM (SELECT vm_state, total_memory_mb FROM inner_cte GROUP BY vm_state, total_memory_mb) AS state_totals) AS avg_totals
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH q1 AS (SELECT i.id AS instance_id, i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.user_id, i.project_id, i.display_name, i.vm_state, i.memory_mb, i.vcpus, c.id AS info_cache_id, MAX(i.id) OVER (ORDER BY i.id ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_instance_id_next_2 FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON i.uuid = c.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND c.deleted = 0), q2 AS (SELECT i.uuid, i.display_name, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, m.status, MIN(i.memory_mb) OVER (ORDER BY m.id ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN migrations AS m ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed') SELECT q1.instance_id, q1.instance_uuid, q1.user_id, q1.project_id, q1.display_name, q1.vm_state, q1.memory_mb, q1.vcpus, q1.info_cache_id, q1.max_instance_id_next_2, q2.source_compute, q2.dest_compute, q2.status AS migration_status, q2.window_column AS min_instance_memory_mb_window FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.instance_uuid = q2.uuid ORDER BY q1.instance_id) SELECT inner_cte.project_id, MAX(inner_cte.vcpus) AS max_vcpus_in_project, COUNT(DISTINCT inner_cte.instance_uuid) AS instance_count_in_project FROM inner_cte GROUP BY inner_cte.project_id ORDER BY inner_cte.project_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH CompletedMigrations AS (SELECT m.id AS migration_id, m.status, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, m.dest_host, i.display_name, i.vm_state, MAX(i.memory_mb) OVER (ORDER BY m.id ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_memory_mb_next_2 FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS ic ON i.uuid = ic.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed'), Flare3InstanceExtras AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name, iic.network_info, COUNT(ie.id) AS extra_record_count, SUM(i.memory_mb) AS total_memory_mb FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON ie.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS iic ON iic.instance_uuid = i.uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND i.availability_zone = 'flare3' GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, iic.network_info) SELECT cm.migration_id, cm.status, cm.source_compute, cm.dest_compute, cm.dest_host, cm.display_name, cm.vm_state, cm.max_memory_mb_next_2, fie.network_info, fie.extra_record_count, fie.total_memory_mb FROM CompletedMigrations AS cm JOIN instances AS i ON i.display_name = cm.display_name JOIN Flare3InstanceExtras AS fie ON fie.instance_uuid = i.uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND i.availability_zone = 'flare3' ORDER BY cm.migration_id), AverageTotalMemory AS (SELECT AVG(total_memory_mb) AS avg_total_memory_mb FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.migration_id, ic.status, ic.source_compute, ic.dest_compute, ic.dest_host, ic.display_name, ic.vm_state, ic.max_memory_mb_next_2, ic.network_info, ic.extra_record_count, ic.total_memory_mb, atm.avg_total_memory_mb AS benchmark_total_memory_mb, CASE WHEN ic.total_memory_mb > atm.avg_total_memory_mb * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_MEMORY' WHEN ic.total_memory_mb < atm.avg_total_memory_mb * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_MEMORY' ELSE 'AVERAGE_MEMORY' END AS memory_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageTotalMemory AS atm
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH InstanceZoneStateStats AS (SELECT ins.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, ins.VM_STATE, COUNT(ins.UUID) AS instance_count, MIN(ins.CREATED_AT) AS earliest_created_at FROM INSTANCES AS ins JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = ins.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = ins.UUID WHERE ins.DELETED = 0 GROUP BY ins.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, ins.VM_STATE), ShelvedOffloadedInstanceMetadata AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.PROJECT_ID AS project_id, i.POWER_STATE AS power_state, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, MAX(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (ORDER BY i.UUID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_memory_mb_next_2 FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded') SELECT s.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, s.VM_STATE, s.instance_count, s.earliest_created_at, som.instance_uuid, som.display_name, som.project_id, som.power_state, som.metadata_key, som.metadata_value, som.max_memory_mb_next_2 FROM InstanceZoneStateStats AS s JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = s.AVAILABILITY_ZONE AND i.VM_STATE = s.VM_STATE AND i.DELETED = 0 JOIN ShelvedOffloadedInstanceMetadata AS som ON som.instance_uuid = i.UUID ORDER BY s.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, s.VM_STATE, som.instance_uuid, som.metadata_key) SELECT ic.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, ic.VM_STATE, ic.instance_count, ic.earliest_created_at, ic.instance_uuid, ic.display_name, ic.project_id, ic.power_state, ic.metadata_key, ic.metadata_value, ic.max_memory_mb_next_2, avg_stats.avg_instance_count FROM inner_cte AS ic, (SELECT AVG(instance_count) AS avg_instance_count FROM (SELECT COUNT(ins2.UUID) AS instance_count FROM INSTANCES AS ins2 JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie2 ON ie2.INSTANCE_UUID = ins2.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic2 ON iic2.INSTANCE_UUID = ins2.UUID WHERE ins2.DELETED = 0 GROUP BY ins2.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, ins2.VM_STATE) AS sub) AS avg_stats ORDER BY ic.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, ic.VM_STATE, ic.instance_uuid, ic.metadata_key
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH q1 AS (SELECT i.id AS instance_id, i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.user_id, i.project_id, i.display_name, i.vm_state, i.memory_mb, i.vcpus, c.id AS info_cache_id, MAX(i.id) OVER (ORDER BY i.id ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_instance_id_next_2 FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON i.uuid = c.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND c.deleted = 0), q2 AS (SELECT i.uuid, i.display_name, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, m.status, MIN(i.memory_mb) OVER (ORDER BY m.id ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN migrations AS m ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed') SELECT q1.instance_id, q1.instance_uuid, q1.user_id, q1.project_id, q1.display_name, q1.vm_state, q1.memory_mb, q1.vcpus, q1.info_cache_id, q1.max_instance_id_next_2, q2.source_compute, q2.dest_compute, q2.status AS migration_status, q2.window_column AS min_instance_memory_mb_window FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.instance_uuid = q2.uuid ORDER BY q1.instance_id), VmStateVcpuAverages AS (SELECT i.vm_state, AVG(i.vcpus * 1.0) AS avg_vcpus_in_state FROM instances AS i WHERE i.deleted = 0 GROUP BY i.vm_state) SELECT inner_cte.instance_id, inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.user_id, inner_cte.project_id, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.vcpus, inner_cte.info_cache_id, inner_cte.max_instance_id_next_2, inner_cte.source_compute, inner_cte.dest_compute, inner_cte.migration_status, inner_cte.min_instance_memory_mb_window FROM inner_cte JOIN VmStateVcpuAverages AS vsa ON vsa.vm_state = inner_cte.vm_state WHERE inner_cte.vcpus > (SELECT avg_vcpus_in_state FROM VmStateVcpuAverages WHERE VmStateVcpuAverages.vm_state = inner_cte.vm_state) ORDER BY inner_cte.instance_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH CompletedShelvedMigrations AS (SELECT m.status, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, m.instance_uuid, i.display_name, i.memory_mb, ism.key, ism.value, MIN(i.memory_mb) OVER (ORDER BY m.id ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.vm_state = 'shelved_offloaded'), ForgeInstances AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.HOSTNAME AS hostname, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (ORDER BY ie.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge') SELECT csm.status AS migration_status, csm.source_compute, csm.dest_compute, csm.instance_uuid, csm.display_name, csm.memory_mb, fi.hostname, fi.vm_state, fi.instance_extra_id, csm.key AS system_metadata_key, csm.value AS system_metadata_value, csm.window_column AS migration_window_min_memory_mb, fi.window_column AS instance_extra_window_min_memory_mb FROM CompletedShelvedMigrations AS csm JOIN ForgeInstances AS fi ON csm.instance_uuid = fi.instance_uuid ORDER BY csm.instance_uuid, fi.instance_extra_id), ForgeShelvedAvg AS (SELECT AVG(i.memory_mb * 1.0) AS avg_forge_shelved_memory_mb FROM instances AS i WHERE i.display_name = 'forge' AND i.vm_state = 'shelved_offloaded') SELECT ic.migration_status, ic.source_compute, ic.dest_compute, ic.instance_uuid, ic.display_name, ic.memory_mb, ic.hostname, ic.vm_state, ic.instance_extra_id, ic.system_metadata_key, ic.system_metadata_value, ic.migration_window_min_memory_mb, ic.instance_extra_window_min_memory_mb FROM inner_cte AS ic WHERE ic.memory_mb > (SELECT avg_forge_shelved_memory_mb FROM ForgeShelvedAvg) ORDER BY ic.instance_uuid, ic.instance_extra_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH ShelvedOffloadedHighMemoryByName AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, SUM(i.MEMORY_MB) AS total_memory_mb FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME HAVING SUM(i.MEMORY_MB) > 50000), CompletedMigrationsWithWindowMinMemory AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.STATUS AS migration_status, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, m.INSTANCE_UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.MEMORY_MB AS instance_memory_mb, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (ORDER BY m.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed'), CombinedResults AS (SELECT cm.migration_id, cm.migration_status, cm.source_compute, cm.dest_compute, cm.instance_uuid, cm.instance_display_name, cm.instance_memory_mb, cm.instance_extra_id, cm.window_column, sh.total_memory_mb AS shelved_offloaded_total_memory_mb, 'Completed Migration' AS reason FROM CompletedMigrationsWithWindowMinMemory AS cm JOIN ShelvedOffloadedHighMemoryByName AS sh ON sh.DISPLAY_NAME = cm.instance_display_name UNION ALL SELECT NULL AS migration_id, NULL AS migration_status, NULL AS source_compute, NULL AS dest_compute, NULL AS instance_uuid, sh.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, NULL AS instance_memory_mb, NULL AS instance_extra_id, NULL AS window_column, sh.total_memory_mb AS shelved_offloaded_total_memory_mb, 'Shelved Offloaded High Total Memory' AS reason FROM ShelvedOffloadedHighMemoryByName AS sh) SELECT * FROM CombinedResults ORDER BY instance_display_name, migration_id), HighMemoryAverages AS (SELECT AVG(shelved_offloaded_total_memory_mb * 1.0) AS avg_shelved_offloaded_total_memory_mb FROM inner_cte WHERE NOT shelved_offloaded_total_memory_mb IS NULL) SELECT i.migration_id, i.migration_status, i.source_compute, i.dest_compute, i.instance_uuid, i.instance_display_name, i.instance_memory_mb, i.instance_extra_id, i.window_column, i.shelved_offloaded_total_memory_mb, i.reason FROM inner_cte AS i WHERE i.shelved_offloaded_total_memory_mb > (SELECT avg_shelved_offloaded_total_memory_mb FROM HighMemoryAverages) ORDER BY i.instance_display_name, i.migration_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH az_instance_metadata AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3'), completed_migrations AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic ON i.UUID = ic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND ic.DELETED = 0), flare3_completed_migrations AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, m.INSTANCE_UUID FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3') SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT aim.instance_uuid) AS flare3_instances_with_completed_migrations_count, COUNT(DISTINCT fcm.migration_id) AS flare3_completed_migrations_count FROM flare3_completed_migrations AS fcm JOIN az_instance_metadata AS aim ON fcm.INSTANCE_UUID = aim.instance_uuid JOIN completed_migrations AS cm ON fcm.migration_id = cm.migration_id) SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name AS instance_name, SUM(i.vcpus) AS total_vcpus, SUM(i.memory_mb) AS total_memory, CASE WHEN EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM migrations AS m2 WHERE m2.instance_uuid = i.uuid AND m2.deleted = 0 AND NOT m2.status IN ('completed', 'failed', 'error')) THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_active_migrations FROM inner_cte AS x JOIN instances AS i ON i.availability_zone = 'flare3' AND i.deleted = 0 JOIN migrations AS m ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid AND m.deleted = 0 AND m.status = 'completed' JOIN instance_info_caches AS ic ON ic.instance_uuid = i.uuid AND ic.deleted = 0 GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name ORDER BY total_vcpus DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH InstanceMetadataSummary AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name, i.key_name, COUNT(ism.id) AS metadata_entry_count, SUM(i.vcpus) AS total_vcpus FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, i.key_name), DisplayNameMemoryStats AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, MIN(i.ID) AS min_instance_id, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) AS avg_memory_mb FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME) SELECT ims.instance_uuid, ims.display_name, ims.key_name, ims.metadata_entry_count, ims.total_vcpus, dms.min_instance_id, dms.avg_memory_mb, ROUND(CAST(ims.total_vcpus AS REAL) / NULLIF(ims.metadata_entry_count, 0), 2) AS avg_vcpus_per_metadata_entry FROM InstanceMetadataSummary AS ims JOIN DisplayNameMemoryStats AS dms ON ims.display_name = dms.display_name ORDER BY avg_vcpus_per_metadata_entry DESC) SELECT inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.key_name, inner_cte.metadata_entry_count, inner_cte.total_vcpus, inner_cte.min_instance_id, inner_cte.avg_memory_mb, inner_cte.avg_vcpus_per_metadata_entry FROM inner_cte WHERE inner_cte.avg_vcpus_per_metadata_entry > 0.75 ORDER BY inner_cte.avg_vcpus_per_metadata_entry DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH InstanceNameStateCounts AS (SELECT ins.DISPLAY_NAME, ins.VM_STATE, COUNT(DISTINCT ins.UUID) AS instance_count, MIN(ins.CREATED_AT) AS earliest_created_at FROM INSTANCES AS ins JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = ins.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = ins.UUID WHERE ins.DELETED = 0 AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND ins.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY ins.DISPLAY_NAME, ins.VM_STATE), InstanceDetailsWindowed AS (SELECT i.id AS instance_id, i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.user_id, i.project_id, i.display_name, i.vm_state, i.memory_mb, i.vcpus, c.id AS info_cache_id, MAX(i.id) OVER (ORDER BY i.id ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_instance_id_next_2 FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON i.uuid = c.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND c.deleted = 0 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12') SELECT d.instance_id, d.instance_uuid, d.user_id, d.project_id, d.display_name, d.vm_state, d.memory_mb, d.vcpus, d.info_cache_id, d.max_instance_id_next_2, c.instance_count, c.earliest_created_at FROM InstanceDetailsWindowed AS d JOIN InstanceNameStateCounts AS c ON c.DISPLAY_NAME = d.display_name AND c.VM_STATE = d.vm_state ORDER BY d.display_name, d.vm_state, d.instance_id), AvgGroupInstanceCount AS (SELECT AVG(instance_count) AS avg_instance_count FROM (SELECT display_name, vm_state, MAX(instance_count) AS instance_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY display_name, vm_state) AS grouped_counts) SELECT i.instance_id, i.instance_uuid, i.user_id, i.project_id, i.display_name, i.vm_state, i.memory_mb, i.vcpus, i.info_cache_id, i.max_instance_id_next_2, i.instance_count, i.earliest_created_at, (i.instance_count - a.avg_instance_count) AS deviation_from_avg_group_instance_count FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN AvgGroupInstanceCount AS a ORDER BY i.display_name, i.vm_state, i.instance_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH BlazeCompletedInstanceMigrations AS (SELECT t0.DISPLAY_NAME, t1.STATUS, t1.DEST_COMPUTE, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY t1.DEST_COMPUTE ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS t0 JOIN MIGRATIONS AS t1 ON t0.UUID = t1.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE t1.STATUS = 'completed' AND t0.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), EtherCompletedMigrationsWithMetadata AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.STATUS AS migration_status, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, m.DEST_HOST, m.INSTANCE_UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.VM_STATE AS instance_vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS instance_memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS instance_vcpus, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, m.MEMORY_REMAINING AS migration_memory_remaining, MIN(m.MEMORY_REMAINING) OVER (PARTITION BY m.DEST_COMPUTE ORDER BY m.DEST_COMPUTE) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'ether-18') SELECT ecmwm.DEST_COMPUTE, COUNT(DISTINCT ecmwm.migration_id) AS Ether_Completed_Migration_Count, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT bcm.DISPLAY_NAME) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS Has_Blaze8_12_Completed_Migration_To_Same_Dest_Compute FROM EtherCompletedMigrationsWithMetadata AS ecmwm LEFT JOIN BlazeCompletedInstanceMigrations AS bcm ON ecmwm.DEST_COMPUTE = bcm.DEST_COMPUTE GROUP BY ecmwm.DEST_COMPUTE HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT ecmwm.migration_id) >= 2 ORDER BY Ether_Completed_Migration_Count DESC, ecmwm.DEST_COMPUTE) SELECT ic.DEST_COMPUTE, ic.Ether_Completed_Migration_Count, ic.Has_Blaze8_12_Completed_Migration_To_Same_Dest_Compute, (SELECT AVG(i2.VCPUS * 1.0) FROM MIGRATIONS AS m2 JOIN INSTANCES AS i2 ON i2.UUID = m2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m2.STATUS = 'completed' AND i2.HOST = 'ether-18' AND m2.DEST_COMPUTE = ic.DEST_COMPUTE) AS Avg_Ether18_Instance_VCPUs_To_Dest_Compute FROM inner_cte AS ic
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH MigrationDetails AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.INSTANCE_UUID, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, AVG(m.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY m.INSTANCE_UUID ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' ORDER BY m.ID), InstanceMemoryStats AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, MIN(i.ID) AS min_instance_id, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) AS avg_memory_mb FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME), CombinedResults AS (SELECT md.migration_id, md.INSTANCE_UUID, md.SOURCE_COMPUTE, md.DEST_COMPUTE, md.instance_display_name, md.metadata_key, md.metadata_value, md.window_column, ims.min_instance_id, ims.avg_memory_mb FROM MigrationDetails AS md LEFT JOIN InstanceMemoryStats AS ims ON md.instance_display_name = ims.DISPLAY_NAME) SELECT * FROM CombinedResults ORDER BY migration_id), InstanceCounts AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, COUNT(*) AS total_non_deleted_instances_on_host FROM INSTANCES AS i WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME) SELECT inner_cte.*, ic.total_non_deleted_instances_on_host FROM inner_cte LEFT JOIN InstanceCounts AS ic ON inner_cte.instance_display_name = ic.instance_display_name ORDER BY inner_cte.migration_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH InstanceMetadataRanks AS (SELECT i.UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.VM_STATE, ism.KEY, ism.VALUE, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), CompletedMigrationsWithFlavor AS (SELECT m.status, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, i.display_name, ie.flavor, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.status ORDER BY m.status) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12') SELECT imr.UUID, imr.DISPLAY_NAME, imr.VM_STATE, imr.KEY AS system_metadata_key, imr.VALUE AS system_metadata_value, imr.window_column AS metadata_rank_within_key, cmwf.status AS migration_status, cmwf.source_compute, cmwf.dest_compute, cmwf.flavor AS instance_flavor_json, cmwf.window_column AS migration_status_dense_rank FROM InstanceMetadataRanks AS imr JOIN CompletedMigrationsWithFlavor AS cmwf ON imr.DISPLAY_NAME = cmwf.display_name), VmStateCounts AS (SELECT i.host, i.vm_state, COUNT(*) AS vm_state_count FROM INSTANCES AS i WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.host, i.vm_state), MostCommonVmState AS (SELECT vsc.host, vsc.vm_state FROM VmStateCounts AS vsc WHERE vsc.vm_state_count = (SELECT MAX(vsc2.vm_state_count) FROM VmStateCounts AS vsc2 WHERE vsc2.host = vsc.host)) SELECT ic.UUID, ic.DISPLAY_NAME, ic.VM_STATE, ic.system_metadata_key, ic.system_metadata_value, ic.metadata_rank_within_key, ic.migration_status, ic.source_compute, ic.dest_compute, ic.instance_flavor_json, ic.migration_status_dense_rank FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN INSTANCES AS i2 ON i2.uuid = ic.UUID JOIN MostCommonVmState AS mcvs ON mcvs.host = i2.host WHERE ic.VM_STATE <> mcvs.vm_state

WITH inner_cte AS (WITH InstanceWindowed AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, SUM(i.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY i.VM_STATE ORDER BY i.ID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), DisplayNameGeoMeanMemory AS (SELECT i.display_name, EXP(AVG(LN(i.memory_mb))) AS geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON ism.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS iic ON i.uuid = iic.instance_uuid WHERE i.memory_mb > 0 AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.display_name ORDER BY geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC) SELECT iw.instance_uuid, iw.display_name, iw.vm_state, iw.window_column, dngmm.geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM InstanceWindowed AS iw JOIN DisplayNameGeoMeanMemory AS dngmm ON iw.display_name = dngmm.display_name ORDER BY dngmm.geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC, iw.vm_state, iw.window_column) SELECT CASE WHEN LAG(inner_cte.display_name) OVER (ORDER BY inner_cte.geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC, inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.window_column) = inner_cte.display_name THEN NULL ELSE inner_cte.display_name END AS display_name, inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.window_column, inner_cte.geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM inner_cte ORDER BY inner_cte.geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC, inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.window_column
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH MigrationDetails AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.INSTANCE_UUID, m.MIGRATION_TYPE, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, m.DEST_HOST, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.VM_STATE, i.MEMORY_MB, i.VCPUS, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, m.MEMORY_TOTAL, MIN(m.MEMORY_TOTAL) OVER (PARTITION BY m.MIGRATION_TYPE ORDER BY m.MIGRATION_TYPE) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), MigrationRanks AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic ON i.UUID = ic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND ic.DELETED = 0 AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT md.MIGRATION_TYPE, COUNT(DISTINCT md.migration_id) AS Completed_Migration_Count, MIN(md.window_column) AS Min_Memory_Total_Within_Migration_Type FROM MigrationDetails AS md JOIN MigrationRanks AS mr ON md.migration_id = mr.migration_id GROUP BY md.MIGRATION_TYPE HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT md.migration_id) >= 2 ORDER BY Completed_Migration_Count DESC, md.MIGRATION_TYPE), AverageMemoryTotal AS (SELECT AVG(m.MEMORY_TOTAL) AS avg_memory_total FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic ON i.UUID = ic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' AND ic.DELETED = 0) SELECT cte.MIGRATION_TYPE, cte.Completed_Migration_Count, cte.Min_Memory_Total_Within_Migration_Type, (cte.Min_Memory_Total_Within_Migration_Type - amt.avg_memory_total) AS deviation_from_overall_avg_memory_total FROM inner_cte AS cte CROSS JOIN AverageMemoryTotal AS amt ORDER BY cte.Completed_Migration_Count DESC, cte.MIGRATION_TYPE
