WITH inner_cte AS (WITH CompletedMigrationsOnBlaze AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.HOSTNAME, i.VM_STATE, i.MEMORY_MB, i.VCPUS, i.UUID, m.STATUS, MAX(m.ID) OVER (ORDER BY i.UUID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_migration_id_next2 FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12'), ImageTypeInstancesOnBlaze AS (SELECT i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME, COUNT(i.ID) AS instance_count, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) AS min_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME), CombinedInstanceInfo AS (SELECT cm.DISPLAY_NAME, cm.HOSTNAME, cm.VM_STATE, cm.MEMORY_MB, cm.VCPUS, cm.UUID, cm.STATUS, cm.max_migration_id_next2, iti.instance_count, iti.min_memory_mb FROM CompletedMigrationsOnBlaze AS cm JOIN ImageTypeInstancesOnBlaze AS iti ON cm.VM_STATE = iti.VM_STATE AND cm.DISPLAY_NAME = iti.DISPLAY_NAME) SELECT * FROM CombinedInstanceInfo ORDER BY UUID) SELECT ic.DISPLAY_NAME, ic.HOSTNAME, ic.VM_STATE, ic.MEMORY_MB, ic.VCPUS, ic.UUID, ic.STATUS, ic.max_migration_id_next2, ic.instance_count, ic.min_memory_mb, m.MEMORY_TOTAL, m.MEMORY_REMAINING, (CAST(m.MEMORY_REMAINING * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(m.MEMORY_TOTAL, 0)) AS memory_remaining_ratio FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = ic.UUID AND m.STATUS = ic.STATUS WHERE (CAST(m.MEMORY_REMAINING * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(m.MEMORY_TOTAL, 0)) > 0.10 ORDER BY ic.UUID
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH InstanceImageTypeStats AS (SELECT ins.DISPLAY_NAME, ins.VM_STATE, COUNT(DISTINCT ins.UUID) AS instance_count, MIN(ins.CREATED_AT) AS earliest_created_at FROM INSTANCES AS ins JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = ins.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = ins.UUID WHERE ins.DELETED = 0 AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND ins.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY ins.DISPLAY_NAME, ins.VM_STATE), CompletedMigrationsOnHost AS (SELECT i.UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, m.STATUS, m.DEST_COMPUTE, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12'), TotalCompletedMigrationsOnHost AS (SELECT i.UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, m.STATUS, m.DEST_COMPUTE, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12') SELECT cmh.UUID AS instance_uuid, cmh.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, cmh.STATUS AS migration_status, cmh.DEST_COMPUTE AS destination_compute_host, cmh.window_column AS migration_status_rank, iits.VM_STATE AS vm_state, iits.instance_count AS distinct_instance_count_for_display_name_and_state, iits.earliest_created_at AS earliest_instance_created_at_for_display_name_and_state FROM CompletedMigrationsOnHost AS cmh JOIN InstanceImageTypeStats AS iits ON cmh.DISPLAY_NAME = iits.DISPLAY_NAME WHERE cmh.STATUS = 'completed') SELECT inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.instance_display_name, inner_cte.migration_status, inner_cte.destination_compute_host, inner_cte.migration_status_rank, inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.distinct_instance_count_for_display_name_and_state, inner_cte.earliest_instance_created_at_for_display_name_and_state, avg_counts.avg_instance_count_for_display_name_and_state FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(sub.instance_count) AS avg_instance_count_for_display_name_and_state FROM (SELECT ins.DISPLAY_NAME, ins.VM_STATE, COUNT(DISTINCT ins.UUID) AS instance_count FROM INSTANCES AS ins JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = ins.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = ins.UUID WHERE ins.DELETED = 0 AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND ins.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY ins.DISPLAY_NAME, ins.VM_STATE) AS sub) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH q1 AS (SELECT i.id AS instance_id, i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.user_id, i.project_id, i.display_name, i.vm_state, i.memory_mb, i.vcpus, c.id AS info_cache_id, MAX(i.id) OVER (ORDER BY i.id ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_instance_id_next_2 FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON i.uuid = c.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND c.deleted = 0 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12'), q2 AS (SELECT i.UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, m.STATUS, m.DEST_COMPUTE, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12') SELECT q1.instance_id, q1.instance_uuid, q1.user_id, q1.project_id, q1.display_name, q1.vm_state, q1.memory_mb, q1.vcpus, q1.info_cache_id, q1.max_instance_id_next_2, q2.STATUS AS migration_status, q2.DEST_COMPUTE AS dest_compute, q2.window_column AS migration_status_rank FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.instance_uuid = q2.UUID ORDER BY q1.instance_id), HostAvgMem AS (SELECT AVG(i.memory_mb) AS avg_memory_mb FROM instances AS i WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12') SELECT inner_cte.instance_id, inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.user_id, inner_cte.project_id, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.vcpus, inner_cte.info_cache_id, inner_cte.max_instance_id_next_2, inner_cte.migration_status, inner_cte.dest_compute, inner_cte.migration_status_rank FROM inner_cte CROSS JOIN HostAvgMem WHERE inner_cte.memory_mb > HostAvgMem.avg_memory_mb ORDER BY inner_cte.instance_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH MigrationMetadata AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.INSTANCE_UUID, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, AVG(m.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY m.INSTANCE_UUID ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' ORDER BY m.ID), InstanceExtraDetails AS (SELECT i.UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.VM_STATE, i.MEMORY_MB, i.VCPUS, i.KEY_NAME, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY i.VCPUS ORDER BY i.VCPUS) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT mm.migration_id, mm.INSTANCE_UUID, mm.SOURCE_COMPUTE, mm.DEST_COMPUTE, mm.instance_display_name, mm.metadata_key, mm.metadata_value, mm.window_column AS avg_migration_id_within_instance_uuid, ied.VM_STATE, ied.MEMORY_MB, ied.VCPUS, ied.KEY_NAME, ied.instance_extra_id, ied.window_column AS min_memory_mb_within_vcpus FROM MigrationMetadata AS mm JOIN InstanceExtraDetails AS ied ON mm.INSTANCE_UUID = ied.UUID ORDER BY mm.migration_id) SELECT inner_cte.INSTANCE_UUID, inner_cte.instance_display_name, MAX(inner_cte.VCPUS) AS max_vcpus_per_instance_uuid FROM inner_cte GROUP BY inner_cte.INSTANCE_UUID, inner_cte.instance_display_name
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH MigrationMemoryWindow AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.MEMORY_MB, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), MigrationIdRunningSumByKey AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, SUM(m.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), MetadataKeyMigrationCounts AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, COUNT(*) OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY) AS key_migration_count FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT mmw.migration_id, mmw.SOURCE_COMPUTE, mmw.DEST_COMPUTE, mmw.DISPLAY_NAME, mmw.MEMORY_MB, mmw.metadata_key, mmw.metadata_value, mmw.window_column AS avg_memory_mb_window_2_preceding_2_following, mirs.window_column AS running_sum_migration_id_within_metadata_key, mkmc.key_migration_count FROM MigrationMemoryWindow AS mmw JOIN MigrationIdRunningSumByKey AS mirs ON mmw.migration_id = mirs.migration_id AND mmw.metadata_key = mirs.metadata_key JOIN MetadataKeyMigrationCounts AS mkmc ON mmw.migration_id = mkmc.migration_id AND mmw.metadata_key = mkmc.metadata_key WHERE (CAST(mkmc.key_migration_count * 1.0 AS REAL) / (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12')) > 0.10) SELECT ic.migration_id, ic.SOURCE_COMPUTE, ic.DEST_COMPUTE, ic.DISPLAY_NAME, ic.MEMORY_MB, ic.metadata_key, ic.metadata_value, ic.avg_memory_mb_window_2_preceding_2_following, ic.running_sum_migration_id_within_metadata_key, ic.key_migration_count, i.PROJECT_ID AS project_id FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON ic.migration_id = m.ID JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH q1 AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.HOSTNAME, i.VM_STATE, i.MEMORY_MB, i.VCPUS, i.UUID, m.STATUS, MAX(m.ID) OVER (ORDER BY i.UUID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_migration_id_next2 FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12'), q2 AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name, i.key_name, COUNT(ism.id) AS metadata_entry_count, SUM(i.vcpus) AS total_vcpus FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, i.key_name) SELECT q1.UUID AS instance_uuid, q1.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, q1.HOSTNAME, q1.VM_STATE, q1.MEMORY_MB, q1.VCPUS, q1.STATUS AS migration_status, q1.max_migration_id_next2, q2.key_name, q2.metadata_entry_count, q2.total_vcpus FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.UUID = q2.instance_uuid ORDER BY q1.UUID), HostAzVcpuAverages AS (SELECT i.host, i.availability_zone, AVG(i.vcpus * 1.0) AS avg_vcpus FROM instances AS i WHERE i.host = 'blaze8-12' AND i.availability_zone = 'flare3' GROUP BY i.host, i.availability_zone) SELECT ic.instance_uuid, ic.instance_display_name, ic.hostname, ic.vm_state, ic.memory_mb, ic.vcpus, ic.migration_status, ic.max_migration_id_next2, ic.key_name, ic.metadata_entry_count, ic.total_vcpus FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN instances AS i ON i.uuid = ic.instance_uuid WHERE ic.vcpus > (SELECT ha.avg_vcpus FROM HostAzVcpuAverages AS ha WHERE ha.host = i.host AND ha.availability_zone = i.availability_zone) ORDER BY ic.instance_uuid
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH q1 AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name, i.availability_zone, COUNT(m.id) AS migration_count, SUM(m.memory_processed) AS total_memory_processed FROM instances AS i JOIN migrations AS m ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.host = 'prime-35' GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, i.availability_zone), q2 AS (SELECT ins.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, ins.VM_STATE, COUNT(ins.UUID) AS instance_count, MIN(ins.CREATED_AT) AS earliest_created_at FROM INSTANCES AS ins JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = ins.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = ins.UUID WHERE ins.DELETED = 0 AND ins.HOST = 'prime-35' GROUP BY ins.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, ins.VM_STATE) SELECT q1.instance_uuid, q1.display_name, q1.availability_zone, q2.vm_state, q2.instance_count, q2.earliest_created_at, q1.migration_count, q1.total_memory_processed FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.availability_zone = q2.availability_zone ORDER BY q1.total_memory_processed DESC, q1.migration_count DESC, q1.instance_uuid) SELECT availability_zone, vm_state, instance_count, earliest_created_at, MAX(total_memory_processed) AS max_total_memory_processed_by_any_instance_in_zone FROM inner_cte GROUP BY availability_zone, vm_state, instance_count, earliest_created_at ORDER BY max_total_memory_processed_by_any_instance_in_zone DESC, availability_zone, vm_state
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH MigrationInstanceMetadataWindow AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ism.KEY ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), GeometricMeanMemoryByDisplayName AS (SELECT i.display_name, EXP(AVG(LN(i.memory_mb))) AS geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON ie.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS iic ON i.uuid = iic.instance_uuid WHERE iic.network_info <> '[]' AND i.memory_mb > 0 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.display_name ORDER BY geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC) SELECT mimw.migration_id, mimw.instance_display_name, gmm.geometric_mean_memory_mb, mimw.metadata_key, mimw.metadata_value, mimw.window_column FROM MigrationInstanceMetadataWindow AS mimw JOIN GeometricMeanMemoryByDisplayName AS gmm ON mimw.instance_display_name = gmm.display_name ORDER BY mimw.instance_display_name, mimw.migration_id, mimw.metadata_key) SELECT migration_id, instance_display_name, geometric_mean_memory_mb, metadata_key, metadata_value, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE geometric_mean_memory_mb > (SELECT AVG(geometric_mean_memory_mb) FROM inner_cte)
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH CompletedMigrationsOnHost AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic ON i.UUID = ic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND ic.DELETED = 0 AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), ActiveNotDeletedMigrations AS (SELECT m.status, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, m.dest_host, m.instance_uuid, i.display_name, i.vm_state, ism.key, ism.value, MAX(i.memory_mb) OVER (ORDER BY m.id ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_memory_mb_next2 FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE m.deleted = 0 AND NOT m.status IN ('accepted', 'confirmed', 'reverted', 'error', 'failed', 'completed')) SELECT cm.migration_id, cm.source_compute AS completed_source_compute, cm.dest_compute AS completed_dest_compute, cm.instance_display_name, cm.window_column AS completed_status_rank_by_migration_id, anm.status AS active_migration_status, anm.source_compute AS active_source_compute, anm.dest_compute AS active_dest_compute, anm.dest_host AS active_dest_host, anm.instance_uuid AS active_instance_uuid, anm.display_name AS active_instance_display_name, anm.vm_state AS active_instance_vm_state, anm.key AS system_metadata_key, anm.value AS system_metadata_value, anm.max_memory_mb_next2 FROM CompletedMigrationsOnHost AS cm JOIN ActiveNotDeletedMigrations AS anm ON cm.instance_display_name = anm.display_name ORDER BY cm.migration_id), AvgMaxMem AS (SELECT AVG(max_memory_mb_next2) AS avg_max_memory_mb_next2 FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.migration_id, i.completed_source_compute, i.completed_dest_compute, i.instance_display_name, i.completed_status_rank_by_migration_id, i.active_migration_status, i.active_source_compute, i.active_dest_compute, i.active_dest_host, i.active_instance_uuid, i.active_instance_display_name, i.active_instance_vm_state, i.system_metadata_key, i.system_metadata_value, i.max_memory_mb_next2, a.avg_max_memory_mb_next2 AS benchmark_avg_max_memory_mb_next2, CASE WHEN i.max_memory_mb_next2 > a.avg_max_memory_mb_next2 * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_MEMORY' WHEN i.max_memory_mb_next2 < a.avg_max_memory_mb_next2 * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_MEMORY' ELSE 'AVERAGE_MEMORY' END AS memory_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN AvgMaxMem AS a ORDER BY i.migration_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH Blaze8_12_Instance_Migration_Summary AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name AS instance_display_name, i.availability_zone AS availability_zone, COUNT(m.id) AS completed_migration_count, SUM(m.memory_processed) AS total_memory_processed FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND m.memory_processed > 0 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, i.availability_zone), Ether_18_Completed_Migration_Details AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.STATUS AS migration_status, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, m.DEST_HOST, m.INSTANCE_UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.VM_STATE AS instance_vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS instance_memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS instance_instance_vcpus, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, m.MEMORY_REMAINING AS migration_memory_remaining, MIN(m.MEMORY_REMAINING) OVER (PARTITION BY m.DEST_COMPUTE ORDER BY m.DEST_COMPUTE) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'ether-18') SELECT b.instance_uuid, b.instance_display_name, b.availability_zone, b.completed_migration_count, b.total_memory_processed, COUNT(e.migration_id) AS ether_18_completed_migration_rows, MIN(e.window_column) AS min_migration_memory_remaining_within_dest_compute FROM Blaze8_12_Instance_Migration_Summary AS b CROSS JOIN Ether_18_Completed_Migration_Details AS e GROUP BY b.instance_uuid, b.instance_display_name, b.availability_zone, b.completed_migration_count, b.total_memory_processed) SELECT inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.instance_display_name, inner_cte.availability_zone, inner_cte.completed_migration_count, inner_cte.total_memory_processed, inner_cte.ether_18_completed_migration_rows, avg_min_memory_remaining_across_dest_compute FROM (SELECT m.DEST_COMPUTE, MIN(m.MEMORY_REMAINING) AS min_per_dest_compute FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'ether-18' GROUP BY m.DEST_COMPUTE) AS sub) AS avg_min_per_dest_compute ON 1 = 1
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH migration_details AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, m.DEST_HOST, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.MEMORY_MB, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' ORDER BY m.ID), instance_details AS (SELECT i.uuid, i.display_name, i.memory_mb, i.vcpus, ie.flavor, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY i.project_id ORDER BY i.project_id) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12'), host_summary AS (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT md.migration_id) AS completed_migration_count, COUNT(DISTINCT id.uuid) AS active_instance_count FROM migration_details AS md CROSS JOIN instance_details AS id) SELECT hs.completed_migration_count, hs.active_instance_count FROM host_summary AS hs), HostActiveInstances AS (SELECT i.host, COUNT(DISTINCT i.uuid) AS active_instance_count FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND NOT i.host IS NULL AND i.host <> '' GROUP BY i.host), AverageHostInstances AS (SELECT AVG(active_instance_count) AS avg_active_instances FROM HostActiveInstances) SELECT 'blaze8-12' AS host, ic.completed_migration_count, ic.active_instance_count, ahi.avg_active_instances AS benchmark, CASE WHEN ic.active_instance_count > ahi.avg_active_instances * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_UTILIZATION' WHEN ic.active_instance_count < ahi.avg_active_instances * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_UTILIZATION' ELSE 'AVERAGE_UTILIZATION' END AS utilization_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageHostInstances AS ahi
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH sq1 AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY i.VM_STATE ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), sq2 AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name, i.availability_zone, COUNT(ism.id) AS metadata_entry_count, SUM(i.vcpus) AS total_vcpus FROM instance_extra AS ie JOIN instances AS i ON ie.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, i.availability_zone) SELECT sq1.instance_uuid, sq1.display_name, sq1.vm_state, sq1.memory_mb, sq1.vcpus, sq1.instance_extra_id, sq1.window_column, sq2.availability_zone, sq2.metadata_entry_count, sq2.total_vcpus FROM sq1 JOIN sq2 ON sq1.instance_uuid = sq2.instance_uuid WHERE sq2.availability_zone = 'flare3' ORDER BY sq1.vm_state, sq1.window_column, sq1.instance_uuid), zone_avg AS (SELECT inner_cte.availability_zone, AVG(inner_cte.vcpus * 1.0) AS avg_vcpus_in_zone FROM inner_cte GROUP BY inner_cte.availability_zone) SELECT inner_cte.instance_uuid, inner_cte.display_name, inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.memory_mb, inner_cte.vcpus, inner_cte.instance_extra_id, inner_cte.window_column, inner_cte.availability_zone, inner_cte.metadata_entry_count, inner_cte.total_vcpus FROM inner_cte JOIN zone_avg ON zone_avg.availability_zone = inner_cte.availability_zone WHERE inner_cte.vcpus > (SELECT zone_avg.avg_vcpus_in_zone FROM zone_avg WHERE zone_avg.availability_zone = inner_cte.availability_zone) ORDER BY inner_cte.vm_state, inner_cte.window_column, inner_cte.instance_uuid
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH MigrationDetails AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.INSTANCE_UUID, m.MIGRATION_TYPE, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, m.DEST_HOST, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.VM_STATE, i.MEMORY_MB, m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), InstanceStateSummary AS (SELECT i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME, COUNT(i.UUID) AS instance_count, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) AS min_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.VCPUS > 8 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME) SELECT md.migration_id, md.instance_uuid, md.migration_type, md.source_compute, md.dest_compute, md.dest_host, md.display_name, md.vm_state, md.memory_mb, md.vcpus, iss ON md.vm_state = iss.vm_state AND md.display_name = iss.display_name), ProjectMigrations AS (SELECT i.project_id, COUNT(*) AS total_completed_migrations FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.project_id), AverageProjectMigrations AS (SELECT AVG(total_completed_migrations) AS avg_completed_migrations FROM ProjectMigrations) SELECT ic.migration_id, ic.instance_uuid, ic.migration_type, ic.source_compute, ic.dest_compute, ic.dest_host, ic.display_name, ic.vm_state, ic.memory_mb, ic.vcpus, ic.instance_extra_id, ic.memory_total, ic.min_memory_total_within_migration_type, ic.instance_count, ic.min_memory_mb, i.project_id, pm.total_completed_migrations, apm.avg_completed_migrations AS benchmark_completed_migrations, CASE WHEN pm.total_completed_migrations > apm.avg_completed_migrations * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_UTILIZATION' WHEN pm.total_completed_migrations < apm.avg_completed_migrations * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_UTILIZATION' ELSE 'AVERAGE_UTILIZATION' END AS project_utilization_category FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ic.instance_uuid = i.uuid LEFT JOIN ProjectMigrations AS pm ON i.project_id = pm.project_id CROSS JOIN AverageProjectMigrations AS apm
WITH sq1 AS (SELECT i.display_name, i.availability_zone, i.vm_state, i.memory_mb, i.vcpus, i.uuid, MAX(ism.id) OVER (ORDER BY i.uuid ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_metadata_id_next_2 FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3'), sq2 AS (SELECT m.status, EXP(AVG(LN(i.memory_mb))) AS geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE i.memory_mb > 0 GROUP BY m.status ORDER BY geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC) SELECT sq1.display_name, sq1.availability_zone, sq1.vm_state, sq1.memory_mb, sq1.vcpus, sq1.uuid, sq1.max_metadata_id_next_2, sq2.status AS migration_status, sq2.geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM sq1 JOIN migrations AS m ON m.instance_uuid = sq1.uuid JOIN sq2 ON sq2.status = m.status JOIN instances AS i2 ON i2.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE i2.availability_zone = 'flare3' AND i2.memory_mb > 0 ORDER BY sq2.geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC, sq1.uuid
WITH InstanceDetails AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.POWER_STATE AS power_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, MAX(i.ID) OVER (ORDER BY i.ID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_instance_id_next_2 FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3'), TopCompletedMigrations AS (SELECT i.display_name, COUNT(m.id) AS completed_migrations FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND NOT i.key_name IS NULL GROUP BY i.display_name ORDER BY completed_migrations DESC LIMIT 10) SELECT id.instance_uuid, id.display_name, id.vm_state, id.power_state, id.memory_mb, id.vcpus, id.instance_extra_id, id.max_instance_id_next_2, tcm.completed_migrations, ROUND(CAST(tcm.completed_migrations AS REAL) / NULLIF(id.vcpus, 0), 2) AS completed_migrations_per_vcpu FROM InstanceDetails AS id JOIN TopCompletedMigrations AS tcm ON tcm.display_name = id.display_name ORDER BY completed_migrations_per_vcpu DESC, tcm.completed_migrations DESC
WITH CompletedMigrations AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.INSTANCE_UUID, m.MIGRATION_TYPE, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, m.DEST_HOST, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.VM_STATE, i.MEMORY_MB, i.VCPUS, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, m.MEMORY_TOTAL, MIN(m.MEMORY_TOTAL) OVER (PARTITION BY m.MIGRATION_TYPE ORDER BY m.MIGRATION_TYPE) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed'), TopMetadataInstances AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, COUNT(ism.ID) AS metadata_entries FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME ORDER BY metadata_entries DESC LIMIT 10), TotalCompletedMigrations AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, COUNT(m.ID) AS completed_migration_count FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME) SELECT cm.migration_id, cm.INSTANCE_UUID, cm.MIGRATION_TYPE, cm.SOURCE_COMPUTE, cm.DEST_COMPUTE, cm.DEST_HOST, cm.DISPLAY_NAME, cm.VM_STATE, cm.MEMORY_MB, cm.VCPUS, cm.instance_extra_id, cm.MEMORY_TOTAL, cm.window_column, tmi.metadata_entries, tcm.completed_migration_count FROM CompletedMigrations AS cm JOIN TopMetadataInstances AS tmi ON cm.DISPLAY_NAME = tmi.DISPLAY_NAME JOIN TotalCompletedMigrations AS tcm ON cm.DISPLAY_NAME = tcm.DISPLAY_NAME
WITH HighMemoryByMetadata AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, MAX(i.MEMORY_MB) AS max_memory_mb, i.LAUNCHED_AT FROM INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON i.UUID = iic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME, ism.KEY, i.LAUNCHED_AT HAVING MAX(i.MEMORY_MB) > 16000), TopNetworkCacheByInstance AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, COUNT(iic.ID) AS cache_records FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON i.UUID = iic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME ORDER BY cache_records DESC LIMIT 10) SELECT hmbm.DISPLAY_NAME, hmbm.metadata_key, hmbm.max_memory_mb, hmbm.LAUNCHED_AT, tnc.cache_records FROM HighMemoryByMetadata AS hmbm JOIN TopNetworkCacheByInstance AS tnc ON hmbm.DISPLAY_NAME = tnc.DISPLAY_NAME ORDER BY tnc.cache_records DESC, hmbm.max_memory_mb DESC
WITH subq1 AS (SELECT sg.NAME AS security_group_name, sgr.PROTOCOL, sgr.FROM_PORT, SUM(sgr.FROM_PORT) OVER (PARTITION BY sgr.PROTOCOL ORDER BY sgr.FROM_PORT) AS window_column FROM SECURITY_GROUPS AS sg JOIN SECURITY_GROUP_RULES AS sgr ON sg.ID = sgr.PARENT_GROUP_ID WHERE sg.NAME = 'aurum-xenon'), subq2 AS (SELECT sg.NAME AS security_group_name, COUNT(sgr.ID) AS rule_count FROM SECURITY_GROUPS AS sg JOIN SECURITY_GROUP_RULES AS sgr ON sg.ID = sgr.PARENT_GROUP_ID WHERE sg.DELETED = 0 AND sgr.DELETED = 0 GROUP BY sg.NAME ORDER BY rule_count DESC LIMIT 10) SELECT s1.security_group_name, s2.rule_count, s1.PROTOCOL, s1.FROM_PORT, s1.window_column FROM subq1 AS s1 JOIN subq2 AS s2 ON s1.security_group_name = s2.security_group_name ORDER BY s2.rule_count DESC, s1.PROTOCOL, s1.FROM_PORT
WITH top_metadata AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, COUNT(ism.ID) AS metadata_entries FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME ORDER BY metadata_entries DESC LIMIT 10), completed_migrations AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE AS instance_availability_zone, m.STATUS AS migration_status, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed') SELECT cm.migration_id, cm.instance_display_name, cm.instance_availability_zone, cm.migration_status, cm.window_column, tm.metadata_entries FROM completed_migrations AS cm JOIN top_metadata AS tm ON tm.DISPLAY_NAME = cm.instance_display_name ORDER BY tm.metadata_entries DESC, cm.migration_id
WITH ForgeInstanceMetadata AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, ism.key AS metadata_key, ism.value AS metadata_value, i.power_state, SUM(i.id) OVER (PARTITION BY i.power_state ORDER BY i.id) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ism.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON i.uuid = iic.instance_uuid WHERE i.display_name = 'forge'), CompletedMigrations AS (SELECT i.uuid, i.display_name, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, m.status, MIN(i.memory_mb) OVER (ORDER BY m.id ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN migrations AS m ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed') SELECT fim.instance_uuid, fim.metadata_key, fim.metadata_value, fim.power_state, fim.window_column AS running_sum_instance_ids_within_power_state, cm.source_compute, cm.dest_compute, cm.status AS migration_status, cm.window_column AS min_instance_memory_mb_window FROM ForgeInstanceMetadata AS fim JOIN CompletedMigrations AS cm ON cm.uuid = fim.instance_uuid WHERE cm.display_name = 'forge' ORDER BY fim.instance_uuid, fim.metadata_key
WITH MigrationCompletedFlare3 AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, m.STATUS AS migration_status, SUM(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY m.MIGRATION_TYPE ORDER BY i.MEMORY_MB) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND m.STATUS = 'completed'), ShelvedOffloadedInstances AS (SELECT i.ID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, c.NETWORK_INFO, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE ORDER BY i.ID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS c ON i.UUID = c.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded') SELECT mcf.instance_uuid, mcf.instance_display_name, mcf.migration_status, mcf.window_column AS migration_type_memory_running_sum, soi.ID AS instance_id, soi.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, soi.NETWORK_INFO, soi.window_column AS az_instance_id_rank FROM MigrationCompletedFlare3 AS mcf JOIN ShelvedOffloadedInstances AS soi ON soi.DISPLAY_NAME = mcf.instance_display_name WHERE soi.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' ORDER BY soi.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, soi.ID, mcf.instance_uuid
WITH MigrationMetadata AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, SUM(m.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed'), InstanceMetadataDetails AS (SELECT i.ID AS instance_id, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.USER_ID AS user_id, i.PROJECT_ID AS project_id, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.POWER_STATE AS power_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE AS availability_zone, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, ism.ID AS metadata_id, MIN(ism.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY ism.KEY) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.UUID = '0003c04d-211b-4847-8916-102efabca5bc') SELECT imd.instance_id, imd.display_name, imd.user_id, imd.project_id, imd.vm_state, imd.power_state, imd.memory_mb, imd.vcpus, imd.availability_zone, imd.metadata_key, imd.metadata_value, imd.metadata_id, imd.window_column AS min_metadata_id_in_key_group, mm.migration_id, mm.instance_display_name AS migration_instance_display_name, mm.window_column AS running_sum_migration_ids_in_key_group, ROUND(CAST(mm.window_column AS REAL) / NULLIF(imd.window_column, 0), 2) AS running_sum_to_min_metadata_id_ratio FROM InstanceMetadataDetails AS imd JOIN MigrationMetadata AS mm ON mm.metadata_key = imd.metadata_key AND mm.instance_display_name = imd.display_name ORDER BY running_sum_to_min_metadata_id_ratio DESC, mm.migration_id
WITH MigrationCompleted AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.HOSTNAME AS instance_hostname, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE AS migration_source_compute, m.DEST_COMPUTE AS migration_dest_compute, m.DEST_HOST AS migration_dest_host, m.MIGRATION_TYPE AS migration_type, m.MEMORY_TOTAL AS memory_total, m.MEMORY_PROCESSED AS memory_processed, m.MEMORY_REMAINING AS memory_remaining, MIN(m.MEMORY_REMAINING) OVER (PARTITION BY m.INSTANCE_UUID ORDER BY m.INSTANCE_UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed'), InstanceMetadataWindow AS (SELECT i.display_name, i.availability_zone, i.vm_state, i.memory_mb, i.vcpus, i.uuid, MAX(ism.id) OVER (ORDER BY i.uuid ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_metadata_id_next_2 FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3') SELECT mc.instance_uuid, mc.instance_display_name, imw.availability_zone, imw.vm_state, imw.memory_mb, imw.vcpus, mc.instance_hostname, mc.migration_source_compute, mc.migration_dest_compute, mc.migration_dest_host, mc.migration_type, mc.memory_total, mc.memory_processed, mc.memory_remaining, mc.window_column AS min_memory_remaining_per_instance_uuid, imw.max_metadata_id_next_2, ROUND(CAST(mc.memory_processed AS REAL) / NULLIF(mc.memory_total, 0), 4) AS memory_processed_ratio FROM MigrationCompleted AS mc JOIN InstanceMetadataWindow AS imw ON imw.uuid = mc.instance_uuid ORDER BY memory_processed_ratio DESC
WITH MetaByStateAZ AS (SELECT ins.VM_STATE, ins.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, COUNT(ins.UUID) AS instance_count, MIN(ins.MEMORY_MB) AS min_memory_mb FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS ins ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = ins.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ins.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY ins.VM_STATE, ins.AVAILABILITY_ZONE), BigByStateName AS (SELECT i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME, COUNT(i.UUID) AS instance_count, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) AS min_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.VCPUS > 8 GROUP BY i.VM_STATE, i.DISPLAY_NAME) SELECT m.VM_STATE, m.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, b.DISPLAY_NAME, m.instance_count AS meta_instance_count, b.instance_count AS big_instance_count, m.min_memory_mb AS meta_min_memory_mb, b.min_memory_mb AS big_min_memory_mb, ROUND(CAST(b.instance_count AS REAL) / NULLIF(m.instance_count, 0), 2) AS Big_to_Meta_Instance_Count_Ratio, ROUND(CAST(b.min_memory_mb AS REAL) / NULLIF(m.min_memory_mb, 0), 2) AS Big_to_Meta_Min_Memory_Ratio FROM MetaByStateAZ AS m JOIN BigByStateName AS b ON m.VM_STATE = b.VM_STATE ORDER BY Big_to_Meta_Instance_Count_Ratio DESC
WITH ShelvedOffloadedMemory AS (SELECT i.display_name, SUM(i.memory_mb) AS total_memory_mb FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON ie.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS iic ON i.uuid = iic.instance_uuid WHERE i.vm_state = 'shelved_offloaded' GROUP BY i.display_name HAVING SUM(i.memory_mb) > 20000), ForgeInstanceMetadataWindow AS (SELECT i.UUID, i.HOSTNAME, i.VM_STATE, ism.KEY, ism.VALUE, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (ORDER BY i.ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge') SELECT f.UUID, f.HOSTNAME, f.VM_STATE, f.KEY, f.VALUE, f.window_column, som.total_memory_mb, ROUND(CAST(f.window_column AS REAL) / NULLIF(som.total_memory_mb, 0), 4) AS Window_Min_to_TotalMemory_Ratio FROM ForgeInstanceMetadataWindow AS f JOIN ShelvedOffloadedMemory AS som ON som.display_name = 'forge' WHERE f.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded' ORDER BY Window_Min_to_TotalMemory_Ratio DESC, f.UUID, f.KEY
WITH ForgeInstanceDetails AS (SELECT t0.UUID, t0.HOSTNAME, t0.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, t2.NETWORK_INFO, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY t0.AVAILABILITY_ZONE ORDER BY t0.ID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS t0 JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS t1 ON t1.INSTANCE_UUID = t0.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS t2 ON t2.INSTANCE_UUID = t0.UUID WHERE t0.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge'), ImageTypeCacheMemoryStats AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name, ism.value AS image_type_value, COUNT(iic.id) AS cache_record_count, SUM(i.memory_mb) AS total_memory_mb FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON ism.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS iic ON iic.instance_uuid = i.uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, ism.value) SELECT fid.UUID AS instance_uuid, fid.HOSTNAME, fid.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, fid.NETWORK_INFO, fid.window_column AS az_rank_by_instance_id, itcms.display_name, itcms.image_type_value, itcms.cache_record_count, itcms.total_memory_mb FROM ForgeInstanceDetails AS fid JOIN ImageTypeCacheMemoryStats AS itcms ON fid.UUID = itcms.instance_uuid ORDER BY fid.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, fid.window_column, fid.UUID

WITH TopCompletedMigrations AS (SELECT i.display_name, COUNT(m.id) AS migrations FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.display_name ORDER BY migrations DESC LIMIT 10), ShelvedOffloadedHighMemory AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, SUM(i.MEMORY_MB) AS total_memory_mb FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME HAVING SUM(i.MEMORY_MB) > 50000) SELECT tcm.display_name, tcm.migrations AS completed_migrations, sohm.total_memory_mb FROM TopCompletedMigrations AS tcm JOIN ShelvedOffloadedHighMemory AS sohm ON tcm.display_name = sohm.display_name ORDER BY tcm.migrations DESC
WITH CompletedMigrations AS (SELECT m.status, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, i.display_name, ism.key, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.instance_uuid ORDER BY m.instance_uuid) AS window_column FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed'), ActiveInstancesWithFlavor AS (SELECT i.uuid, i.display_name, i.memory_mb, i.vcpus, ie.flavor, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY i.project_id ORDER BY i.project_id) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0), CombinedInstanceDetails AS (SELECT ai.uuid AS instance_uuid, ai.display_name, ai.memory_mb, ai.vcpus, ai.flavor, cm.status AS migration_status, cm.source_compute, cm.dest_compute, cm.key AS system_metadata_key, cm.window_column AS migration_dense_rank, ai.window_column AS project_dense_rank FROM ActiveInstancesWithFlavor AS ai JOIN CompletedMigrations AS cm ON ai.display_name = cm.display_name) SELECT instance_uuid, display_name, memory_mb, vcpus, flavor, migration_status, source_compute, dest_compute, system_metadata_key, migration_dense_rank, project_dense_rank FROM CombinedInstanceDetails ORDER BY instance_uuid, system_metadata_key
WITH q1 AS (SELECT m.status AS migration_status, i.vm_state AS instance_vm_state, COUNT(m.id) AS migration_count, MIN(i.memory_mb) AS min_instance_memory_mb FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE ism.key = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY m.status, i.vm_state), q2 AS (SELECT i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, m.STATUS AS migration_status, COUNT(i.ID) AS instance_count, MIN(m.MEMORY_TOTAL) AS min_memory_total FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.HIDDEN = 0 GROUP BY i.VM_STATE, m.STATUS) SELECT q1.migration_status, q1.instance_vm_state, q1.migration_count, q1.min_instance_memory_mb, q2.instance_count, q2.min_memory_total FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.migration_status = q2.migration_status AND q1.instance_vm_state = q2.vm_state ORDER BY q1.migration_count DESC
WITH CompletedMigrationsWithMetadata AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ism.KEY ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed'), Flare3InstancesMetadataRunningSum AS (SELECT i.UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, ism.KEY, SUM(i.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY i.ID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3') SELECT c.migration_id, f.UUID AS instance_uuid, c.instance_display_name, c.metadata_key, c.metadata_value, c.window_column AS completed_migrations_row_number_window, f.window_column AS flare3_instance_id_running_sum_window FROM CompletedMigrationsWithMetadata AS c JOIN Flare3InstancesMetadataRunningSum AS f ON c.instance_display_name = f.DISPLAY_NAME AND c.metadata_key = f.KEY ORDER BY c.migration_id, c.metadata_key
WITH vm_state_geom_mean_migrated_mem AS (SELECT i.vm_state, EXP(AVG(LN(m.memory_total))) AS geometric_mean_memory_total FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON i.uuid = c.instance_uuid WHERE m.memory_total > 0 AND c.network_info <> '' GROUP BY i.vm_state ORDER BY geometric_mean_memory_total DESC), flare3_instances_ranked_by_mem AS (SELECT i.UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.VM_STATE, i.MEMORY_MB, c.NETWORK_INFO, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY i.VM_STATE ORDER BY i.MEMORY_MB DESC) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS c ON i.UUID = c.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3') SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT fir.UUID) AS flare3_instance_count_with_geom_mean_migration_mem, AVG(vsgmmm.geometric_mean_memory_total) AS avg_geometric_mean_memory_total_across_matching_vm_states FROM flare3_instances_ranked_by_mem AS fir JOIN vm_state_geom_mean_migrated_mem AS vsgmmm ON fir.VM_STATE = vsgmmm.vm_state WHERE fir.NETWORK_INFO <> ''
WITH VmStateGeometricMeanMemory AS (SELECT i.vm_state, EXP(AVG(LN(i.memory_mb))) AS geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.memory_mb > 0 AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.vm_state ORDER BY geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC), Flare3NonDeletedInstances AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.POWER_STATE AS power_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, MAX(i.ID) OVER (ORDER BY i.ID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_instance_id_next_2 FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3') SELECT f.instance_uuid, f.display_name, f.vm_state, f.power_state, f.memory_mb, f.vcpus, f.instance_extra_id, f.max_instance_id_next_2, v.geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM Flare3NonDeletedInstances AS f JOIN VmStateGeometricMeanMemory AS v ON f.vm_state = v.vm_state WHERE v.geometric_mean_memory_mb > 0 ORDER BY v.geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC, f.instance_uuid
WITH q1 AS (SELECT i.uuid, i.display_name, i.vm_state, i.memory_mb, c.network_info, AVG(i.memory_mb) OVER (PARTITION BY i.vm_state ORDER BY i.memory_mb ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON i.uuid = c.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3' ORDER BY i.memory_mb), q2 AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ism.KEY ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed') SELECT q2.migration_id, q1.uuid AS instance_uuid, q1.display_name, q1.vm_state, q1.memory_mb, q1.network_info, q1.window_column AS avg_memory_mb_window, q2.metadata_key, q2.metadata_value, q2.window_column AS metadata_row_number_window FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.display_name = q2.instance_display_name ORDER BY q2.migration_id, q1.memory_mb, q2.metadata_key
WITH ForgeWindowRanks AS (SELECT i.uuid, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY i.memory_mb ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS ic ON i.uuid = ic.instance_uuid WHERE i.display_name = 'forge'), CompletedMigrationStats AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name AS instance_display_name, i.availability_zone AS availability_zone, COUNT(m.id) AS completed_migration_count, SUM(m.memory_processed) AS total_memory_processed FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND m.memory_processed > 0 GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, i.availability_zone) SELECT cms.instance_uuid, cms.instance_display_name, cms.availability_zone, fwr.window_column AS dense_rank_by_memory_window, cms.completed_migration_count, cms.total_memory_processed FROM CompletedMigrationStats AS cms JOIN ForgeWindowRanks AS fwr ON cms.instance_uuid = fwr.uuid ORDER BY cms.completed_migration_count DESC, cms.total_memory_processed DESC
WITH InstanceNetworkWindow AS (SELECT i.uuid, i.display_name, i.vm_state, i.memory_mb, c.network_info, AVG(i.memory_mb) OVER (PARTITION BY i.vm_state ORDER BY i.memory_mb ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON i.uuid = c.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3' ORDER BY i.memory_mb), InstanceMetadataAgg AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name, i.key_name, COUNT(ism.id) AS metadata_entry_count, SUM(i.vcpus) AS total_vcpus FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3' GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, i.key_name) SELECT nw.uuid AS instance_uuid, nw.display_name, nw.vm_state, nw.memory_mb, nw.network_info, nw.window_column AS avg_memory_mb_window, ma.key_name, ma.metadata_entry_count, ma.total_vcpus FROM InstanceNetworkWindow AS nw JOIN InstanceMetadataAgg AS ma ON nw.uuid = ma.instance_uuid ORDER BY nw.memory_mb, nw.uuid
WITH TopMigrationInstances AS (SELECT i.display_name, COUNT(m.id) AS migrations FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.display_name ORDER BY migrations DESC LIMIT 10), Flare3InstanceDetails AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.POWER_STATE AS power_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, MAX(i.ID) OVER (ORDER BY i.ID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_instance_id_next_2 FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3') SELECT fid.instance_uuid, fid.display_name, fid.vm_state, fid.power_state, fid.memory_mb, fid.vcpus, fid.instance_extra_id, fid.max_instance_id_next_2, tmi.migrations AS completed_migrations_for_display_name FROM Flare3InstanceDetails AS fid JOIN TopMigrationInstances AS tmi ON fid.display_name = tmi.display_name
WITH ForgeInstanceDetails AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.HOSTNAME AS hostname, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, iic.NETWORK_INFO AS network_info, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (ORDER BY i.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge' AND i.DELETED = 0), CompletedMigrationMemoryByState AS (SELECT i.vm_state, EXP(AVG(LN(i.memory_mb))) AS geometric_mean FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.memory_mb > 0 GROUP BY i.vm_state ORDER BY geometric_mean DESC) SELECT f.instance_uuid, f.hostname, f.vm_state, f.metadata_key, f.metadata_value, f.network_info, f.window_column, c.geometric_mean AS completed_migration_geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM ForgeInstanceDetails AS f JOIN CompletedMigrationMemoryByState AS c ON c.vm_state = f.vm_state ORDER BY f.vm_state, f.instance_uuid, f.metadata_key
WITH instance_zone_metadata_rank AS (SELECT t1.UUID AS instance_uuid, t2.VALUE AS metadata_value, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY t1.MEMORY_MB ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS t0 JOIN INSTANCES AS t1 ON t0.INSTANCE_UUID = t1.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS t2 ON t1.UUID = t2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE t1.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND t2.KEY = 'image_image_type'), completed_migrations_memory AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.HOSTNAME AS instance_hostname, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE AS migration_source_compute, m.DEST_COMPUTE AS migration_dest_compute, m.DEST_HOST AS migration_dest_host, m.MIGRATION_TYPE AS migration_type, m.MEMORY_TOTAL AS memory_total, m.MEMORY_PROCESSED AS memory_processed, m.MEMORY_REMAINING AS memory_remaining, MIN(m.MEMORY_REMAINING) OVER (PARTITION BY m.INSTANCE_UUID ORDER BY m.INSTANCE_UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed'), flare3_completed_migrations_with_image_type AS (SELECT cmm.instance_uuid, cmm.instance_display_name, cmm.instance_hostname, cmm.migration_source_compute, cmm.migration_dest_compute, cmm.migration_dest_host, cmm.migration_type, cmm.memory_total, cmm.memory_processed, cmm.memory_remaining, cmm.window_column AS min_memory_remaining_within_instance_uuid FROM completed_migrations_memory AS cmm JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON i.UUID = cmm.instance_uuid JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type') SELECT COUNT(*) AS flare3_completed_migrations_with_image_type_count FROM flare3_completed_migrations_with_image_type
WITH ForgeInstancesWindow AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.HOSTNAME AS hostname, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (ORDER BY ie.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge'), CompletedMigrationsWindow AS (SELECT t0.STATUS AS migration_status, t1.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, t2.FLAVOR AS instance_flavor_json, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY t1.DISPLAY_NAME ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS t0 JOIN INSTANCES AS t1 ON t0.INSTANCE_UUID = t1.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS t2 ON t1.UUID = t2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE t0.STATUS = 'completed'), ForgeCompletedMigrationCounts AS (SELECT m.INSTANCE_UUID AS instance_uuid, COUNT(*) AS completed_migration_count FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge' GROUP BY m.INSTANCE_UUID) SELECT fiw.instance_uuid, fiw.hostname, fiw.vm_state, fiw.memory_mb, fiw.instance_extra_id, fiw.window_column AS forge_min_memory_mb_window, cmw.migration_status, cmw.instance_flavor_json, cmw.window_column AS completed_migrations_row_number_window, fcmc.completed_migration_count FROM ForgeInstancesWindow AS fiw JOIN ForgeCompletedMigrationCounts AS fcmc ON fcmc.instance_uuid = fiw.instance_uuid JOIN CompletedMigrationsWindow AS cmw ON cmw.instance_display_name = 'forge' WHERE fcmc.completed_migration_count >= 1 ORDER BY fiw.instance_extra_id
WITH subq1 AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, SUM(i.ROOT_GB) AS total_root_gb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME HAVING SUM(i.ROOT_GB) > 50), subq2 AS (SELECT i.UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, m.STATUS, m.DEST_COMPUTE, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed') SELECT subq2.UUID, subq2.DISPLAY_NAME, subq1.total_root_gb, subq2.STATUS, subq2.DEST_COMPUTE, subq2.window_column FROM subq2 JOIN subq1 ON subq2.DISPLAY_NAME = subq1.DISPLAY_NAME ORDER BY subq1.total_root_gb DESC, subq2.window_column ASC
WITH ImageTypeMetadataInstances AS (SELECT t1.DISPLAY_NAME, t0.VALUE AS metadata_value, t2.NETWORK_INFO, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY t0.VALUE ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS t0 JOIN INSTANCES AS t1 ON t0.INSTANCE_UUID = t1.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS t2 ON t1.UUID = t2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE t0.KEY = 'image_image_type'), ForgeInstanceDetails AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.HOSTNAME AS hostname, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (ORDER BY ie.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge') SELECT f.instance_uuid, f.hostname, f.vm_state, f.memory_mb, f.instance_extra_id, f.window_column AS forge_min_memory_window, im.metadata_value, im.NETWORK_INFO, im.window_column AS image_type_row_number_window FROM ForgeInstanceDetails AS f JOIN ImageTypeMetadataInstances AS im ON im.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge' ORDER BY f.instance_extra_id, f.instance_uuid
WITH InstanceZoneStateStats AS (SELECT ins.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, ins.VM_STATE, COUNT(ins.UUID) AS instance_count, MIN(ins.CREATED_AT) AS earliest_created_at FROM INSTANCES AS ins JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = ins.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = ins.UUID WHERE ins.DELETED = 0 GROUP BY ins.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, ins.VM_STATE), CompletedMigrationWindow AS (SELECT i.uuid, i.display_name, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, m.status, MIN(i.memory_mb) OVER (ORDER BY m.id ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN migrations AS m ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed'), CompletedMigrationZoneState AS (SELECT ins.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, ins.VM_STATE, COUNT(DISTINCT cmw.uuid) AS completed_migration_instance_count, AVG(cmw.window_column) AS avg_window_min_memory_mb FROM CompletedMigrationWindow AS cmw JOIN INSTANCES AS ins ON ins.UUID = cmw.uuid WHERE ins.DELETED = 0 GROUP BY ins.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, ins.VM_STATE) SELECT izss.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, izss.VM_STATE, izss.instance_count, izss.earliest_created_at, cmzs.completed_migration_instance_count, cmzs.avg_window_min_memory_mb FROM InstanceZoneStateStats AS izss JOIN CompletedMigrationZoneState AS cmzs ON izss.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = cmzs.AVAILABILITY_ZONE AND izss.VM_STATE = cmzs.VM_STATE ORDER BY izss.instance_count DESC
WITH CompletedShelvedMigrations AS (SELECT m.status, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, m.instance_uuid, i.display_name, i.memory_mb, ism.key, ism.value, MIN(i.memory_mb) OVER (ORDER BY m.id ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.vm_state = 'shelved_offloaded'), ForgeInstances AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.HOSTNAME AS hostname, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (ORDER BY ie.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge') SELECT csm.status AS migration_status, csm.source_compute, csm.dest_compute, csm.instance_uuid, csm.display_name, csm.memory_mb, fi.hostname, fi.vm_state, fi.instance_extra_id, csm.key AS system_metadata_key, csm.value AS system_metadata_value, csm.window_column AS migration_window_min_memory_mb, fi.window_column AS instance_extra_window_min_memory_mb FROM CompletedShelvedMigrations AS csm JOIN ForgeInstances AS fi ON csm.instance_uuid = fi.instance_uuid ORDER BY csm.instance_uuid, fi.instance_extra_id
WITH InstanceCacheDetails AS (SELECT i.display_name, i.vm_state, i.memory_mb, c.network_info, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY i.project_id ORDER BY i.project_id) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON i.uuid = c.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0), ShelvedOffloadedMetadata AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.PROJECT_ID AS project_id, i.POWER_STATE AS power_state, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, MAX(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (ORDER BY i.UUID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_memory_mb_next_2 FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded'), CombinedInstanceViews AS (SELECT sm.instance_uuid AS instance_uuid, sm.display_name AS display_name, sm.project_id AS project_id, sm.power_state AS power_state, sm.metadata_key AS metadata_key, sm.metadata_value AS metadata_value, sm.max_memory_mb_next_2 AS max_memory_mb_next_2, icd.vm_state AS vm_state, icd.memory_mb AS memory_mb, icd.network_info AS network_info, icd.window_column AS window_column, 'Shelved Offloaded With Metadata' AS reason FROM ShelvedOffloadedMetadata AS sm JOIN InstanceCacheDetails AS icd ON icd.display_name = sm.display_name AND icd.vm_state = 'shelved_offloaded' UNION ALL SELECT NULL AS instance_uuid, icd.display_name AS display_name, NULL AS project_id, NULL AS power_state, NULL AS metadata_key, NULL AS metadata_value, NULL AS max_memory_mb_next_2, icd.vm_state AS vm_state, icd.memory_mb AS memory_mb, icd.network_info AS network_info, icd.window_column AS window_column, 'Non-deleted Instance Cache Details' AS reason FROM InstanceCacheDetails AS icd) SELECT * FROM CombinedInstanceViews ORDER BY display_name, reason
WITH RuleWindow AS (SELECT sg.NAME AS security_group_name, sgr.PROTOCOL, sgr.FROM_PORT, SUM(sgr.FROM_PORT) OVER (PARTITION BY sgr.PROTOCOL ORDER BY sgr.FROM_PORT) AS window_column FROM SECURITY_GROUPS AS sg JOIN SECURITY_GROUP_RULES AS sgr ON sg.ID = sgr.PARENT_GROUP_ID WHERE sg.NAME = 'aurum-xenon'), TopRuleCounts AS (SELECT sg.NAME AS security_group_name, COUNT(sgr.ID) AS rule_count FROM SECURITY_GROUPS AS sg JOIN SECURITY_GROUP_RULES AS sgr ON sg.ID = sgr.PARENT_GROUP_ID WHERE sg.DELETED = 0 AND sgr.DELETED = 0 GROUP BY sg.NAME ORDER BY rule_count DESC LIMIT 10) SELECT rw.security_group_name, rw.PROTOCOL, rw.FROM_PORT, rw.window_column, trc.rule_count, ROUND(CAST(rw.window_column AS REAL) / NULLIF(trc.rule_count, 0), 2) AS normalized_running_from_port_per_rule FROM RuleWindow AS rw JOIN TopRuleCounts AS trc ON rw.security_group_name = trc.security_group_name ORDER BY normalized_running_from_port_per_rule DESC, rw.PROTOCOL, rw.FROM_PORT
WITH ShelvedCompletedMigrations AS (SELECT m.status, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, m.instance_uuid, i.display_name, i.memory_mb, ism.key, ism.value, MIN(i.memory_mb) OVER (ORDER BY m.id ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.vm_state = 'shelved_offloaded'), MigrationStatusVmStateStats AS (SELECT m.status AS migration_status, i.vm_state AS instance_vm_state, COUNT(m.id) AS migration_count, MIN(i.memory_mb) AS min_instance_memory_mb FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE ism.key = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY m.status, i.vm_state) SELECT scm.status, scm.source_compute, scm.dest_compute, scm.instance_uuid, scm.display_name, scm.memory_mb, scm.key, scm.value, scm.window_column, msvs.migration_count, msvs.min_instance_memory_mb FROM ShelvedCompletedMigrations AS scm JOIN MigrationStatusVmStateStats AS msvs ON msvs.migration_status = scm.status AND msvs.instance_vm_state = 'shelved_offloaded'
WITH AZInstanceWindow AS (SELECT i.uuid, i.display_name, i.vm_state, i.memory_mb, c.network_info, AVG(i.memory_mb) OVER (PARTITION BY i.vm_state ORDER BY i.memory_mb ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON i.uuid = c.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3' ORDER BY i.memory_mb), NotDeletedInstanceDenseRank AS (SELECT i.display_name, i.vm_state, i.memory_mb, c.network_info, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY i.project_id ORDER BY i.project_id) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON i.uuid = c.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0), NotDeletedInstanceDenseRankWithUUID AS (SELECT i.uuid, i.display_name, i.vm_state, i.memory_mb, c.network_info, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY i.project_id ORDER BY i.project_id) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON i.uuid = c.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0) SELECT az.uuid, az.display_name, az.vm_state, az.memory_mb, az.network_info, az.window_column AS Avg_MemoryMB_Window_2_Preceding_2_Following_By_VMState, nd.window_column AS Dense_Rank_By_Project FROM AZInstanceWindow AS az JOIN NotDeletedInstanceDenseRankWithUUID AS nd ON az.uuid = nd.uuid ORDER BY az.memory_mb
WITH az_instances_with_metadata_window AS (SELECT i.display_name, i.availability_zone, i.vm_state, i.memory_mb, i.vcpus, i.uuid, MAX(ism.id) OVER (ORDER BY i.uuid ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_metadata_id_next_2 FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3'), shelved_offloaded_instances_with_network_rank AS (SELECT i.ID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, c.NETWORK_INFO, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE ORDER BY i.ID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS c ON i.UUID = c.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.VM_STATE = 'shelved_offloaded') SELECT COUNT(*) AS flare3_shelved_offloaded_instances_with_metadata_and_network_count FROM az_instances_with_metadata_window AS az JOIN shelved_offloaded_instances_with_network_rank AS so ON az.uuid = (SELECT i2.uuid FROM instances AS i2 WHERE i2.id = so.id AND i2.availability_zone = 'flare3')
WITH MetadataStats AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name, i.key_name, COUNT(ism.id) AS metadata_entry_count, SUM(i.vcpus) AS total_vcpus FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3' GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, i.key_name), NetworkWindow AS (SELECT t1.DISPLAY_NAME, t1.UUID AS instance_uuid, t2.NETWORK_INFO, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY t1.DISPLAY_NAME ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS t0 JOIN INSTANCES AS t1 ON t0.INSTANCE_UUID = t1.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS t2 ON t1.UUID = t2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE t1.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3'), CombinedInstanceDetails AS (SELECT ms.instance_uuid, ms.display_name, ms.key_name, ms.metadata_entry_count, ms.total_vcpus, nw.NETWORK_INFO, nw.window_column FROM MetadataStats AS ms JOIN NetworkWindow AS nw ON ms.instance_uuid = nw.instance_uuid) SELECT * FROM CombinedInstanceDetails ORDER BY display_name, instance_uuid
WITH ForgeSystemMetadata AS (SELECT i.UUID, ism.KEY, ism.VALUE, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ism.KEY ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge'), Flare3InstanceNetworkInfo AS (SELECT t1.DISPLAY_NAME, t1.UUID AS instance_uuid, t2.NETWORK_INFO, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY t1.DISPLAY_NAME ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS t0 JOIN INSTANCES AS t1 ON t0.INSTANCE_UUID = t1.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS t2 ON t1.UUID = t2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE t1.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3') SELECT fn.DISPLAY_NAME, fn.instance_uuid, fsm.KEY AS system_metadata_key, fsm.VALUE AS system_metadata_value, fn.NETWORK_INFO, fsm.window_column AS metadata_window_row_number, fn.window_column AS network_window_row_number FROM Flare3InstanceNetworkInfo AS fn JOIN ForgeSystemMetadata AS fsm ON fn.instance_uuid = fsm.UUID WHERE fn.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge' ORDER BY fn.instance_uuid, fsm.KEY
WITH InstanceDetails AS (SELECT i.UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.VM_STATE, i.MEMORY_MB, c.NETWORK_INFO, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY i.VM_STATE ORDER BY i.MEMORY_MB DESC) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS c ON i.UUID = c.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3'), GeometricMeanMemoryByState AS (SELECT i.vm_state, EXP(AVG(LN(i.memory_mb))) AS geometric_mean FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON i.uuid = c.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 GROUP BY i.vm_state ORDER BY geometric_mean DESC) SELECT id.UUID, id.DISPLAY_NAME, id.VM_STATE, id.MEMORY_MB, id.NETWORK_INFO, id.window_column, gm.geometric_mean FROM InstanceDetails AS id JOIN GeometricMeanMemoryByState AS gm ON id.VM_STATE = gm.vm_state
WITH completed_migrations AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.STATUS AS migration_status, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, m.DEST_HOST, m.INSTANCE_UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.VM_STATE AS instance_vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS instance_memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS instance_vcpus, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, m.MEMORY_REMAINING AS migration_memory_remaining, MIN(m.MEMORY_REMAINING) OVER (PARTITION BY m.DEST_COMPUTE ORDER BY m.DEST_COMPUTE) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed'), high_memory_shelved_display_names AS (SELECT i.display_name, SUM(i.memory_mb) AS total_memory_mb FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.vm_state = 'shelved_offloaded' AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.display_name HAVING SUM(i.memory_mb) > 20000) SELECT cm.migration_id, cm.migration_status, cm.source_compute, cm.dest_compute, cm.dest_host, cm.instance_uuid, cm.instance_display_name, cm.instance_vm_state, cm.instance_memory_mb, cm.instance_vcpus, cm.metadata_key, cm.metadata_value, cm.migration_memory_remaining, cm.window_column, hmsdn.total_memory_mb FROM completed_migrations AS cm JOIN high_memory_shelved_display_names AS hmsdn ON cm.instance_display_name = hmsdn.display_name ORDER BY cm.dest_compute
WITH CompletedMigrationMaxMemory AS (SELECT i.display_name, MAX(m.memory_total) AS max_memory_total, i.created_at FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' GROUP BY i.display_name, i.created_at, m.status HAVING MAX(m.memory_total) > 0), ActiveInstanceMetadataWindow AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.POWER_STATE AS power_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, MAX(ie.ID) OVER (ORDER BY i.UUID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_instance_extra_id_next_2 FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND ism.DELETED = 0), CombinedInstanceReport AS (SELECT aim.instance_uuid, aim.display_name, aim.vm_state, aim.power_state, aim.memory_mb, aim.vcpus, aim.metadata_key, aim.metadata_value, aim.max_instance_extra_id_next_2, cmm.max_memory_total, cmm.created_at AS instance_created_at, 'Has Completed Migration Max Memory' AS reason FROM ActiveInstanceMetadataWindow AS aim JOIN CompletedMigrationMaxMemory AS cmm ON aim.display_name = cmm.display_name UNION ALL SELECT aim.instance_uuid, aim.display_name, aim.vm_state, aim.power_state, aim.memory_mb, aim.vcpus, aim.metadata_key, aim.metadata_value, aim.max_instance_extra_id_next_2, NULL AS max_memory_total, NULL AS instance_created_at, 'Active Instance Metadata Window' AS reason FROM ActiveInstanceMetadataWindow AS aim) SELECT instance_uuid, display_name, vm_state, power_state, memory_mb, vcpus, metadata_key, metadata_value, max_instance_extra_id_next_2, max_memory_total, instance_created_at, reason FROM CombinedInstanceReport ORDER BY display_name, instance_uuid, metadata_key
WITH MigrationWindowMaxMemory AS (SELECT m.id AS migration_id, m.status, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, m.dest_host, i.display_name, i.vm_state, MAX(i.memory_mb) OVER (ORDER BY m.id ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_memory_mb_next_2 FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS ic ON i.uuid = ic.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed'), MigrationMetadataWindowAvgMemory AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.MEMORY_MB, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed') SELECT mwmm.migration_id, mwmm.status, mwmm.source_compute, mwmm.dest_compute, mwmm.dest_host, mwmm.display_name, mwmm.vm_state, mwmm.max_memory_mb_next_2, mmwam.memory_mb, mmwam.metadata_key, mmwam.metadata_value, mmwam.window_column AS avg_memory_mb_status_window_2_pre_2_fol FROM MigrationWindowMaxMemory AS mwmm JOIN MigrationMetadataWindowAvgMemory AS mmwam ON mwmm.migration_id = mmwam.migration_id ORDER BY mwmm.migration_id, mmwam.metadata_key
WITH CompletedMigrationInstanceMetadata AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ism.KEY ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed'), InstanceCountsByAZAndState AS (SELECT ins.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, ins.VM_STATE, COUNT(ins.UUID) AS instance_count, MIN(ins.CREATED_AT) AS earliest_created_at FROM INSTANCES AS ins JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = ins.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = ins.UUID WHERE ins.DELETED = 0 GROUP BY ins.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, ins.VM_STATE) SELECT cmim.migration_id, cmim.instance_display_name, icb.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, icb.VM_STATE, icb.instance_count, icb.earliest_created_at, cmim.metadata_key, cmim.metadata_value, cmim.window_column FROM CompletedMigrationInstanceMetadata AS cmim JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON i.DISPLAY_NAME = cmim.instance_display_name JOIN InstanceCountsByAZAndState AS icb ON icb.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE AND icb.VM_STATE = i.VM_STATE WHERE icb.instance_count >= 2 ORDER BY icb.instance_count DESC, cmim.migration_id, cmim.metadata_key
WITH InstanceStateAZStats AS (SELECT ins.VM_STATE, ins.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, COUNT(ins.UUID) AS instance_count, MIN(ins.MEMORY_MB) AS min_memory_mb FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS ins ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = ins.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ins.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY ins.VM_STATE, ins.AVAILABILITY_ZONE), CompletedMigrationsWithMetadata AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.STATUS AS migration_status, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, m.DEST_HOST, m.INSTANCE_UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.VM_STATE AS instance_vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS instance_memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS instance_vcpus, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, m.MEMORY_REMAINING AS migration_memory_remaining, MIN(m.MEMORY_REMAINING) OVER (PARTITION BY m.DEST_COMPUTE ORDER BY m.DEST_COMPUTE) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed'), CompletedMigrationCountsByStateAZ AS (SELECT i.VM_STATE, i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, COUNT(m.ID) AS completed_migration_count FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.VM_STATE, i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE) SELECT isaz.VM_STATE, isaz.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, isaz.instance_count, isaz.min_memory_mb, cmc.completed_migration_count FROM InstanceStateAZStats AS isaz JOIN CompletedMigrationCountsByStateAZ AS cmc ON isaz.VM_STATE = cmc.VM_STATE AND isaz.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = cmc.AVAILABILITY_ZONE WHERE (CAST(cmc.completed_migration_count * 1.0 AS REAL) / isaz.instance_count) > 0.75
WITH CompletedMigrationDetails AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.INSTANCE_UUID, m.MIGRATION_TYPE, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, m.DEST_HOST, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.VM_STATE, i.MEMORY_MB, i.VCPUS, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, m.MEMORY_TOTAL, MIN(m.MEMORY_TOTAL) OVER (PARTITION BY m.MIGRATION_TYPE ORDER BY m.MIGRATION_TYPE) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed'), NonDeletedInstanceWindowStats AS (SELECT i.uuid, i.display_name, i.vm_state, i.power_state, i.memory_mb, i.vcpus, MAX(ie.id) OVER (ORDER BY i.uuid ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_instance_extra_id_next2 FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0) SELECT cmd.migration_id, cmd.INSTANCE_UUID, cmd.MIGRATION_TYPE, cmd.SOURCE_COMPUTE, cmd.DEST_COMPUTE, cmd.DEST_HOST, cmd.DISPLAY_NAME AS migration_instance_display_name, cmd.VM_STATE AS migration_instance_vm_state, cmd.MEMORY_MB AS migration_instance_memory_mb, cmd.VCPUS AS migration_instance_vcpus, cmd.instance_extra_id AS migration_instance_extra_id, cmd.MEMORY_TOTAL, cmd.window_column AS min_memory_total_within_migration_type, nis.power_state AS nondeleted_instance_power_state, nis.max_instance_extra_id_next2 FROM CompletedMigrationDetails AS cmd JOIN NonDeletedInstanceWindowStats AS nis ON cmd.INSTANCE_UUID = nis.uuid ORDER BY cmd.MIGRATION_TYPE, cmd.migration_id
WITH InstanceCacheDetails AS (SELECT i.display_name, i.vm_state, i.memory_mb, c.network_info, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY i.project_id ORDER BY i.project_id) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON i.uuid = c.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0), InstanceFlavorAndMetadata AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.FLAVOR AS flavor, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY i.MEMORY_MB ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3') SELECT ifm.instance_uuid, ifm.display_name, icd.vm_state, ifm.memory_mb, ifm.vcpus, ifm.flavor, ifm.metadata_key, ifm.metadata_value, icd.network_info, icd.window_column AS project_dense_rank, ifm.window_column AS metadata_key_memory_avg_window, ROUND(CAST(ifm.memory_mb AS REAL) / NULLIF(ifm.vcpus, 0), 2) AS memory_mb_per_vcpu FROM InstanceFlavorAndMetadata AS ifm JOIN InstanceCacheDetails AS icd ON icd.display_name = ifm.display_name ORDER BY memory_mb_per_vcpu DESC
