WITH q1 AS (SELECT m.id AS migration_id, m.status, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, m.dest_host, i.display_name, i.vm_state, MAX(i.memory_mb) OVER (ORDER BY m.id ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_memory_mb_next_2 FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS ic ON i.uuid = ic.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed'), q2 AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.MEMORY_MB, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed') SELECT q1.migration_id, q1.status, q1.source_compute, q1.dest_compute, q1.dest_host, q1.display_name, q1.vm_state, q1.max_memory_mb_next_2, q2.memory_mb, q2.metadata_key, q2.metadata_value, q2.window_column AS avg_memory_mb_2_pre_2_fol FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.migration_id = q2.migration_id ORDER BY q1.migration_id
WITH TcpRuleStats AS (SELECT sg.ID AS security_group_id, sg.NAME AS security_group_name, sg.DESCRIPTION AS security_group_description, COUNT(sgr.ID) AS rule_count, SUM(sgr.TO_PORT) AS total_to_port FROM SECURITY_GROUPS AS sg JOIN SECURITY_GROUP_RULES AS sgr ON sg.ID = sgr.PARENT_GROUP_ID WHERE sgr.PROTOCOL = 'tcp' GROUP BY sg.ID, sg.NAME, sg.DESCRIPTION), GeoMeanPortRange AS (SELECT sg.NAME AS security_group_name, EXP(AVG(LN(sgr.TO_PORT - sgr.FROM_PORT + 1))) AS geometric_mean_port_range_size FROM SECURITY_GROUPS AS sg JOIN SECURITY_GROUP_RULES AS sgr ON sg.ID = sgr.PARENT_GROUP_ID WHERE sgr.TO_PORT >= sgr.FROM_PORT GROUP BY sg.NAME ORDER BY geometric_mean_port_range_size DESC), CombinedSecurityGroupMetrics AS (SELECT t.security_group_id, t.security_group_name, t.security_group_description, t.rule_count, t.total_to_port, g.geometric_mean_port_range_size FROM TcpRuleStats AS t JOIN GeoMeanPortRange AS g ON t.security_group_name = g.security_group_name) SELECT security_group_id, security_group_name, security_group_description, rule_count, total_to_port, geometric_mean_port_range_size FROM CombinedSecurityGroupMetrics ORDER BY security_group_name
WITH CompletedMigrations AS (SELECT m.status, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, i.display_name, ism.key, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.instance_uuid ORDER BY m.instance_uuid) AS window_column FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed'), ForgeInstances AS (SELECT t0.UUID, t0.HOSTNAME, t0.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, t2.NETWORK_INFO, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY t0.AVAILABILITY_ZONE ORDER BY t0.ID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS t0 JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS t1 ON t1.INSTANCE_UUID = t0.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS t2 ON t2.INSTANCE_UUID = t0.UUID WHERE t0.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge') SELECT cm.status AS migration_status, cm.source_compute, cm.dest_compute, cm.display_name, cm.key AS instance_system_metadata_key, cm.window_column AS migration_dense_rank_per_instance_uuid, fi.UUID AS instance_uuid, fi.HOSTNAME, fi.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, fi.NETWORK_INFO, fi.window_column AS instance_rank_within_availability_zone FROM CompletedMigrations AS cm JOIN ForgeInstances AS fi ON fi.UUID = (SELECT i2.uuid FROM instances AS i2 WHERE i2.display_name = cm.display_name LIMIT 1)
WITH InstanceDetails AS (SELECT i.id AS instance_id, i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.user_id, i.project_id, i.display_name, i.vm_state, i.memory_mb, i.vcpus, c.id AS info_cache_id, MAX(i.id) OVER (ORDER BY i.id ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_instance_id_next_2 FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON i.uuid = c.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND c.deleted = 0), InstanceMetadataAgg AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name, i.availability_zone, COUNT(ism.id) AS metadata_entry_count, SUM(i.vcpus) AS total_vcpus FROM instance_extra AS ie JOIN instances AS i ON ie.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, i.availability_zone) SELECT idt.instance_id, idt.instance_uuid, idt.user_id, idt.project_id, idt.display_name, idt.vm_state, idt.memory_mb, idt.vcpus, idt.info_cache_id, idt.max_instance_id_next_2, ima.availability_zone, ima.metadata_entry_count, ima.total_vcpus FROM InstanceDetails AS idt JOIN InstanceMetadataAgg AS ima ON idt.instance_uuid = ima.instance_uuid ORDER BY idt.instance_id
WITH AZInstances AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3'), NotDeletedInstances AS (SELECT i.uuid, i.display_name, ism.key, ism.value, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY i.uuid ORDER BY i.uuid) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3'), CombinedInstanceMetadata AS (SELECT instance_uuid, display_name, vm_state, memory_mb, vcpus, metadata_key, metadata_value, window_column, 'Availability Zone flare3' AS reason FROM AZInstances UNION ALL SELECT ndi.uuid AS instance_uuid, ndi.display_name AS display_name, NULL AS vm_state, NULL AS memory_mb, NULL AS vcpus, ndi.key AS metadata_key, ndi.value AS metadata_value, ndi.window_column AS window_column, 'Not Deleted (DELETED = 0)' AS reason FROM NotDeletedInstances AS ndi) SELECT * FROM CombinedInstanceMetadata ORDER BY instance_uuid, metadata_key, reason
WITH CompletedMigrationsWithMetadata AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.STATUS AS migration_status, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, m.DEST_HOST, m.INSTANCE_UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.VM_STATE AS instance_vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS instance_memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS instance_vcpus, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, m.MEMORY_REMAINING AS migration_memory_remaining, MIN(m.MEMORY_REMAINING) OVER (PARTITION BY m.DEST_COMPUTE ORDER BY m.DEST_COMPUTE) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed'), ShelvedOffloadedHighMemoryDisplayNames AS (SELECT i.display_name, SUM(i.memory_mb) AS total_memory_mb FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.vm_state = 'shelved_offloaded' AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.display_name HAVING SUM(i.memory_mb) > 20000) SELECT cmwm.DEST_COMPUTE, COUNT(DISTINCT cmwm.migration_id) AS completed_migration_count, MIN(cmwm.window_column) AS min_migration_memory_remaining_in_dest_compute, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT sohdn.display_name) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS Has_Shelved_Offloaded_High_Memory_DisplayName FROM CompletedMigrationsWithMetadata AS cmwm LEFT JOIN ShelvedOffloadedHighMemoryDisplayNames AS sohdn ON cmwm.instance_display_name = sohdn.display_name GROUP BY cmwm.DEST_COMPUTE HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT cmwm.migration_id) >= 2 ORDER BY completed_migration_count DESC, cmwm.DEST_COMPUTE
WITH q1 AS (SELECT i.id AS instance_id, i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.user_id, i.project_id, i.display_name, i.vm_state, i.memory_mb, i.vcpus, c.id AS info_cache_id, MAX(i.id) OVER (ORDER BY i.id ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_instance_id_next_2 FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON i.uuid = c.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND c.deleted = 0), q2 AS (SELECT i.uuid, i.display_name, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, m.status, MIN(i.memory_mb) OVER (ORDER BY m.id ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN migrations AS m ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed') SELECT q1.instance_id, q1.instance_uuid, q1.user_id, q1.project_id, q1.display_name, q1.vm_state, q1.memory_mb, q1.vcpus, q1.info_cache_id, q1.max_instance_id_next_2, q2.source_compute, q2.dest_compute, q2.status AS migration_status, q2.window_column AS min_instance_memory_mb_window FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.instance_uuid = q2.uuid ORDER BY q1.instance_id
WITH CompletedMigrations AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, SUM(m.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed'), ForgeInstanceMetadataExtra AS (SELECT t1.UUID AS instance_uuid, t2.KEY AS metadata_key, t2.VALUE AS metadata_value, t0.CREATED_AT AS extra_created_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY t2.KEY ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS t0 JOIN INSTANCES AS t1 ON t0.INSTANCE_UUID = t1.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS t2 ON t1.UUID = t2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE t1.DISPLAY_NAME = 'forge'), CompletedMigrationsForForge AS (SELECT cm.migration_id, cm.instance_display_name, cm.metadata_key, cm.window_column AS completed_migrations_running_sum, fime.instance_uuid, fime.metadata_value, fime.extra_created_at, fime.window_column AS forge_metadata_row_number FROM CompletedMigrations AS cm JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON i.DISPLAY_NAME = cm.instance_display_name JOIN ForgeInstanceMetadataExtra AS fime ON fime.instance_uuid = i.UUID AND fime.metadata_key = cm.metadata_key) SELECT migration_id, instance_display_name, metadata_key, completed_migrations_running_sum, instance_uuid, metadata_value, extra_created_at, forge_metadata_row_number FROM CompletedMigrationsForForge
WITH RuleWindow AS (SELECT sg.NAME AS security_group_name, sgr.PROTOCOL, sgr.FROM_PORT, sgr.TO_PORT, sgr.CIDR, AVG(sgr.FROM_PORT) OVER (PARTITION BY sgr.PROTOCOL ORDER BY sgr.FROM_PORT ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM SECURITY_GROUPS AS sg JOIN SECURITY_GROUP_RULES AS sgr ON sg.ID = sgr.PARENT_GROUP_ID WHERE sg.NAME = 'aurum-xenon' ORDER BY sgr.FROM_PORT), TcpPortCoverage AS (SELECT sg.NAME AS security_group_name, SUM(sgr.TO_PORT - sgr.FROM_PORT + 1) AS total_ports_covered FROM SECURITY_GROUPS AS sg JOIN SECURITY_GROUP_RULES AS sgr ON sg.ID = sgr.PARENT_GROUP_ID WHERE sgr.PROTOCOL = 'tcp' GROUP BY sg.NAME HAVING SUM(sgr.TO_PORT - sgr.FROM_PORT + 1) > 100) SELECT rw.security_group_name, rw.PROTOCOL, rw.FROM_PORT, rw.TO_PORT, rw.CIDR, rw.window_column AS avg_from_port_window_2_pre_2_fol, tpc.total_ports_covered, ROUND(CAST(rw.window_column AS REAL) / NULLIF(tpc.total_ports_covered, 0), 6) AS normalized_window_avg_to_total_ports_ratio FROM RuleWindow AS rw JOIN TcpPortCoverage AS tpc ON rw.security_group_name = tpc.security_group_name WHERE rw.security_group_name = 'aurum-xenon' ORDER BY normalized_window_avg_to_total_ports_ratio DESC, rw.FROM_PORT
WITH MigrationDetails AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.INSTANCE_UUID, m.MIGRATION_TYPE, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, m.DEST_HOST, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.VM_STATE, i.MEMORY_MB, i.VCPUS, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, m.MEMORY_TOTAL, MIN(m.MEMORY_TOTAL) OVER (PARTITION BY m.MIGRATION_TYPE ORDER BY m.MIGRATION_TYPE) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), MigrationRanks AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic ON i.UUID = ic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND ic.DELETED = 0 AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT md.MIGRATION_TYPE, COUNT(DISTINCT md.migration_id) AS Completed_Migration_Count, MIN(md.window_column) AS Min_Memory_Total_Within_Migration_Type FROM MigrationDetails AS md JOIN MigrationRanks AS mr ON md.migration_id = mr.migration_id GROUP BY md.MIGRATION_TYPE HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT md.migration_id) >= 2 ORDER BY Completed_Migration_Count DESC, md.MIGRATION_TYPE
WITH az_host_instances AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, ie.FLAVOR AS flavor, iic.NETWORK_INFO AS network_info, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE ORDER BY i.MEMORY_MB) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' ORDER BY i.MEMORY_MB), non_deleted_host_instances AS (SELECT i.id AS instance_id, i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.user_id, i.project_id, i.display_name, i.vm_state, i.memory_mb, i.vcpus, c.id AS info_cache_id, MAX(i.id) OVER (ORDER BY i.id ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_instance_id_next_2 FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON i.uuid = c.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND c.deleted = 0 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12') SELECT COUNT(*) AS matching_instance_count, AVG(az.window_column) AS avg_running_avg_memory_mb_in_az, AVG(nd.max_instance_id_next_2) AS avg_max_instance_id_next_2 FROM az_host_instances AS az JOIN non_deleted_host_instances AS nd ON az.instance_uuid = nd.instance_uuid
WITH MigrationWindow AS (SELECT m.id AS migration_id, m.status, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, m.dest_host, i.display_name, i.vm_state, MAX(i.memory_mb) OVER (ORDER BY m.id ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_memory_mb_next_2 FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS ic ON i.uuid = ic.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12'), InstanceMetadataWindow AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.FLAVOR AS flavor, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY i.MEMORY_MB ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT imw.metadata_key, COUNT(DISTINCT mw.migration_id) AS completed_migrations_on_blaze8_12, AVG(imw.window_column) AS avg_instance_memory_window_by_key, MAX(mw.max_memory_mb_next_2) AS max_of_migration_next2_memory_window FROM MigrationWindow AS mw JOIN instances AS i ON i.display_name = mw.display_name AND i.vm_state = mw.vm_state AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' JOIN InstanceMetadataWindow AS imw ON imw.instance_uuid = i.uuid GROUP BY imw.metadata_key ORDER BY completed_migrations_on_blaze8_12 DESC, imw.metadata_key
WITH InstanceExtraStats AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name, iic.network_info, COUNT(ie.id) AS extra_record_count, SUM(i.memory_mb) AS total_memory_mb FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON ie.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS iic ON iic.instance_uuid = i.uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND i.availability_zone = 'flare3' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, iic.network_info), CompletedMigrationsOnHost AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS ic ON i.UUID = ic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND ic.DELETED = 0 AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT ies.instance_uuid, ies.display_name, ies.network_info, ies.extra_record_count, ies.total_memory_mb, cm.migration_id, cm.source_compute, cm.dest_compute, cm.window_column AS migration_rank_within_completed_status FROM InstanceExtraStats AS ies JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = ies.instance_uuid JOIN CompletedMigrationsOnHost AS cm ON cm.migration_id = m.id ORDER BY ies.instance_uuid, cm.migration_id
WITH MigrationCounts AS (SELECT i.display_name, COUNT(m.id) AS migrations FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.display_name ORDER BY migrations DESC LIMIT 10), GeometricMeanMemory AS (SELECT i.display_name, EXP(AVG(LN(i.memory_mb))) AS geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON ism.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS iic ON i.uuid = iic.instance_uuid WHERE i.memory_mb > 0 AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.display_name ORDER BY geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC) SELECT mc.display_name, mc.migrations, gmm.geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM MigrationCounts AS mc JOIN GeometricMeanMemory AS gmm ON mc.display_name = gmm.display_name ORDER BY mc.migrations DESC
WITH InstanceMetadataAndWindowMax AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.POWER_STATE AS power_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, MAX(ie.ID) OVER (ORDER BY i.UUID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_instance_extra_id_next_2 FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND ism.DELETED = 0 AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), InstanceFlavorAndDenseRank AS (SELECT ie.INSTANCE_UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, ie.FLAVOR, ism.KEY, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY ism.KEY) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE ie.DELETED = 0 AND ism.DELETED = 0 AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT imwm.instance_uuid, imwm.display_name, imwm.vm_state, imwm.power_state, imwm.memory_mb, imwm.vcpus, imwm.metadata_key, imwm.metadata_value, ifdr.FLAVOR AS instance_extra_flavor_json, ifdr.window_column AS dense_rank_by_metadata_key, imwm.max_instance_extra_id_next_2 FROM InstanceMetadataAndWindowMax AS imwm JOIN InstanceFlavorAndDenseRank AS ifdr ON imwm.instance_uuid = ifdr.INSTANCE_UUID AND imwm.metadata_key = ifdr.KEY ORDER BY imwm.instance_uuid, imwm.metadata_key
WITH InstanceMemoryDenseRank AS (SELECT t1.UUID AS instance_uuid, t2.VALUE AS metadata_value, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY t1.MEMORY_MB ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS t0 JOIN INSTANCES AS t1 ON t0.INSTANCE_UUID = t1.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS t2 ON t1.UUID = t2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE t1.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND t1.HOST = 'blaze8-12' AND t2.KEY = 'image_image_type'), InstanceNetworkAndAvgMemory AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, iic.NETWORK_INFO AS network_info, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY ism.VALUE ORDER BY i.CREATED_AT ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON i.UUID = iic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' ORDER BY i.CREATED_AT) SELECT imdr.instance_uuid, imdr.metadata_value, imdr.window_column AS dense_rank_by_memory_window, inam.instance_display_name, inam.metadata_key, inam.network_info, inam.window_column AS avg_memory_mb_window FROM InstanceMemoryDenseRank AS imdr JOIN InstanceNetworkAndAvgMemory AS inam ON imdr.instance_uuid = inam.instance_uuid AND imdr.metadata_value = inam.metadata_value ORDER BY imdr.instance_uuid
WITH completed_migrations_per_instance AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name AS instance_display_name, i.availability_zone AS availability_zone, COUNT(m.id) AS completed_migration_count, SUM(m.memory_processed) AS total_memory_processed FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND m.memory_processed > 0 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, i.availability_zone), instance_memory_stats_by_display_name AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, MIN(i.ID) AS min_instance_id, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) AS avg_memory_mb FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME) SELECT c.instance_uuid, c.instance_display_name, c.availability_zone, c.completed_migration_count, c.total_memory_processed, s.min_instance_id, s.avg_memory_mb FROM completed_migrations_per_instance AS c JOIN instance_memory_stats_by_display_name AS s ON c.instance_display_name = s.DISPLAY_NAME ORDER BY c.completed_migration_count DESC, c.total_memory_processed DESC
WITH BlazeCompletedInstanceMigrations AS (SELECT t0.DISPLAY_NAME, t1.STATUS, t1.DEST_COMPUTE, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY t1.DEST_COMPUTE ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS t0 JOIN MIGRATIONS AS t1 ON t0.UUID = t1.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE t1.STATUS = 'completed' AND t0.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), EtherCompletedMigrationsWithMetadata AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.STATUS AS migration_status, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, m.DEST_HOST, m.INSTANCE_UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.VM_STATE AS instance_vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS instance_memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS instance_vcpus, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, m.MEMORY_REMAINING AS migration_memory_remaining, MIN(m.MEMORY_REMAINING) OVER (PARTITION BY m.DEST_COMPUTE ORDER BY m.DEST_COMPUTE) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'ether-18') SELECT ecmwm.DEST_COMPUTE, COUNT(DISTINCT ecmwm.migration_id) AS Ether_Completed_Migration_Count, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT bcm.DISPLAY_NAME) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS Has_Blaze8_12_Completed_Migration_To_Same_Dest_Compute FROM EtherCompletedMigrationsWithMetadata AS ecmwm LEFT JOIN BlazeCompletedInstanceMigrations AS bcm ON ecmwm.DEST_COMPUTE = bcm.DEST_COMPUTE GROUP BY ecmwm.DEST_COMPUTE HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT ecmwm.migration_id) >= 2 ORDER BY Ether_Completed_Migration_Count DESC, ecmwm.DEST_COMPUTE
WITH subq1 AS (SELECT i.display_name, i.availability_zone, m.status, m.migration_type, i.uuid, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY i.uuid ORDER BY i.uuid) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN migrations AS m ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12'), subq2 AS (SELECT i.UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.VM_STATE, i.MEMORY_MB, i.VCPUS, i.KEY_NAME, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY i.VCPUS ORDER BY i.VCPUS) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT subq2.UUID AS instance_uuid, subq2.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, subq2.VM_STATE, subq2.MEMORY_MB, subq2.VCPUS, subq2.KEY_NAME, subq2.instance_extra_id, subq2.window_column AS min_memory_mb_in_vcpu_group, subq1.availability_zone AS migration_availability_zone, subq1.status AS migration_status, subq1.migration_type, subq1.window_column AS migration_dense_rank_within_instance_uuid FROM subq2 JOIN subq1 ON subq2.UUID = subq1.uuid ORDER BY subq2.UUID, subq1.window_column
WITH MigrationDetails AS (SELECT i.display_name, i.availability_zone, m.status, m.migration_type, i.uuid, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY i.uuid ORDER BY i.uuid) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN migrations AS m ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12'), InstanceMetadataStats AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name, i.key_name, COUNT(ism.id) AS metadata_entry_count, SUM(i.vcpus) AS total_vcpus FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, i.key_name) SELECT md.uuid AS instance_uuid, md.display_name, md.availability_zone, md.status AS migration_status, md.migration_type, md.window_column AS migration_dense_rank, ims.key_name, ims.metadata_entry_count, ims.total_vcpus FROM MigrationDetails AS md JOIN InstanceMetadataStats AS ims ON md.uuid = ims.instance_uuid WHERE md.availability_zone = 'flare3' ORDER BY md.display_name, md.uuid
WITH InstanceStats AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, MIN(i.ID) AS min_instance_id, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) AS avg_memory_mb FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME), TopMigrations AS (SELECT i.display_name, COUNT(m.id) AS migrations FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.display_name ORDER BY migrations DESC LIMIT 10), CombinedInstanceView AS (SELECT tm.display_name, tm.migrations, is1.min_instance_id, is1.avg_memory_mb, 'Top 10 by completed migrations' AS reason FROM TopMigrations AS tm LEFT JOIN InstanceStats AS is1 ON tm.display_name = is1.display_name UNION ALL SELECT is2.display_name, NULL AS migrations, is2.min_instance_id, is2.avg_memory_mb, 'Has image_image_type system metadata on blaze8-12 (not deleted)' AS reason FROM InstanceStats AS is2) SELECT display_name, migrations, min_instance_id, avg_memory_mb, reason FROM CombinedInstanceView ORDER BY display_name, reason
WITH MigrationStatusRanks AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE AS instance_availability_zone, m.STATUS AS migration_status, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'prime3-77'), InstanceMetadataMemoryAvgs AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.HOSTNAME AS hostname, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY i.UUID ORDER BY ism.KEY) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT msr.migration_status, COUNT(DISTINCT msr.migration_id) AS total_completed_migrations, COUNT(DISTINCT imam.instance_uuid) AS total_instances_with_metadata, AVG(imam.window_column) AS avg_running_avg_instance_memory_mb, MAX(msr.window_column) AS max_migration_rank_within_status FROM MigrationStatusRanks AS msr CROSS JOIN InstanceMetadataMemoryAvgs AS imam GROUP BY msr.migration_status ORDER BY total_completed_migrations DESC
WITH MigrationDetails AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.INSTANCE_UUID, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, AVG(m.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY m.INSTANCE_UUID ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' ORDER BY m.ID), InstanceMemoryStats AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, MIN(i.ID) AS min_instance_id, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) AS avg_memory_mb FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME), CombinedResults AS (SELECT md.migration_id, md.INSTANCE_UUID, md.SOURCE_COMPUTE, md.DEST_COMPUTE, md.instance_display_name, md.metadata_key, md.metadata_value, md.window_column, ims.min_instance_id, ims.avg_memory_mb FROM MigrationDetails AS md LEFT JOIN InstanceMemoryStats AS ims ON md.instance_display_name = ims.DISPLAY_NAME) SELECT * FROM CombinedResults ORDER BY migration_id
WITH MigrationDetails AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.HOSTNAME AS instance_hostname, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE AS migration_source_compute, m.DEST_COMPUTE AS migration_dest_compute, m.DEST_HOST AS migration_dest_host, m.MIGRATION_TYPE AS migration_type, m.MEMORY_TOTAL AS memory_total, m.MEMORY_PROCESSED AS memory_processed, m.MEMORY_REMAINING AS memory_remaining, MIN(m.MEMORY_REMAINING) OVER (PARTITION BY m.INSTANCE_UUID ORDER BY m.INSTANCE_UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), ImageTypeMetadataAndNetwork AS (SELECT t1.DISPLAY_NAME, t0.VALUE AS metadata_value, t2.NETWORK_INFO, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY t0.VALUE ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS t0 JOIN INSTANCES AS t1 ON t0.INSTANCE_UUID = t1.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS t2 ON t1.UUID = t2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE t0.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND t1.host = 'blaze8-12') SELECT md.instance_uuid, md.instance_display_name, md.instance_hostname, md.migration_source_compute, md.migration_dest_compute, md.migration_dest_host, md.migration_type, md.memory_total, md.memory_processed, md.memory_remaining, md.window_column AS min_memory_remaining_within_instance_uuid, imn.metadata_value, imn.NETWORK_INFO, imn.window_column AS metadata_value_row_number_window FROM MigrationDetails AS md JOIN ImageTypeMetadataAndNetwork AS imn ON md.instance_display_name = imn.DISPLAY_NAME ORDER BY md.instance_uuid, imn.metadata_value
WITH PrimeCompletedMigrations AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE AS instance_availability_zone, m.STATUS AS migration_status, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'prime3-77'), BlazeCompletedMigrations AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, m.DEST_HOST, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.MEMORY_MB, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' ORDER BY m.ID) SELECT pcm.migration_id, pcm.instance_display_name AS prime_instance_display_name, pcm.instance_availability_zone AS prime_instance_availability_zone, pcm.migration_status, pcm.window_column AS prime_status_rank, bcm.SOURCE_COMPUTE AS blaze_source_compute, bcm.DEST_COMPUTE AS blaze_dest_compute, bcm.DEST_HOST AS blaze_dest_host, bcm.DISPLAY_NAME AS blaze_instance_display_name, bcm.MEMORY_MB AS blaze_instance_memory_mb, bcm.window_column AS blaze_avg_memory_mb_window_2_pre_2_fol FROM PrimeCompletedMigrations AS pcm CROSS JOIN BlazeCompletedMigrations AS bcm ORDER BY pcm.migration_id, bcm.migration_id
WITH MaxInstanceMemory AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, MAX(i.MEMORY_MB) AS max_memory_mb, i.LAUNCHED_AT FROM INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON i.UUID = iic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME, ism.KEY, i.LAUNCHED_AT HAVING MAX(i.MEMORY_MB) > 16000), CompletedMigrations AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, SUM(m.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT mim.DISPLAY_NAME, mim.metadata_key, mim.max_memory_mb, mim.LAUNCHED_AT, COUNT(cm.migration_id) AS completed_migration_count, MAX(cm.window_column) AS max_migration_id_running_sum, ROUND(CAST(MAX(cm.window_column) AS REAL) / NULLIF(mim.max_memory_mb, 0), 6) AS running_sum_per_mb FROM MaxInstanceMemory AS mim LEFT JOIN CompletedMigrations AS cm ON cm.instance_display_name = mim.DISPLAY_NAME AND cm.metadata_key = mim.metadata_key GROUP BY mim.DISPLAY_NAME, mim.metadata_key, mim.max_memory_mb, mim.LAUNCHED_AT ORDER BY running_sum_per_mb DESC
WITH TopMigratedInstances AS (SELECT i.display_name, COUNT(m.id) AS migrations FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.display_name ORDER BY migrations DESC LIMIT 10), InstanceMetadataNetworkWindow AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, iic.NETWORK_INFO AS network_info, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY ism.VALUE ORDER BY i.CREATED_AT ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON i.UUID = iic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' ORDER BY i.CREATED_AT) SELECT tmi.display_name, tmi.migrations, imnw.instance_uuid, imnw.metadata_key, imnw.metadata_value, imnw.network_info, imnw.window_column FROM TopMigratedInstances AS tmi JOIN InstanceMetadataNetworkWindow AS imnw ON tmi.display_name = imnw.instance_display_name ORDER BY tmi.migrations DESC, imnw.instance_uuid
WITH TopImageTypeMetadataByDisplayName AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, COUNT(ism.ID) AS metadata_entries FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.HOST = 'prime-35' AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME ORDER BY metadata_entries DESC LIMIT 10), Flare3Blaze8_12InstanceMetadata AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.FLAVOR AS flavor, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), CombinedResults AS (SELECT t.DISPLAY_NAME AS name_or_display_name, t.metadata_entries AS metric_value, 'Top 10 by image_image_type metadata entries on prime-35 (not deleted)' AS reason, NULL AS instance_uuid, NULL AS memory_mb, NULL AS vcpus, NULL AS flavor, NULL AS metadata_key, NULL AS metadata_value, NULL AS window_column FROM TopImageTypeMetadataByDisplayName AS t UNION ALL SELECT f.display_name AS name_or_display_name, f.window_column AS metric_value, 'Instance metadata rows in flare3 on blaze8-12' AS reason, f.instance_uuid, f.memory_mb, f.vcpus, f.flavor, f.metadata_key, f.metadata_value, f.window_column FROM Flare3Blaze8_12InstanceMetadata AS f) SELECT name_or_display_name, metric_value, reason, instance_uuid, memory_mb, vcpus, flavor, metadata_key, metadata_value, window_column FROM CombinedResults ORDER BY reason, name_or_display_name
WITH subq1 AS (SELECT t1.UUID AS instance_uuid, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY t1.MEMORY_MB ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS t0 JOIN INSTANCES AS t1 ON t0.INSTANCE_UUID = t1.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS t2 ON t1.UUID = t2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE t0.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND t1.DELETED = 0 AND t1.host = 'blaze8-12'), subq2 AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name AS instance_display_name, i.availability_zone AS availability_zone, COUNT(m.id) AS completed_migration_count, SUM(m.memory_processed) AS total_memory_processed FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND m.memory_processed > 0 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, i.availability_zone) SELECT subq2.instance_uuid, subq2.instance_display_name, subq2.availability_zone, subq2.completed_migration_count, subq2.total_memory_processed, subq1.window_column AS memory_dense_rank_window FROM subq2 JOIN subq1 ON subq2.instance_uuid = subq1.instance_uuid ORDER BY subq2.total_memory_processed DESC
WITH MigrationDetails AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.STATUS AS migration_status, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, i.MEMORY_MB AS instance_memory_mb, c.NETWORK_INFO, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (ORDER BY m.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS c ON i.UUID = c.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), InstanceMemoryStats AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, MIN(i.ID) AS min_instance_id, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) AS avg_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME) SELECT md.migration_id, md.migration_status, md.SOURCE_COMPUTE, md.DEST_COMPUTE, md.instance_display_name, ims.min_instance_id, ims.avg_memory_mb, md.instance_memory_mb, md.NETWORK_INFO, md.window_column FROM MigrationDetails AS md JOIN InstanceMemoryStats AS ims ON md.instance_display_name = ims.DISPLAY_NAME
WITH CompletedMigrationsPerInstance AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name AS instance_display_name, i.availability_zone AS availability_zone, COUNT(m.id) AS completed_migration_count, SUM(m.memory_processed) AS total_memory_processed FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND m.memory_processed > 0 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, i.availability_zone), ImageTypeMetadataWithWindowMem AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, iic.NETWORK_INFO AS network_info, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY ism.VALUE ORDER BY i.CREATED_AT ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON i.UUID = iic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' ORDER BY i.CREATED_AT) SELECT im.metadata_value, COUNT(DISTINCT cm.instance_uuid) AS instances_with_completed_migrations, AVG(cm.completed_migration_count) AS avg_completed_migrations_per_instance, MAX(cm.completed_migration_count) AS max_completed_migrations_per_instance, SUM(cm.total_memory_processed) AS total_memory_processed_bytes, AVG(im.window_column) AS avg_window_memory_mb FROM CompletedMigrationsPerInstance AS cm JOIN ImageTypeMetadataWithWindowMem AS im ON cm.instance_uuid = im.instance_uuid GROUP BY im.metadata_value ORDER BY instances_with_completed_migrations DESC
WITH ActiveMigrationsByState AS (SELECT i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, m.STATUS AS migration_status, COUNT(i.ID) AS instance_count, MIN(m.MEMORY_TOTAL) AS min_memory_total FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN MIGRATIONS AS m ON i.UUID = m.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.HIDDEN = 0 AND NOT m.STATUS IN ('accepted', 'confirmed', 'reverted', 'error', 'failed', 'completed') GROUP BY i.VM_STATE, m.STATUS), HostPrime35InstancesByStateAZ AS (SELECT ins.VM_STATE, ins.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, COUNT(ins.UUID) AS instance_count, MIN(ins.MEMORY_MB) AS min_memory_mb FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS ins ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = ins.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ins.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND ins.HOST = 'prime-35' GROUP BY ins.VM_STATE, ins.AVAILABILITY_ZONE) SELECT h.VM_STATE, h.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, a.migration_status, h.instance_count AS prime35_instance_count, a.instance_count AS active_migration_instance_count, h.min_memory_mb, a.min_memory_total, ROUND(CAST(a.min_memory_total AS REAL) / NULLIF(h.min_memory_mb, 0), 2) AS Min_MigrationMemory_to_Min_InstanceMemory_Ratio FROM HostPrime35InstancesByStateAZ AS h JOIN ActiveMigrationsByState AS a ON h.VM_STATE = a.vm_state ORDER BY Min_MigrationMemory_to_Min_InstanceMemory_Ratio DESC
WITH InstanceStateSummary AS (SELECT ins.DISPLAY_NAME, ins.VM_STATE, COUNT(DISTINCT ins.UUID) AS instance_count, MIN(ins.CREATED_AT) AS earliest_created_at FROM INSTANCES AS ins JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = ins.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = ins.UUID WHERE ins.DELETED = 0 AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND ins.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY ins.DISPLAY_NAME, ins.VM_STATE), InstanceMetadataVcpuSummary AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name, i.key_name, COUNT(ism.id) AS metadata_entry_count, SUM(i.vcpus) AS total_vcpus FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.availability_zone = 'flare3' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, i.key_name) SELECT iss.DISPLAY_NAME, iss.VM_STATE, iss.instance_count, iss.earliest_created_at, COUNT(DISTINCT imvs.instance_uuid) AS flare3_instance_count, SUM(imvs.metadata_entry_count) AS flare3_metadata_entry_count, SUM(imvs.total_vcpus) AS flare3_total_vcpus FROM InstanceStateSummary AS iss JOIN InstanceMetadataVcpuSummary AS imvs ON imvs.display_name = iss.DISPLAY_NAME GROUP BY iss.DISPLAY_NAME, iss.VM_STATE, iss.instance_count, iss.earliest_created_at
WITH MaxMemoryByDisplayAndKey AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, MAX(i.MEMORY_MB) AS max_memory_mb, i.LAUNCHED_AT FROM INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON i.UUID = iic.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME, ism.KEY, i.LAUNCHED_AT HAVING MAX(i.MEMORY_MB) > 16000), InstanceAZMetadataAndVcpus AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name, i.availability_zone, COUNT(ism.id) AS metadata_entry_count, SUM(i.vcpus) AS total_vcpus FROM instance_extra AS ie JOIN instances AS i ON ie.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND ism.key = 'image_image_type' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, i.availability_zone) SELECT ia.instance_uuid, ia.display_name, ia.availability_zone, ia.metadata_entry_count, ia.total_vcpus, mm.metadata_key, mm.max_memory_mb, mm.launched_at, ROUND(CAST(ia.total_vcpus AS REAL) / NULLIF(mm.max_memory_mb, 0), 4) AS vcpus_per_mb_ratio FROM InstanceAZMetadataAndVcpus AS ia JOIN MaxMemoryByDisplayAndKey AS mm ON ia.display_name = mm.display_name ORDER BY vcpus_per_mb_ratio DESC, mm.max_memory_mb DESC
WITH sq1 AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, COUNT(ism.ID) AS metadata_entries FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.HOST = 'prime-35' AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME ORDER BY metadata_entries DESC LIMIT 10), sq2 AS (SELECT i.UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.VM_STATE, i.MEMORY_MB, c.NETWORK_INFO, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY i.VM_STATE ORDER BY i.MEMORY_MB DESC) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS c ON i.UUID = c.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT sq1.DISPLAY_NAME, sq1.metadata_entries, sq2.UUID, sq2.VM_STATE, sq2.MEMORY_MB, sq2.NETWORK_INFO, sq2.window_column FROM sq1 JOIN sq2 ON sq1.DISPLAY_NAME = sq2.DISPLAY_NAME ORDER BY sq1.metadata_entries DESC, sq2.VM_STATE, sq2.window_column
WITH InstanceExtrasByAZHost AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name, iic.network_info, COUNT(ie.id) AS extra_record_count, SUM(i.memory_mb) AS total_memory_mb FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON ie.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS iic ON iic.instance_uuid = i.uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND i.availability_zone = 'flare3' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, iic.network_info), InstanceWindowMaxExtra AS (SELECT i.uuid, i.display_name, i.vm_state, i.power_state, i.memory_mb, i.vcpus, MAX(ie.id) OVER (ORDER BY i.uuid ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_instance_extra_id_next2 FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.uuid = ie.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12') SELECT a.instance_uuid, a.display_name, a.network_info, a.extra_record_count, a.total_memory_mb, w.vm_state, w.power_state, w.memory_mb, w.vcpus, w.max_instance_extra_id_next2 FROM InstanceExtrasByAZHost AS a JOIN InstanceWindowMaxExtra AS w ON w.uuid = a.instance_uuid
WITH MemoryWindow AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, m.SOURCE_COMPUTE, m.DEST_COMPUTE, m.DEST_HOST, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.MEMORY_MB, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY m.STATUS ORDER BY m.ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' ORDER BY m.ID), MetadataRunningSum AS (SELECT m.ID AS migration_id, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS instance_display_name, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, SUM(m.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY m.ID) AS window_column FROM MIGRATIONS AS m JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON m.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE m.STATUS = 'completed' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), CombinedMigrations AS (SELECT mw.migration_id, mw.SOURCE_COMPUTE, mw.DEST_COMPUTE, mw.DEST_HOST, mw.DISPLAY_NAME, mw.MEMORY_MB, mw.window_column AS avg_memory_mb_window, NULL AS metadata_key, NULL AS running_sum_migration_id_by_key, 'Memory Window Avg' AS reason FROM MemoryWindow AS mw UNION ALL SELECT mrs.migration_id, NULL AS SOURCE_COMPUTE, NULL AS DEST_COMPUTE, NULL AS DEST_HOST, mrs.instance_display_name AS DISPLAY_NAME, NULL AS MEMORY_MB, NULL AS avg_memory_mb_window, mrs.metadata_key, mrs.window_column AS running_sum_migration_id_by_key, 'Metadata Key Running Sum' AS reason FROM MetadataRunningSum AS mrs) SELECT migration_id, SOURCE_COMPUTE, DEST_COMPUTE, DEST_HOST, DISPLAY_NAME, MEMORY_MB, avg_memory_mb_window, metadata_key, running_sum_migration_id_by_key, reason FROM CombinedMigrations ORDER BY migration_id, reason
WITH q1 AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name, i.availability_zone, COUNT(m.id) AS migration_count, SUM(m.memory_processed) AS total_memory_processed FROM instances AS i JOIN migrations AS m ON i.uuid = m.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.host = 'prime-35' GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, i.availability_zone), q2 AS (SELECT ins.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, ins.VM_STATE, COUNT(ins.UUID) AS instance_count, MIN(ins.CREATED_AT) AS earliest_created_at FROM INSTANCES AS ins JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = ins.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS iic ON iic.INSTANCE_UUID = ins.UUID WHERE ins.DELETED = 0 AND ins.HOST = 'prime-35' GROUP BY ins.AVAILABILITY_ZONE, ins.VM_STATE) SELECT q1.instance_uuid, q1.display_name, q1.availability_zone, q2.vm_state, q2.instance_count, q2.earliest_created_at, q1.migration_count, q1.total_memory_processed FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.availability_zone = q2.availability_zone ORDER BY q1.total_memory_processed DESC, q1.migration_count DESC, q1.instance_uuid
WITH completed_migrations_on_blaze AS (SELECT m.id AS migration_id, m.status, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, m.dest_host, i.display_name, i.vm_state, MAX(i.memory_mb) OVER (ORDER BY m.id ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_memory_mb_next_2 FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS ic ON i.uuid = ic.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12'), flare3_instances_metadata AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.FLAVOR AS flavor, ism.KEY AS metadata_key, ism.VALUE AS metadata_value, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON ism.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT cm.migration_id) AS completed_migration_count, COUNT(DISTINCT fim.instance_uuid) AS flare3_instance_count FROM completed_migrations_on_blaze AS cm CROSS JOIN flare3_instances_metadata AS fim
WITH TopMetadataDisplayNames AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, COUNT(ism.ID) AS metadata_entries FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.HOST = 'prime-35' AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME ORDER BY metadata_entries DESC LIMIT 10), Flare3BlazeInstances AS (SELECT i.UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, i.VM_STATE, i.MEMORY_MB, i.VCPUS, i.KEY_NAME, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, MIN(i.MEMORY_MB) OVER (PARTITION BY i.VCPUS ORDER BY i.VCPUS) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12'), TopMetadataInstancesOnPrime35 AS (SELECT i.UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME FROM INSTANCES AS i WHERE i.DELETED = 0 AND i.HOST = 'prime-35' AND i.DISPLAY_NAME IN (SELECT DISPLAY_NAME FROM TopMetadataDisplayNames)) SELECT f.UUID, f.DISPLAY_NAME, f.VM_STATE, f.MEMORY_MB, f.VCPUS, f.KEY_NAME, f.instance_extra_id, f.window_column FROM Flare3BlazeInstances AS f WHERE f.DISPLAY_NAME IN (SELECT DISPLAY_NAME FROM TopMetadataDisplayNames) ORDER BY f.VCPUS, f.UUID
WITH MigrationStats AS (SELECT i.uuid AS instance_uuid, i.display_name AS instance_display_name, m.status AS migration_status, COUNT(m.id) AS migration_count, SUM(m.memory_processed) AS total_memory_processed FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE ism.key = 'image_image_type' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.uuid, i.display_name, m.status), MetadataWindowStats AS (SELECT i.UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, ism.KEY, SUM(i.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY i.ID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT ms.instance_uuid, ms.instance_display_name, ms.migration_status, ms.migration_count, ms.total_memory_processed, mws.KEY AS system_metadata_key, mws.window_column FROM MigrationStats AS ms JOIN MetadataWindowStats AS mws ON ms.instance_uuid = mws.UUID ORDER BY ms.instance_uuid, ms.migration_status, mws.KEY, mws.window_column
WITH geometric_mean_by_display_name AS (SELECT i.display_name, EXP(AVG(LN(i.memory_mb))) AS geometric_mean_memory_mb FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_extra AS ie ON ie.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_info_caches AS iic ON i.uuid = iic.instance_uuid WHERE iic.network_info <> '[]' AND i.memory_mb > 0 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.display_name ORDER BY geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC), min_id_and_avg_memory_by_display_name AS (SELECT i.DISPLAY_NAME, MIN(i.ID) AS min_instance_id, AVG(i.MEMORY_MB) AS avg_memory_mb FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND ism.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12' GROUP BY i.DISPLAY_NAME) SELECT g.display_name, g.geometric_mean_memory_mb, m.min_instance_id, m.avg_memory_mb FROM geometric_mean_by_display_name AS g JOIN min_id_and_avg_memory_by_display_name AS m ON g.display_name = m.display_name ORDER BY g.geometric_mean_memory_mb DESC
WITH InstanceNetworkCache AS (SELECT i.display_name, i.vm_state, i.memory_mb, c.network_info, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY i.project_id ORDER BY i.project_id) AS window_column FROM instances AS i JOIN instance_info_caches AS c ON i.uuid = c.instance_uuid WHERE i.deleted = 0 AND i.host = 'blaze8-12'), InstanceFlavorAndMetadata AS (SELECT ie.INSTANCE_UUID, i.DISPLAY_NAME, ie.FLAVOR, ism.KEY, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY ism.KEY ORDER BY ism.KEY) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie JOIN INSTANCES AS i ON ie.INSTANCE_UUID = i.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS ism ON i.UUID = ism.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE ie.DELETED = 0 AND ism.DELETED = 0 AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT ifm.INSTANCE_UUID, ifm.DISPLAY_NAME, inc.vm_state, inc.memory_mb, inc.network_info, ifm.FLAVOR, ifm.KEY, inc.window_column AS project_dense_rank, ifm.window_column AS metadata_key_dense_rank FROM InstanceFlavorAndMetadata AS ifm JOIN InstanceNetworkCache AS inc ON ifm.DISPLAY_NAME = inc.display_name ORDER BY ifm.INSTANCE_UUID, ifm.KEY
WITH MigrationDetails AS (SELECT m.status, m.source_compute, m.dest_compute, i.display_name, ism.key, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY m.instance_uuid ORDER BY m.instance_uuid) AS window_column FROM migrations AS m JOIN instances AS i ON m.instance_uuid = i.uuid JOIN instance_system_metadata AS ism ON i.uuid = ism.instance_uuid WHERE m.status = 'completed' AND i.host = 'blaze8-12'), ImageTypeMetadata AS (SELECT t1.DISPLAY_NAME, t0.VALUE AS metadata_value, t2.NETWORK_INFO, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY t0.VALUE ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS t0 JOIN INSTANCES AS t1 ON t0.INSTANCE_UUID = t1.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS t2 ON t1.UUID = t2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE t0.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND t1.host = 'blaze8-12') SELECT md.status AS migration_status, md.source_compute, md.dest_compute, md.display_name, md.key AS instance_system_metadata_key, itm.metadata_value, itm.network_info, md.window_column AS migration_dense_rank, itm.window_column AS image_type_row_number FROM MigrationDetails AS md JOIN ImageTypeMetadata AS itm ON md.display_name = itm.display_name ORDER BY md.display_name, itm.metadata_value
WITH ImageTypeMetadataOnHost AS (SELECT t1.DISPLAY_NAME, t0.VALUE AS metadata_value, t2.NETWORK_INFO, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY t0.VALUE ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM INSTANCE_SYSTEM_METADATA AS t0 JOIN INSTANCES AS t1 ON t0.INSTANCE_UUID = t1.UUID JOIN INSTANCE_INFO_CACHES AS t2 ON t1.UUID = t2.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE t0.KEY = 'image_image_type' AND t1.host = 'blaze8-12'), InstancesInAZOnHost AS (SELECT i.UUID AS instance_uuid, i.DISPLAY_NAME AS display_name, i.VM_STATE AS vm_state, i.MEMORY_MB AS memory_mb, i.VCPUS AS vcpus, ie.ID AS instance_extra_id, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY i.VM_STATE ORDER BY i.UUID) AS window_column FROM INSTANCES AS i JOIN INSTANCE_EXTRA AS ie ON i.UUID = ie.INSTANCE_UUID WHERE i.AVAILABILITY_ZONE = 'flare3' AND i.HOST = 'blaze8-12') SELECT iaoh.vm_state, AVG(iaoh.memory_mb) AS avg_memory_mb, COUNT(DISTINCT iaoh.instance_uuid) AS instance_count, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT itmo.display_name) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS Has_ImageType_Metadata_On_Host FROM InstancesInAZOnHost AS iaoh LEFT JOIN ImageTypeMetadataOnHost AS itmo ON iaoh.display_name = itmo.display_name WHERE iaoh.memory_mb > 0 GROUP BY iaoh.vm_state HAVING AVG(iaoh.memory_mb) > 16000 ORDER BY avg_memory_mb DESC
